def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : {E : Type u_1} →
  [inst : NormedAddCommGroup E] →
    [inst_1 : NormedSpace ℝ E] →
      [inst_2 : FiniteDimensional ℝ E] →
        {H : Type u_2} →
          [inst_3 : TopologicalSpace H] →
            {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} →
              {M : Type u_3} →
                [inst_4 : TopologicalSpace M] →
                  [inst_5 : ChartedSpace H M] →
                    [inst_6 : IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] →
                      DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M →
                        DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) 2 →
                          DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) 2 :=
fun {E} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H} [TopologicalSpace H] {I} {M}
    [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] g T ↦
  have S := DifferentialGeometry.MultilinearSection.domDomCongr (↑⊤) (Equiv.swap 0 1) T;
  2⁻¹ • (T + S) -
    DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.tensor0SFieldSmulByFun (↑⊤)
      (fun x ↦ DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.metricTracePair0SAt g (T x) / ↑(Module.finrank ℝ E)) ⋯
      (DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.metricTensorField g)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart_apply.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (T : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) 2)
  (x : M) :
  (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart g T) x =
    2⁻¹ • (T x + (T x).domDomCongr (Equiv.swap 0 1)) -
      (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.metricTracePair0SAt g (T x) / ↑(Module.finrank ℝ E)) •
        DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.metricTensor0S g x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart_apply_of_finrank_eq_two.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M]
  (hdim : Module.finrank ℝ E = 2) (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M)
  (T : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) 2) (x : M) :
  (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart g T) x =
    2⁻¹ • (T x + (T x).domDomCongr (Equiv.swap 0 1)) -
      (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.metricTracePair0SAt g (T x) / 2) •
        DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.metricTensor0S g x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart_symmetric.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (T : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) 2)
  (x : M) :
  ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart g T) x).domDomCongr (Equiv.swap 0 1) =
    (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart g T) x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart_trace_eq_zero.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] (hdim : Module.finrank ℝ E ≠ 0)
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (T : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) 2)
  (x : M) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.metricTracePair0SAt g ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart g T) x) = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart_add.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E]
  [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3}
  [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M)
  (S T : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) 2) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart g (S + T) =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart g S + RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart g T
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart_smul.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E]
  [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3}
  [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M)
  (c : ℝ) (T : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) 2) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart g (c • T) = c • RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart g T
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereCoordinateOneForm_apply (i : Fin 3)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
  (v : TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) x) :
  (((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereCoordinateOneForm i) x) fun x ↦ v) =
    ((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl x) v).ofLp i
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereCoordinateOneForm_two :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereCoordinateOneForm 2 = RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.heightOneForm
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereAzimuthalOneForm_apply (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
  (v : TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) x) :
  ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereAzimuthalOneForm x) fun x ↦ v) =
    -(↑x).ofLp 1 * ((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl x) v).ofLp 0 +
      (↑x).ofLp 0 * ((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl x) v).ofLp 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.heightOneForm_eq_zero_at_pole (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
  (hx : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x ^ 2 = 1) : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.heightOneForm x = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereAzimuthalOneForm_eq_zero_at_pole
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
  (hx : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x ^ 2 = 1) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereAzimuthalOneForm x = 0
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.meridionalOneForm : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData →
  (r : ℝ → ℝ) → ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r → DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection :=
fun D r hr ↦
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.tensor0SFieldSmulByFun (↑⊤)
    (fun x ↦ D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) * r (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x))
    ⋯ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.heightOneForm
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.azimuthalOneForm : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData →
  (s : ℝ → ℝ) → ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s → DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection :=
fun D s hs ↦
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.tensor0SFieldSmulByFun (↑⊤)
    (fun x ↦ D.b (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) * s (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x))
    ⋯ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereAzimuthalOneForm
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.meridionalOneForm_apply_dIncl
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
  (v : TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) x) :
  (((D.meridionalOneForm r hr) x) fun x ↦ v) =
    D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) * r (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
      ((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl x) v).ofLp 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.azimuthalOneForm_apply_dIncl
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (s : ℝ → ℝ) (hs : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
  (v : TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) x) :
  (((D.azimuthalOneForm s hs) x) fun x ↦ v) =
    D.b (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) * s (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
      (-(↑x).ofLp 1 * ((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl x) v).ofLp 0 +
        (↑x).ofLp 0 * ((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl x) v).ofLp 1)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.meridionalOneForm_cylinderMap_mfderiv
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r)
  (q : ↥RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderDomain) (V : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) :
  (((D.meridionalOneForm r hr) (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap q)) fun x ↦
      (mfderiv% RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap q) V) =
    D.a ((↑q).ofLp 0) * r ((↑q).ofLp 0) * V.ofLp 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.azimuthalOneForm_cylinderMap_mfderiv
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (s : ℝ → ℝ) (hs : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s)
  (q : ↥RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderDomain) (V : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) :
  (((D.azimuthalOneForm s hs) (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap q)) fun x ↦
      (mfderiv% RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap q) V) =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp D.a ((↑q).ofLp 0) * s ((↑q).ofLp 0) * V.ofLp 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.meridionalOneForm_eq_zero_at_pole
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
  (hx : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x ^ 2 = 1) : (D.meridionalOneForm r hr) x = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.azimuthalOneForm_eq_zero_at_pole
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (s : ℝ → ℝ) (hs : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
  (hx : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x ^ 2 = 1) : (D.azimuthalOneForm s hs) x = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.meridionalOneForm_one_ne_zero
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) : D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ 1) ⋯ ≠ 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.azimuthalOneForm_one_ne_zero
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) : D.azimuthalOneForm (fun x ↦ 1) ⋯ ≠ 0
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationDensity.{u_1} : {M : Type u_1} →
  [inst : TopologicalSpace M] →
    [inst_1 : ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M] →
      [inst_2 : IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M] →
        [T2Space M] →
          DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M →
            DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection → M → ℝ :=
fun {M} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M]
    [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M] g h x ↦
  have K := DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt g x / 2;
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 1 ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.roughLap0SField g h) x) +
        K ^ 2 * DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 1 (h x) -
      K *
        DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 2 ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g h) x) +
    2 * K *
      DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 2
        ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart g (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g h)) x)
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairingDensity.{u_1} : {M : Type u_1} →
  [inst : TopologicalSpace M] →
    [inst_1 : ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M] →
      [inst_2 : IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M] →
        [T2Space M] →
          DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M →
            DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection →
              DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection → M → ℝ :=
fun {M} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M]
    [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M] g h k x ↦
  have K := DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt g x / 2;
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.inner0S g x 1 ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.roughLap0SField g h) x)
          ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.roughLap0SField g k) x) +
        K ^ 2 * DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.inner0S g x 1 (h x) (k x) -
      K *
        DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.inner0S g x 2 ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g h) x)
          ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g k) x) +
    2 * K *
      DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.inner0S g x 2
        ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart g (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g h)) x)
        ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart g (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g k)) x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationDensity_smooth.{u_1} {M : Type u_1} [TopologicalSpace M]
  [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M] [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M]
  [T2Space M] (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  ContMDiff (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ) (↑⊤)
    (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationDensity g h)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationDensity_integrable.{u_1} {M : Type u_1} [TopologicalSpace M]
  [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M] [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M]
  [T2Space M] [CompactSpace M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  MeasureTheory.Integrable (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationDensity g h)
    (DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M
      g)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairingDensity_smooth.{u_1} {M : Type u_1} [TopologicalSpace M]
  [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M] [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M]
  [T2Space M] (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M)
  (h k : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  ContMDiff (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ) (↑⊤)
    (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairingDensity g h k)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairingDensity_integrable.{u_1} {M : Type u_1} [TopologicalSpace M]
  [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M] [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M]
  [T2Space M] [CompactSpace M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M)
  (h k : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  MeasureTheory.Integrable (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairingDensity g h k)
    (DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M
      g)
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation.{u_1} : {M : Type u_1} →
  [inst : TopologicalSpace M] →
    [inst_1 : ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M] →
      [inst_2 : IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M] →
        [T2Space M] →
          [CompactSpace M] →
            DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M →
              DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection → ℝ :=
fun {M} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M]
    [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M] [CompactSpace M] g h ↦
  ∫ (x : M),
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationDensity g h
      x ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
      (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M g
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing.{u_1} : {M : Type u_1} →
  [inst : TopologicalSpace M] →
    [inst_1 : ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M] →
      [inst_2 : IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M] →
        [T2Space M] →
          [CompactSpace M] →
            DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M →
              DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection →
                DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection → ℝ :=
fun {M} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M]
    [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M] [CompactSpace M] g h k ↦
  ∫ (x : M),
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairingDensity g h k
      x ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
      (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M g
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing_self.{u_1} {M : Type u_1} [TopologicalSpace M]
  [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M] [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M]
  [T2Space M] [CompactSpace M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing g h h =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g h
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing_symm.{u_1} {M : Type u_1} [TopologicalSpace M]
  [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M] [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M]
  [T2Space M] [CompactSpace M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M)
  (h k : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing g h k =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing g k h
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing_add_left.{u_1} {M : Type u_1} [TopologicalSpace M]
  [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M] [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M]
  [T2Space M] [CompactSpace M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M)
  (h k l : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing g (h + k) l =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing g h l +
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing g k l
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing_add_right.{u_1} {M : Type u_1} [TopologicalSpace M]
  [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M] [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M]
  [T2Space M] [CompactSpace M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M)
  (h k l : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing g h (k + l) =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing g h k +
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing g h l
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing_smul_left.{u_1} {M : Type u_1} [TopologicalSpace M]
  [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M] [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M]
  [T2Space M] [CompactSpace M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M) (c : ℝ)
  (h k : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing g (c • h) k =
    c * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing g h k
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing_smul_right.{u_1} {M : Type u_1} [TopologicalSpace M]
  [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M] [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M]
  [T2Space M] [CompactSpace M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M) (c : ℝ)
  (h k : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing g h (c • k) =
    c * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing g h k
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation_zero.{u_1} {M : Type u_1} [TopologicalSpace M]
  [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M] [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M]
  [T2Space M] [CompactSpace M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g 0 = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation_smul.{u_1} {M : Type u_1} [TopologicalSpace M]
  [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M] [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M]
  [T2Space M] [CompactSpace M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M) (c : ℝ)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g (c • h) =
    c ^ 2 * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g h
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation_add.{u_1} {M : Type u_1} [TopologicalSpace M]
  [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M] [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M]
  [T2Space M] [CompactSpace M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M)
  (h k : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g (h + k) =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g h +
        2 * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing g h k +
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g k
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation_sub.{u_1} {M : Type u_1} [TopologicalSpace M]
  [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M] [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M]
  [T2Space M] [CompactSpace M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M)
  (h k : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g (h - k) =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g h -
        2 * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing g h k +
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g k
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation_parallelogram.{u_1} {M : Type u_1} [TopologicalSpace M]
  [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M] [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M]
  [T2Space M] [CompactSpace M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M)
  (h k : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g (h + k) +
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g (h - k) =
    2 *
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g h + RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g k)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipationDensity_metric
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationDensity D.metric h x =
    DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S D.metric x 1
            ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.roughLap0SField D.metric h) x) +
          (1 / D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x)) ^ 2 *
            DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S D.metric x 1 (h x) -
        1 / D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
          DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S D.metric x 2
            ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S D.metric h) x) +
      2 * (1 / D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x)) *
        DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S D.metric x 2
          ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart D.metric
              (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S D.metric h))
            x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_eq_weightedRoundIntegral
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric h =
    ∫ (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
      D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationDensity D.metric h
          x ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
        (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.round
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.weighted_dissipationDensity_integrable
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  MeasureTheory.Integrable
    (fun x ↦
      D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationDensity D.metric h x)
    (DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      (↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.round)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.roughLap0SField_duSec_apply.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (f : M → ℝ) (hf : ContMDiff I (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ) (↑⊤) f)
  (x : M) (Y : TangentSpace I x) :
  (((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.roughLap0SField g (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.duSec f hf)) x)
      fun x ↦ Y) =
    ((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.differential1FormFun
          (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.laplacian (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricCov g) g f)
          x)
        fun x ↦ Y) +
      (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricRicciAt g x)
        (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.gradientFun g f x) Y)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.roughLap0SField_duSec_of_finrank_eq_two.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M]
  [I.Boundaryless] (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (hdim : Module.finrank ℝ E = 2) (f : M → ℝ)
  (hf : ContMDiff I (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ) (↑⊤) f) (x : M) :
  (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.roughLap0SField g (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.duSec f hf)) x =
    DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.differential1FormFun
        (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.laplacian (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricCov g) g f) x +
      (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt g x / 2) •
        (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.duSec f hf) x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.metricTracePair0SAt_product_one.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (x : M)
  (α β : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SSpace 1 I x) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.metricTracePair0SAt g (α.product β) =
    DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.inner0S g x 1 α β
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.inner0S_product_one.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (x : M)
  (α β γ δ : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SSpace 1 I x) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.inner0S g x 2 (α.product β) (γ.product δ) =
    DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.inner0S g x 1 α γ * DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.inner0S g x 1 β δ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.normSq0S_of_apply_eq_rankOne_add_metric.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (x : M)
  (T : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SSpace 2 I x)
  (α : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SSpace 1 I x) (u c : ℝ)
  (hT : ∀ (v : Fin 2 → TangentSpace I x), T v = ((u * α fun x ↦ v 0) * α fun x ↦ v 1) + c * ((g.inner x) (v 0)) (v 1)) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 2 T =
    u ^ 2 * DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 1 α ^ 2 +
        2 * u * c * DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 1 α +
      c ^ 2 * ↑(Module.finrank ℝ E)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.normSq0S_ahlforsPart_of_apply_eq_rankOne_add_metric.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M]
  (hdim : Module.finrank ℝ E = 2) (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (x : M)
  (T : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) 2)
  (α : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SSpace 1 I x) (u c : ℝ)
  (hT :
    ∀ (v : Fin 2 → TangentSpace I x), (T x) v = ((u * α fun x ↦ v 0) * α fun x ↦ v 1) + c * ((g.inner x) (v 0)) (v 1)) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 2 ((ahlforsPart g T) x) =
    u ^ 2 / 2 * DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 1 α ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.inverseMetricSharpFib_heightOneForm
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere) :
  (DifferentialGeometry.inverseMetricSharpFib D.metric x) (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.heightOneForm x) =
    (D.b (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) / D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) ^ 2) •
      DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.gradFun RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.round
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.heightOneForm_normSq
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S D.metric x 1 (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.heightOneForm x) =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) /
      D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.meridionalOneForm_normSq
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S D.metric x 1 ((D.meridionalOneForm r hr) x) =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
      r (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.meridionalWeight_hasDerivAt
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (v : ℝ) (hv : v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1) :
  HasDerivAt D.meridionalWeight (-2 * v - D.meridionalWeight v * deriv D.a v / D.a v) v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.meridionalDensity_intervalIntegrable
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) :
  IntervalIntegrable (D.meridionalDensity r) MeasureTheory.volume (-1) 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.meridionalAction_constant_one (c : ℝ) (hc : 0 < c) :
  ((constant c hc).meridionalAction fun x ↦ 1) = 4 / 3
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.duSec_meridionalPotential
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.duSec (D.meridionalPotential r) ⋯ = D.meridionalOneForm r hr
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.metricNabla0S_meridionalOneForm
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
  (slots : Fin 2 → TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) x) :
  ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm r hr)) x) slots =
    ((D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) * deriv r (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
          (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.heightOneForm x) fun x ↦ slots 0) *
        (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.heightOneForm x) fun x ↦ slots 1) -
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x * r (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
        ((D.metric.inner x) (slots 0)) (slots 1)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.meridionalOneForm_nabla_trace
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.metricTracePair0SAt D.metric
      ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm r hr)) x) =
    D.meridionalWeight (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
        deriv r (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) -
      2 * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x * r (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.meridionalOneForm_nabla_normSq
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S D.metric x 2
      ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm r hr)) x) =
    (D.meridionalWeight (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
            deriv r (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) -
          RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x * r (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x)) ^
        2 +
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x * r (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x)) ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.meridionalOneForm_ahlfors_normSq
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S D.metric x 2
      ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart D.metric
          (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm r hr)))
        x) =
    (D.meridionalWeight (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
          deriv r (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x)) ^
        2 /
      2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.laplacian_meridionalPotential
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.laplacian (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricCov D.metric) D.metric
      (D.meridionalPotential r) x =
    D.meridionalWeight (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
        deriv r (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) -
      2 * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x * r (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.roughLap0SField_meridionalOneForm
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere) :
  (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.roughLap0SField D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm r hr)) x =
    D.meridionalOperator r (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) •
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.heightOneForm x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipationDensity_meridional
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere) :
  D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationDensity D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm r hr) x =
    D.meridionalDensity r (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_meridional
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm r hr) =
    2 * Real.pi * D.meridionalAction r
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_constant_meridional_one (c : ℝ)
  (hc : 0 < c) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation (constant c hc).metric
      ((constant c hc).meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ 1) ⋯) =
    8 * Real.pi / 3
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.meridionalWeight : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData →
  ℝ → ℝ :=
fun D v ↦ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp D.a v / D.a v
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.meridionalOperator : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData →
  (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun D r v ↦ D.meridionalWeight v * deriv (deriv r) v + (deriv D.meridionalWeight v - 2 * v) * deriv r v - r v
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.meridionalDensity : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData →
  (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun D r v ↦
  D.meridionalWeight v * D.meridionalOperator r v ^ 2 + (D.meridionalWeight v - 2 * v ^ 2) * r v ^ 2 +
    2 * v * D.meridionalWeight v * r v * deriv r v
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.meridionalAction : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData →
  (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ :=
fun D r ↦ ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, D.meridionalDensity r v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] (f : Diffeomorph I I M M ↑⊤) {s : ℕ}
  (A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback_apply.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] (f : Diffeomorph I I M M ↑⊤) {s : ℕ}
  (A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) (x : M) (v : Fin s → TangentSpace I x) :
  ((diffeomorphTensorPullback f A) x) v = (A (f x)) fun i ↦ (mfderiv% ⇑f x) (v i)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback_refl.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}

  (A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) :
  diffeomorphTensorPullback (Diffeomorph.refl I M ↑⊤) A = A
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback_trans.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] (f g : Diffeomorph I I M M ↑⊤) {s : ℕ}
  (A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) :
  diffeomorphTensorPullback (f.trans g) A = diffeomorphTensorPullback f (diffeomorphTensorPullback g A)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback_symm_apply.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}

  diffeomorphTensorPullback f.symm (diffeomorphTensorPullback f A) = A
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback_apply_symm.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]

  diffeomorphTensorPullback f (diffeomorphTensorPullback f.symm A) = A
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.metricNabla0S_diffeomorphTensorPullback.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M]

      (diffeomorphTensorPullback f A) =
    diffeomorphTensorPullback f (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g A)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.metricNabla0S_twice_diffeomorphTensorPullback.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M]

  (A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) :
  DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric g f)
      (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric g f)
        (diffeomorphTensorPullback f A)) =
    diffeomorphTensorPullback f
      (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g A))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.roughLap0SField_diffeomorphTensorPullback.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}

      (diffeomorphTensorPullback f A) =
    diffeomorphTensorPullback f (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.roughLap0SField g A)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart_diffeomorphTensorPullback.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (f : Diffeomorph I I M M ↑⊤)
  (A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) 2) :
  ahlforsPart (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric g f) (diffeomorphTensorPullback f A) =
    diffeomorphTensorPullback f (ahlforsPart g A)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation_diffeomorphTensorPullback.{u_4} {S : Type u_4} [TopologicalSpace S]
  [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) S] [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) S]

  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) S)
  (f :
    Diffeomorph (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      S S ↑⊤)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  oneFormDissipation (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric g f) (diffeomorphTensorPullback f h) =
    oneFormDissipation g h
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing_diffeomorphTensorPullback_of_isometry.{u_4} {S : Type u_4}
  [TopologicalSpace S] [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) S]

  (h k : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  oneFormDissipationPairing g (diffeomorphTensorPullback f h) (diffeomorphTensorPullback f k) =

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.meridianReflectionSphereDiffeo_coe (x : ↑RotationalSphere) :
  ↑(meridianReflectionSphereDiffeo x) = !₂[(↑x).ofLp 0, -(↑x).ofLp 1, (↑x).ofLp 2]

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.metricNabla0S_eq_zero_of_alternating_const_normSq.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]

  (A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) 2)
  (hA :
    ∀ (x : M) (v w : TangentSpace I x),
      (A x) (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 v w) =
        -(A x) (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 w v))
  (k : ℝ) (hnorm : ∀ (x : M), DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 2 (A x) = k) :
  DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g A = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.normSq0S_eq_two_of_unit_alternating.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M]
  (hdim : Module.finrank ℝ E = 2) (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (x : M)
  (A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SSpace 2 I x)
  (hA :
    ∀ (v w : TangentSpace I x),
      A (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 v w) = -A (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 w v))
  (hunit :
    ∀ (basis : Module.Basis (Fin 2) ℝ (TangentSpace I x)),
      (∀ (i j : Fin 2), ((g.inner x) (basis i)) (basis j) = if i = j then 1 else 0) →
        A (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 (basis 0) (basis 1)) ^ 2 = 1) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 2 A = 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.roundSphereAreaForm_apply (x : ↑RotationalSphere)
  (u v : TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) x) :
  (roundSphereAreaForm x) (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 u v) =
    (↑x).ofLp 2 *
          (((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl x) u).ofLp 0 * ((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl x) v).ofLp 1 -
            ((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl x) u).ofLp 1 * ((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl x) v).ofLp 0) +
        (↑x).ofLp 0 *
          (((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl x) u).ofLp 1 * ((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl x) v).ofLp 2 -
            ((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl x) u).ofLp 2 * ((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl x) v).ofLp 1) +
      (↑x).ofLp 1 *
        (((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl x) u).ofLp 2 * ((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl x) v).ofLp 0 -
          ((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl x) u).ofLp 0 * ((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl x) v).ofLp 2)

  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.meridianReflectionSphereDiffeo =
    D.metric
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipationPairing_meridional_azimuthal
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (r s : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r)
  (hs : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm r hr)
      (D.azimuthalOneForm s hs) =
    0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_meridional_add_azimuthal
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (r s : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r)
  (hs : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm r hr + D.azimuthalOneForm s hs) =

      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.azimuthalOneForm s hs)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.areaForm_apply
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
  (slots : Fin 2 → TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) x) :
  (D.areaForm x) slots =
    D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) * (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.roundSphereAreaForm x) slots

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.dIncl_round_grad_sphereHeight (x : ↑RotationalSphere) :
  (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl x)
      (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.gradFun HeightMetricCoefficients.round sphereHeight x) =
    EuclideanSpace.single 2 1 - sphereHeight x • ↑x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.roundSphereAreaForm_round_grad_sphereHeight (x : ↑RotationalSphere)

      (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2
        (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.gradFun HeightMetricCoefficients.round sphereHeight x) v) =
    -(sphereAzimuthalOneForm x) fun x ↦ v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.normSq0S_one_eq_of_rotation.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] (hdim : Module.finrank ℝ E = 2)
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (x : M)
  (Ω : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SSpace 2 I x)
  (hΩ :
    ∀ (v w : TangentSpace I x),
      Ω (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 v w) = -Ω (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 w v))
  (hunit : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 2 Ω = 2)
  (α β : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SSpace 1 I x)
  (hRot :
    ∀ (Y : TangentSpace I x),
      (β fun x ↦ Y) =
        Ω (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 (DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.cotangentSharp g x α) Y)) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 1 β = DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 1 α

      Ω (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 v w) = -Ω (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 w v))

            (DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.cotangentSharp g x
              (((DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.tensor0SCurry 1 x) T) X))
            Y)) :

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormAreaContraction.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (Ω : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.TwoTensorSection)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormAreaContraction_apply.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (Ω : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.TwoTensorSection)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) (x : M) (Y : TangentSpace I x) :
  (((oneFormAreaContraction g Ω h) x) fun x ↦ Y) =
    (Ω x)
      (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 (DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.cotangentSharp g x (h x)) Y)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormAreaContraction_add.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (Ω : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.TwoTensorSection)
  (h k : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  oneFormAreaContraction g Ω (h + k) = oneFormAreaContraction g Ω h + oneFormAreaContraction g Ω k
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormAreaContraction_smul.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (Ω : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.TwoTensorSection)
  (c : ℝ) (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  oneFormAreaContraction g Ω (c • h) = c • oneFormAreaContraction g Ω h
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.metricNabla0S_oneFormAreaContraction.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M]
  (hdim : Module.finrank ℝ E = 2) (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M)
  (Ω : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.TwoTensorSection)
  (hΩalt :
    ∀ (x : M) (U V : TangentSpace I x),
      (Ω x) (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 U V) =
        -(Ω x) (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 V U))
  (hΩnorm : ∀ (x : M), DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 2 (Ω x) = 2)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) (x : M) (X Y : TangentSpace I x) :
  ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g (oneFormAreaContraction g Ω h)) x)
      (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 X Y) =
    (Ω x)
      (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2
        (DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.cotangentSharp g x
          (((DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.tensor0SCurry 1 x)
              ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g h) x))
            X))
        Y)

            X Y))

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.inverseMetricSharpFib_meridionalOneForm
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere) :
  (DifferentialGeometry.inverseMetricSharpFib D.metric x) ((D.meridionalOneForm r hr) x) =
    (D.b (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) / D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
        r (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x)) •
      DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.gradFun RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.round
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.areaForm_inverseMetricSharpFib_meridionalOneForm
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
  (v : TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) x) :
  (D.areaForm x)
      (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2
        ((DifferentialGeometry.inverseMetricSharpFib D.metric x) ((D.meridionalOneForm r hr) x)) v) =
    -((D.azimuthalOneForm r hr) x) fun x ↦ v

  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.hodgeRotation_apply
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)

  (((D.hodgeRotation h) x) fun x ↦ Y) =
    (D.areaForm x)
      (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2
        (DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.cotangentSharp D.metric x (h x)) Y)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.hodgeRotation_meridionalOneForm
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) :
  D.hodgeRotation (D.meridionalOneForm r hr) = -1 • D.azimuthalOneForm r hr
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_hodgeRotation
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.hodgeRotation h) =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric h
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_azimuthal_eq_meridional
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.azimuthalOneForm r hr) =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm r hr)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_azimuthal
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.azimuthalOneForm r hr) =
    2 * Real.pi * D.meridionalAction r
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_zonal
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (r s : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r)
  (hs : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm r hr + D.azimuthalOneForm s hs) =
    2 * Real.pi * (D.meridionalAction r + D.meridionalAction s)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.contMDiffOn_intervalIntegral.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} {E : Type u_1} {H : Type u_2}
  {M : Type u_3} {F : Type u_4} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} [I.Boundaryless] [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M]
  [NormedAddCommGroup F] [NormedSpace ℝ F] (n : ℕ∞) (a b : ℝ) {f : ℝ × M → F} {U : Set M} {S : Set ℝ} (hU : IsOpen U)
  (hS : IsOpen S) (hI : Set.uIcc a b ⊆ S)
  (hf : ContMDiffOn ((modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ).prod I) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ F) (↑n) f (S ×ˢ U)) :
  ContMDiffOn I (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ F) (↑n) (fun x ↦ ∫ (t : ℝ) in a..b, f (t, x)) U

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.axisRotation_apply (z : Circle) (x : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 3)) :
  (axisRotation z) x = !₂[(↑z).re * x.ofLp 0 - (↑z).im * x.ofLp 1, (↑z).im * x.ofLp 0 + (↑z).re * x.ofLp 1, x.ofLp 2]
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.axisRotationRepresentation :
  Circle →* EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 3) ≃ₗᵢ[ℝ] EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 3)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.circleSphereDiffeo_coe (z : Circle) (x : ↑RotationalSphere) :
  ↑((circleSphereDiffeo z) x) = (axisRotation z) ↑x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.circleSphereDiffeo_mul (z w : Circle) (x : ↑RotationalSphere) :
  (circleSphereDiffeo (z * w)) x = (circleSphereDiffeo z) ((circleSphereDiffeo w) x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.circleSphereDiffeo_inv (z : Circle) :
  circleSphereDiffeo z⁻¹ = (circleSphereDiffeo z).symm
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.circleSphereDiffeo_dIncl_mfderiv (z : Circle) (x : ↑RotationalSphere)
  (v : TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) x) :
  (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl ((circleSphereDiffeo z) x)) ((mfderiv% ⇑(circleSphereDiffeo z) x) v) =
    (axisRotation z) ((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl x) v)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.angleRotation_add (theta phi : ℝ) (x : ↑RotationalSphere) :
  (angleRotation (theta + phi)) x = (angleRotation theta) ((angleRotation phi) x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.angleRotation_periodic : Function.Periodic angleRotation (2 * Real.pi)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.angleRotation_contMDiff :
  ContMDiff ((modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ).prod (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))))
    (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) ↑⊤ fun p ↦ (angleRotation p.1) p.2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.mfderiv_family_zero_parameter.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4, u_5, u_6} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] {EP : Type u_4} [NormedAddCommGroup EP] [NormedSpace ℝ EP]
  {HP : Type u_5} [TopologicalSpace HP] {IP : ModelWithCorners ℝ EP HP} {P : Type u_6} [TopologicalSpace P]
  [ChartedSpace HP P] (f : P → Diffeomorph I I M M ↑⊤) (hf : ContMDiff (IP.prod I) I ↑⊤ fun p ↦ (f p.1) p.2) (t : P)
  (x : M) (v : TangentSpace I x) : ((mfderiv% fun p ↦ (f p.1) p.2) (t, x)) (0, v) = (mfderiv% ⇑(f t) x) v

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback_angleRotation_add (θ φ : ℝ) {s : ℕ}
  (A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) :
  diffeomorphTensorPullback (angleRotation (θ + φ)) A =
    diffeomorphTensorPullback (angleRotation θ) (diffeomorphTensorPullback (angleRotation φ) A)

    ((modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))).prod
      (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SModel s ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))))
    ↑⊤ fun p ↦ ⟨p.2, (diffeomorphTensorPullback (angleRotation p.1) A) p.2⟩

    ContMDiff ((modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ).prod I)
      (I.prod (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SModel 2 ℝ E))) ↑⊤ fun p ↦
      ⟨p.2, (A p.1) p.2⟩)
  (a b : ℝ) (x : M) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.metricTracePair0SAt g ((tensorIntervalIntegral A hA a b) x) =
    ∫ (t : ℝ) in a..b, DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.metricTracePair0SAt g ((A t) x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.roughLap0STensor_tensorIntervalIntegral_apply.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M]
  [I.Boundaryless] {s : ℕ} (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M)
  (A : ℝ → DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) (s + 2))
  (hA :
    ContMDiff ((modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ).prod I)
      (I.prod (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SModel (s + 2) ℝ E))) ↑⊤ fun p ↦
      ⟨p.2, (A p.1) p.2⟩)
  (a b : ℝ) (x : M) (tail : Fin s → TangentSpace I x) :
  (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.roughLap0STensor g ((tensorIntervalIntegral A hA a b) x)) tail =
    ∫ (t : ℝ) in a..b, (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.roughLap0STensor g ((A t) x)) tail
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart_tensorIntervalIntegral_apply.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M]
  [I.Boundaryless] (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M)
  (A : ℝ → DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) 2)
  (hA :
    ContMDiff ((modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ).prod I)
      (I.prod (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SModel 2 ℝ E))) ↑⊤ fun p ↦
      ⟨p.2, (A p.1) p.2⟩)
  (a b : ℝ) (x : M) (v : Fin 2 → TangentSpace I x) :
  ((ahlforsPart g (tensorIntervalIntegral A hA a b)) x) v = ∫ (t : ℝ) in a..b, ((ahlforsPart g (A t)) x) v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage_apply {s : ℕ}
  (A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) (x : ↑RotationalSphere)
  (v : Fin s → TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) x) :
  ((rotationalAverage A) x) v =
    (2 * Real.pi)⁻¹ *
      ∫ (θ : ℝ) in 0..2 * Real.pi, (A ((angleRotation θ) x)) fun i ↦ (mfderiv% ⇑(angleRotation θ) x) (v i)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage_add {s : ℕ}
  (A B : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) :
  rotationalAverage (A + B) = rotationalAverage A + rotationalAverage B
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage_smul {s : ℕ} (c : ℝ)
  (A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) : rotationalAverage (c • A) = c • rotationalAverage A
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback_circle_rotationalAverage (z : Circle) {s : ℕ}
  (A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) :
  diffeomorphTensorPullback (circleSphereDiffeo z) (rotationalAverage A) = rotationalAverage A
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage_eq_self_iff {s : ℕ}
  (A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) :
  rotationalAverage A = A ↔ ∀ (z : Circle), diffeomorphTensorPullback (circleSphereDiffeo z) A = A
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage_idempotent {s : ℕ}
  (A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) :
  rotationalAverage (rotationalAverage A) = rotationalAverage A
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage_sub_average {s : ℕ}
  (A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) : rotationalAverage (A - rotationalAverage A) = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.pullbackMetric_angleRotation
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (theta : ℝ) :

    D.metric

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.continuous_oneFormDissipationPairingDensity_angleRotation
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h k : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  Continuous fun p ↦
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairingDensity D.metric
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.angleRotation p.1) h)
      k p.2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.integrable_oneFormDissipationPairingDensity_angleRotation
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h k : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) (a b : ℝ) :
  MeasureTheory.Integrable
    (fun p ↦
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairingDensity D.metric
        (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.angleRotation p.1)
          h)
        k p.2)
    ((MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.uIoc a b)).prod
      (DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
        (↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere) D.metric))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.intervalIntegrable_oneFormDissipationPairing_angleRotation
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h k : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) (a b : ℝ) :
  IntervalIntegrable
    (fun θ ↦
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing D.metric
        (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.angleRotation θ) h)
        k)
    MeasureTheory.volume a b
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.integral_intervalIntegral_oneFormDissipationPairingDensity_angleRotation
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h k : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) (a b : ℝ) :
  ∫ (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
      ∫ (θ : ℝ) in a..b,
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairingDensity D.metric
          (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.angleRotation θ)
            h)
          k
          x ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
        (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
        D.metric =
    ∫ (θ : ℝ) in a..b,
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing D.metric
        (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.angleRotation θ) h)
        k
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.rotationalAverage_meridionalOneForm

  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) :

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.metricNabla0S_twice_rotationalAverage

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.ahlforsPart_metricNabla0S_rotationalAverage
