RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.exists_contDiff_even_sequence_Icc {a : ℝ → ℝ} (ha : UniformContinuous a)
  (heven : Function.Even a) {lo hi : ℝ} (hbox : ∀ (x : ℝ), lo ≤ a x ∧ a x ≤ hi) :
  ∃ f,
    (∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (f n) ∧ Function.Even (f n) ∧ ∀ (x : ℝ), lo ≤ f n x ∧ f n x ≤ hi) ∧
      TendstoUniformly f a Filter.atTop
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.evenReciprocalProfile (C v : ℝ) : ℝ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.evenReciprocalProfile_eq (C v : ℝ) (hv : v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1) :
  evenReciprocalProfile C v = Real.exp (-obstacleLogProfile C |v|)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.even_evenReciprocalProfile (C : ℝ) : Function.Even (evenReciprocalProfile C)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.uniformContinuous_evenReciprocalProfile (C : ℝ) :
  UniformContinuous (evenReciprocalProfile C)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.evenReciprocalProfile_bounds (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) (v : ℝ) :
  1 / C ≤ evenReciprocalProfile C v ∧ evenReciprocalProfile C v ≤ 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.balance_eq_zero_of_even (a : ℝ → ℝ) (he : Function.Even a) :
  balance a = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp_reflected_of_balance (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : Continuous a)
  (hb : balance a = 0) (v : ℝ) : warp (fun s ↦ a (-s)) v = warp a (-v)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.integral_warp_div_eq_pairFunctional_sum (a : ℝ → ℝ)
  (ha : Continuous a) (hpos : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < a v) (hb : balance a = 0) (M : ℝ) (hM : 0 < M) :
  ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, warp a v / a v =
    (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional fun v ↦ Real.log (M / a v)) +
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional fun v ↦ Real.log (M / a (-v))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.integral_warp_div_evenReciprocalProfile (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) :
  ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1,
      warp (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.evenReciprocalProfile C) v /
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.evenReciprocalProfile C v =
    2 * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.constantProbeIntegral_evenReciprocalProfile (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) :
  2 * Real.pi *
      ((2 *
          ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1,
            warp (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.evenReciprocalProfile C) v /
              RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.evenReciprocalProfile C v) -
        4 / 3) =
    8 * Real.pi *
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C) -
        1 / 3)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.continuousOn_warp {a : ℝ → ℝ} (ha : ContinuousOn a (Set.Icc (-1) 1)) :
  ContinuousOn (warp a) (Set.Icc (-1) 1)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.intervalIntegrable_warp_div {a : ℝ → ℝ}
  (ha : ContinuousOn a (Set.Icc (-1) 1)) (hpos : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < a v) :
  IntervalIntegrable (fun v ↦ warp a v / a v) MeasureTheory.volume (-1) 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.tendsto_integral_warp_div_of_tendstoUniformlyOn {A : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ}
  {a : ℝ → ℝ} {lo hi : ℝ} (hlo : 0 < lo) (hA : ∀ (n : ℕ), ContinuousOn (A n) (Set.Icc (-1) 1))
  (hbox : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, lo ≤ A n v ∧ A n v ≤ hi)
  (hlim : TendstoUniformlyOn A a Filter.atTop (Set.Icc (-1) 1)) :
  Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, warp (A n) v / A n v) Filter.atTop
    (nhds (∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, warp a v / a v))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.abs_integral_warp_div_sub_le {a b : ℝ → ℝ} {lo hi ε : ℝ}
  (hlo : 0 < lo) (ha : ContinuousOn a (Set.Icc (-1) 1)) (hb : ContinuousOn b (Set.Icc (-1) 1))
  (habox : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, lo ≤ a v ∧ a v ≤ hi) (hbbox : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, lo ≤ b v ∧ b v ≤ hi)
  (hclose : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, |a v - b v| ≤ ε) :
  |(∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, warp a v / a v) - ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, warp b v / b v| ≤ 8 * (1 / lo + hi / lo ^ 2) * ε
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.hemisphereDeficit_logProfile_eq_normalized_constant_zonal
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (m M : ℝ) (hm : 0 < m)
  (hb : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, m ≤ D.a v ∧ D.a v ≤ M) (c d : ℝ) (hcd : 0 < c ^ 2 + d ^ 2) :
  (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.hemisphereDeficit (M / m) fun v ↦ Real.log (M / D.a v)) =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric
            (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ c) ⋯ + D.azimuthalOneForm (fun x ↦ d) ⋯) /
          (4 * Real.pi * (c ^ 2 + d ^ 2)) +
        2 / 3 -
      2 *
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional
          (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile (M / m))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_constant_zonal_controls_evenExtension
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (m M : ℝ) (hm : 0 < m)
  (hb : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, m ≤ D.a v ∧ D.a v ≤ M) (c d : ℝ) (hcd : 0 < c ^ 2 + d ^ 2) :
  ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, (Real.log (M / D.a v) - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile (M / m) |v|) ^ 2 ≤
    (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric
              (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ c) ⋯ + D.azimuthalOneForm (fun x ↦ d) ⋯) /
            (4 * Real.pi * (c ^ 2 + d ^ 2)) +
          2 / 3 -
        2 *
          RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional
            (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile (M / m))) /
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.stabilityConstant (M / m)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_constant_zonal_controls_reflection
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (m M : ℝ) (hm : 0 < m)
  (hb : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, m ≤ D.a v ∧ D.a v ≤ M) (c d : ℝ) (hcd : 0 < c ^ 2 + d ^ 2) :
  ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, (Real.log (M / D.a v) - Real.log (M / D.a (-v))) ^ 2 ≤
    4 *
        (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric
                (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ c) ⋯ + D.azimuthalOneForm (fun x ↦ d) ⋯) /
              (4 * Real.pi * (c ^ 2 + d ^ 2)) +
            2 / 3 -
          2 *
            RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional
              (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile (M / m))) /
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.stabilityConstant (M / m)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_constant_zonal_controls_symmetrization
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (m M : ℝ) (hm : 0 < m)
  (hb : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, m ≤ D.a v ∧ D.a v ≤ M) (c d : ℝ) (hcd : 0 < c ^ 2 + d ^ 2) :
  ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, (Real.log (M / D.a v) - (Real.log (M / D.a v) + Real.log (M / D.a (-v))) / 2) ^ 2 ≤
    (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric
              (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ c) ⋯ + D.azimuthalOneForm (fun x ↦ d) ⋯) /
            (4 * Real.pi * (c ^ 2 + d ^ 2)) +
          2 / 3 -
        2 *
          RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional
            (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile (M / m))) /
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.stabilityConstant (M / m)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.constant_zonal_zero_of_sq_sum_eq_zero
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (c d : ℝ) (hcd : c ^ 2 + d ^ 2 = 0) :
  c = 0 ∧
    d = 0 ∧
      D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ c) ⋯ + D.azimuthalOneForm (fun x ↦ d) ⋯ = 0 ∧
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric
            (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ c) ⋯ + D.azimuthalOneForm (fun x ↦ d) ⋯) =
          0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.exists_smooth_even_reciprocal_profiles (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) :
  ∃ A,
    (∀ (n : ℕ),
        ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (A n) ∧ Function.Even (A n) ∧ (∀ (v : ℝ), 1 / C ≤ A n v ∧ A n v ≤ 1) ∧ balance (A n) = 0) ∧
      TendstoUniformly A (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.evenReciprocalProfile C) Filter.atTop
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_meridional_one_eq_integral
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ 1) ⋯) =
    2 * Real.pi *
      ((2 * ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp D.a v / D.a v) - 4 / 3)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.exists_poleData_unit_probe_recovery (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) :
  ∃ A D,
    (∀ (n : ℕ),
        (D n).a = A n ∧
          ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (A n) ∧ Function.Even (A n) ∧ (∀ (v : ℝ), 1 / C ≤ A n v ∧ A n v ≤ 1) ∧ balance (A n) = 0) ∧
      TendstoUniformly A (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.evenReciprocalProfile C) Filter.atTop ∧
        Filter.Tendsto
          (fun n ↦
            RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation (D n).metric ((D n).meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ 1) ⋯))
          Filter.atTop
          (nhds
            (8 * Real.pi *
              (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional
                  (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C) -
                1 / 3)))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.exists_negative_unit_meridional_of_criticalCap_lt (C : ℝ)
  (hC : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap < C) :
  have C₀ := (C + RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap) / 2;
  ∃ a D,
    D.a = a ∧
      ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) a ∧
        Function.Even a ∧
          (∀ (v : ℝ), 1 / C₀ ≤ a v ∧ a v ≤ 1) ∧
            balance a = 0 ∧
              D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ 1) ⋯ ≠ 0 ∧
                RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.IsConformalKillingOneForm D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ 1) ⋯) ∧
                  (∀ (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
                      1 ≤ DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt D.metric x / 2 ∧
                        DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt D.metric x / 2 ≤ C₀) ∧
                    C₀ < C ∧
                      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ 1) ⋯) <
                        0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.exists_critical_unit_probe_recovery :
  ∃ A D,
    (∀ (n : ℕ),
        (D n).a = A n ∧
          ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (A n) ∧
            Function.Even (A n) ∧
              (∀ (v : ℝ), 1 / RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap ≤ A n v ∧ A n v ≤ 1) ∧
                balance (A n) = 0 ∧
                  (D n).meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ 1) ⋯ ≠ 0 ∧
                    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.IsConformalKillingOneForm (D n).metric
                        ((D n).meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ 1) ⋯) ∧
                      (∀ (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
                          1 ≤ DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt (D n).metric x / 2 ∧
                            DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt (D n).metric x / 2 ≤
                              RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap) ∧
                        0 <
                          RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation (D n).metric
                            ((D n).meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ 1) ⋯)) ∧
      TendstoUniformly A
          (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.evenReciprocalProfile RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap)
          Filter.atTop ∧
        Filter.Tendsto
          (fun n ↦
            RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation (D n).metric ((D n).meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ 1) ⋯))
          Filter.atTop (nhds 0)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback_zero.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] (F : Diffeomorph I I M M ↑⊤) (s : ℕ) :
  diffeomorphTensorPullback F 0 = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback_neg.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] (F : Diffeomorph I I M M ↑⊤) {s : ℕ}
  (A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) :
  diffeomorphTensorPullback F (-A) = -diffeomorphTensorPullback F A
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback_sub.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] (F : Diffeomorph I I M M ↑⊤) {s : ℕ}
  (A B : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) :
  diffeomorphTensorPullback F (A - B) = diffeomorphTensorPullback F A - diffeomorphTensorPullback F B
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback_injective.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]

  (F : Diffeomorph I I M M ↑⊤) (s : ℕ) : Function.Injective (diffeomorphTensorPullback F)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback_eq_zero_iff.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]

  (F : Diffeomorph I I M M ↑⊤) {s : ℕ} (A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) :
  diffeomorphTensorPullback F A = 0 ↔ A = 0

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.intervalIntegrable_conjugatedAngleRotation_apply
  (F :
    Diffeomorph (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      ↑RotationalSphere ↑RotationalSphere ↑⊤)
  {s : ℕ} (A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) (x : ↑RotationalSphere)
  (v : Fin s → TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) x) (a b : ℝ) :

    (fun theta ↦
      (A ((conjugatedAngleRotation F theta) x)) fun i ↦ (mfderiv% ⇑(conjugatedAngleRotation F theta) x) (v i))
    MeasureTheory.volume a b
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage_eval
  (F :
    Diffeomorph (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      ↑RotationalSphere ↑RotationalSphere ↑⊤)
  {s : ℕ} (A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) (x : ↑RotationalSphere)
  (v : Fin s → TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) x) :
  ((conjugatedRotationalAverage F A) x) v =
    (2 * Real.pi)⁻¹ *
      ∫ (theta : ℝ) in 0..2 * Real.pi, ((diffeomorphTensorPullback (conjugatedAngleRotation F theta) A) x) v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage_apply
  (F :
    Diffeomorph (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      ↑RotationalSphere ↑RotationalSphere ↑⊤)
  {s : ℕ} (A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) (x : ↑RotationalSphere)
  (v : Fin s → TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) x) :
  ((conjugatedRotationalAverage F A) x) v =
    (2 * Real.pi)⁻¹ *
      ∫ (theta : ℝ) in 0..2 * Real.pi,
        (A ((conjugatedAngleRotation F theta) x)) fun i ↦ (mfderiv% ⇑(conjugatedAngleRotation F theta) x) (v i)

      ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑⊤)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F)
      (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F)
        (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h)) =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F)
        (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F)
          h))

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.areaForm_pullback_apply
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (F :
    Diffeomorph (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑⊤)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
  (slots : Fin 2 → TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) x) :
  ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F D.areaForm) x) slots =
    D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight (F x)) *
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.roundSphereAreaForm (F x)) fun i ↦ (mfderiv% ⇑F x) (slots i)

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_pullback_zonal
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (F :
    Diffeomorph (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑⊤)
  (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) (s : ℝ → ℝ) (hs : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F)
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.meridionalOneForm r hr) +
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.azimuthalOneForm s hs)) =
    2 * Real.pi * (D.meridionalAction r + D.meridionalAction s)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.exists_smooth_zonal_decomposition_conjugatedRotationalAverage
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (F :
    Diffeomorph (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑⊤)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  ∃ r s,
    ∃ (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) (hs : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s),
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h =
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.meridionalOneForm r hr) +
          RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.azimuthalOneForm s hs)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.exists_conjugated_haar_zonal_dissipation
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (F :
    Diffeomorph (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑⊤)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  ∃ r s,
    ∃ (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) (hs : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s),
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h =
          RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.meridionalOneForm r hr) +
            RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.azimuthalOneForm s hs) ∧
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F)
            h =
          2 * Real.pi * (D.meridionalAction r + D.meridionalAction s) +
            RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation
              (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F)
              (h - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h)

  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_eq_conjugatedRotationalAverage_iff_invariant
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (F :
    Diffeomorph (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.exists_conjugated_haar_zonal_lower_bound
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (F :
    Diffeomorph (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑⊤)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  ∃ r s,
    ∃ (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) (hs : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s),
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h =
          RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.meridionalOneForm r hr) +
            RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.azimuthalOneForm s hs) ∧
        2 * Real.pi * (D.meridionalAction r + D.meridionalAction s) ≤
            RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation
              (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F) h ∧
          (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation
                (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F) h =
              2 * Real.pi * (D.meridionalAction r + D.meridionalAction s) ↔
            h =
              RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.meridionalOneForm r hr) +
                RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.azimuthalOneForm s hs))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.isConformalKillingOneForm_conjugatedRotationalAverage
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (F :
    Diffeomorph (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑⊤)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection)
  (hCK :
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.IsConformalKillingOneForm
      (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F) h) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.IsConformalKillingOneForm (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F)
    (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.exists_conjugated_constant_zonal_of_conformalKilling
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (F :
    Diffeomorph (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑⊤)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection)
  (hCK :
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.IsConformalKillingOneForm
      (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F) h) :
  ∃ c d,
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h =
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ c) ⋯) +
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.azimuthalOneForm (fun x ↦ d) ⋯)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.IsRepresentedRotationalMetric.exists_haar_representation
  {g :
    DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere}
  (hg : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.IsRepresentedRotationalMetric g) :
  ∃ D F,
    g = DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F ∧
      (∀ (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection),
          RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g h =
              RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g
                  (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h) +
                RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g
                  (h - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h) ∧
            0 ≤
                RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g
                  (h - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h) ∧
              (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g
                      (h - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h) =
                    0 ↔
                  h - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h = 0) ∧
                (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g h =
                    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g
                      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h) ↔
                  ∀ (z : Circle),
                    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback
                        (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedCircleSphereDiffeo F z) h =
                      h)) ∧
        ∀ (r s : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) (hs : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s),
          RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g
              (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.meridionalOneForm r hr) +
                RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.azimuthalOneForm s hs)) =
            2 * Real.pi * (D.meridionalAction r + D.meridionalAction s)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.IsRepresentedRotationalMetric.exists_haar_zonal_decomposition
  {g :
    DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere}
  (hg : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.IsRepresentedRotationalMetric g)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  ∃ D F r s,
    ∃ (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) (hs : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s),
      g = DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F ∧
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h =
            RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.meridionalOneForm r hr) +
              RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.azimuthalOneForm s hs) ∧
          RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g h =
              2 * Real.pi * (D.meridionalAction r + D.meridionalAction s) +
                RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g
                  (h - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h) ∧
            2 * Real.pi * (D.meridionalAction r + D.meridionalAction s) ≤
                RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g h ∧
              (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g h =
                  2 * Real.pi * (D.meridionalAction r + D.meridionalAction s) ↔
                h =
                  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.meridionalOneForm r hr) +
                    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.azimuthalOneForm s hs))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.hemisphereDeficit_logProfile_eq_normalized_pullback_constant_zonal
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (F :
    Diffeomorph (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑⊤)
  (m M : ℝ) (hm : 0 < m) (hb : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, m ≤ D.a v ∧ D.a v ≤ M) (c d : ℝ) (hcd : 0 < c ^ 2 + d ^ 2) :
  (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.hemisphereDeficit (M / m) fun v ↦ Real.log (M / D.a v)) =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F)
            (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ c) ⋯) +
              RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.azimuthalOneForm (fun x ↦ d) ⋯)) /
          (4 * Real.pi * (c ^ 2 + d ^ 2)) +
        2 / 3 -
      2 *
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional
          (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile (M / m))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_pullback_constant_zonal_controls_evenExtension
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (F :
    Diffeomorph (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑⊤)
  (m M : ℝ) (hm : 0 < m) (hb : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, m ≤ D.a v ∧ D.a v ≤ M) (c d : ℝ) (hcd : 0 < c ^ 2 + d ^ 2) :
  ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, (Real.log (M / D.a v) - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile (M / m) |v|) ^ 2 ≤
    (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F)
              (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ c) ⋯) +
                RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.azimuthalOneForm (fun x ↦ d) ⋯)) /
            (4 * Real.pi * (c ^ 2 + d ^ 2)) +
          2 / 3 -
        2 *
          RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional
            (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile (M / m))) /
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.stabilityConstant (M / m)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_pullback_constant_zonal_controls_reflection
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (F :
    Diffeomorph (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))

              (4 * Real.pi * (c ^ 2 + d ^ 2)) +

            RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional
              (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile (M / m))) /
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.stabilityConstant (M / m)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_pullback_constant_zonal_controls_symmetrization
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (F :
    Diffeomorph (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))

              (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ c) ⋯) +

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.pullback_constant_zonal_zero_of_sq_sum_eq_zero
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (F :
    Diffeomorph (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑⊤)
  (c d : ℝ) (hcd : c ^ 2 + d ^ 2 = 0) :
  c = 0 ∧
    d = 0 ∧
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ c) ⋯) +
            RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.azimuthalOneForm (fun x ↦ d) ⋯) =
          0 ∧
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F)
            (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ c) ⋯) +
              RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.azimuthalOneForm (fun x ↦ d) ⋯)) =
          0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.IsRepresentedRotationalMetric.exists_stability_representation
  {g :
    DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere}
  (hg : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.IsRepresentedRotationalMetric g) :
  ∃ D F,
    g = DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F ∧
      (∀ (m M : ℝ),
          0 < m →
            (∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, m ≤ D.a v ∧ D.a v ≤ M) →
              ∀ (c d : ℝ),
                0 < c ^ 2 + d ^ 2 →
                  have B :=
                    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g
                            (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F
                                (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ c) ⋯) +
                              RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F
                                (D.azimuthalOneForm (fun x ↦ d) ⋯)) /
                          (4 * Real.pi * (c ^ 2 + d ^ 2)) +
                        2 / 3 -
                      2 *
                        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional
                          (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile (M / m));
                  (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.hemisphereDeficit (M / m) fun v ↦ Real.log (M / D.a v)) = B ∧
                    ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1,
                          (Real.log (M / D.a v) - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile (M / m) |v|) ^
                            2 ≤
                        B / RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.stabilityConstant (M / m) ∧
                      ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, (Real.log (M / D.a v) - Real.log (M / D.a (-v))) ^ 2 ≤
                          4 * B / RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.stabilityConstant (M / m) ∧
                        ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1,
                            (Real.log (M / D.a v) - (Real.log (M / D.a v) + Real.log (M / D.a (-v))) / 2) ^ 2 ≤
                          B / RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.stabilityConstant (M / m)) ∧
        ∀ (c d : ℝ),
          c ^ 2 + d ^ 2 = 0 →
            RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ c) ⋯) +
                  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.azimuthalOneForm (fun x ↦ d) ⋯) =
                0 ∧
              RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g
                  (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ c) ⋯) +
                    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.azimuthalOneForm (fun x ↦ d) ⋯)) =
                0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conformalKillingSafeCaps : Set ℝ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conformalKillingSafeCaps_eq :
  conformalKillingSafeCaps = Set.Icc 1 RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conformalKillingSafeCaps_nonempty : conformalKillingSafeCaps.Nonempty
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conformalKillingSafeCaps_bddAbove : BddAbove conformalKillingSafeCaps
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conformalKillingThreshold : ℝ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conformalKillingThreshold_eq_criticalCap :
  conformalKillingThreshold = RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conformalKillingThreshold_closedForm :
  conformalKillingThreshold = ((5 + √13) / 3) ^ (1 / 3) * Real.exp ((4 + 2 * √13) / 9)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.IsRepresentedRotationalMetric.oneFormDissipation_pos_of_conformalKilling
  {g :
    DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere}
  (hg : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.IsRepresentedRotationalMetric g) (κ Λ : ℝ) (hκ : 0 < κ)
  (hb :
    ∀ (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
      κ ≤ DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt g x / 2 ∧
        DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt g x / 2 ≤ Λ)
  (hcap : Λ / κ ≤ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection)
  (hCK : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.IsConformalKillingOneForm g h) (hne : h ≠ 0) :
  0 < RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g h
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.exists_represented_negative_unit_meridional (C : ℝ)
  (hC : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap < C) :
  have C₀ := (C + RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap) / 2;
  ∃ a D,
    D.a = a ∧
      IsRepresentedRotationalMetric D.metric ∧
        ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) a ∧
          Function.Even a ∧
            (∀ (v : ℝ), 1 / C₀ ≤ a v ∧ a v ≤ 1) ∧
              RotationalProfile.balance a = 0 ∧
                D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ 1) ⋯ ≠ 0 ∧
                  IsConformalKillingOneForm D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ 1) ⋯) ∧
                    (∀ (x : ↑RotationalSphere),
                        1 ≤ DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt D.metric x / 2 ∧
                          DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt D.metric x / 2 ≤ C₀) ∧
                      C₀ < C ∧ oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ 1) ⋯) < 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.exists_represented_critical_unit_probe_recovery :
  ∃ A D,
    (∀ (n : ℕ),
        (D n).a = A n ∧
          IsRepresentedRotationalMetric (D n).metric ∧
            ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (A n) ∧
              Function.Even (A n) ∧
                (∀ (v : ℝ), 1 / RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap ≤ A n v ∧ A n v ≤ 1) ∧
                  RotationalProfile.balance (A n) = 0 ∧
                    (D n).meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ 1) ⋯ ≠ 0 ∧
                      IsConformalKillingOneForm (D n).metric ((D n).meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ 1) ⋯) ∧
                        (∀ (x : ↑RotationalSphere),
                            1 ≤ DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt (D n).metric x / 2 ∧
                              DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt (D n).metric x / 2 ≤
                                RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap) ∧
                          0 < oneFormDissipation (D n).metric ((D n).meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ 1) ⋯)) ∧
      TendstoUniformly A
          (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.evenReciprocalProfile RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap)
          Filter.atTop ∧
        Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ oneFormDissipation (D n).metric ((D n).meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ 1) ⋯)) Filter.atTop
          (nhds 0)

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.warpSlope_zero (K : ℝ → ℝ) : warpSlope K 0 = 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.warpSlope_eq_neg_two_height (K : ℝ → ℝ) (x : ℝ) :
  warpSlope K x = -2 * heightCoordinate K x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.SecondPrimitive.integral_kernel_eq (u : ℝ → ℝ) (hu : Continuous u) (a x : ℝ) :
  ∫ (t : ℝ) in a..x, (x - t) * u t = (x * ∫ (t : ℝ) in a..x, u t) - ∫ (t : ℝ) in a..x, t * u t
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.SecondPrimitive.integral_kernel_hasDerivAt (u : ℝ → ℝ) (hu : Continuous u) (a x : ℝ) :
  HasDerivAt (fun y ↦ ∫ (t : ℝ) in a..y, (y - t) * u t) (∫ (t : ℝ) in a..x, u t) x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.SecondPrimitive.integral_contDiff (u : ℝ → ℝ) (hu : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) u) (a : ℝ) :
  ContDiff ℝ ↑⊤ fun x ↦ ∫ (t : ℝ) in a..x, u t
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.SecondPrimitive.integral_kernel_contDiff (u : ℝ → ℝ) (hu : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) u) (a : ℝ) :
  ContDiff ℝ ↑⊤ fun x ↦ ∫ (t : ℝ) in a..x, (x - t) * u t

  HasDerivAt (warpSlope K) (-2 * K x) x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.deriv_warp (K : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K) : deriv (warp K) = warpSlope K
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.deriv_warpSlope (K : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K) :
  deriv (warpSlope K) = fun x ↦ -2 * K x

  deriv (deriv (warp K)) = fun x ↦ -2 * K x

  Continuous (warpSlope K)

  (hpos : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 < K x) : StrictMonoOn (heightCoordinate K) (Set.Icc 0 1)

  (hpos : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 < K x) : StrictConcaveOn ℝ (Set.Icc 0 1) (warp K)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.warp_pos_of_moments (K : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hpos : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 < K x) (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2) (hone : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1)
  (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1) : 0 < warp K x

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.exists_contDiff_extension_Icc (f : ℝ → ℝ) (a b : ℝ) (hab : a < b)
  (hf : ContDiffOn ℝ (↑⊤) f (Set.Icc a b)) : ∃ F, ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) F ∧ Set.EqOn F f (Set.Icc a b)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.exists_contDiff_inverse_on_Icc (V : ℝ → ℝ) (a b : ℝ) (hab : a < b)
  (hV : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) V) (hpos : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc a b, 0 < deriv V x) :
  ∃ X,
    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) X ∧
      Set.MapsTo X (Set.Icc (V a) (V b)) (Set.Icc a b) ∧
        (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc a b, X (V x) = x) ∧
          (∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (V a) (V b), V (X v) = v) ∧
            X (V a) = a ∧ X (V b) = b ∧ ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (V a) (V b), deriv X v * deriv V (X v) = 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction_integrand_continuous (K r : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) :
  Continuous fun x ↦ meridionalActionDensity (warp K x) (warpSlope K x) (K x) (r x) (deriv r x) (deriv (deriv r) x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction_integrand_intervalIntegrable (K r : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hK : Continuous K) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) :
  IntervalIntegrable
    (fun x ↦ meridionalActionDensity (warp K x) (warpSlope K x) (K x) (r x) (deriv r x) (deriv (deriv r) x))
    MeasureTheory.volume 0 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.warp_two (x : ℝ) : warp (fun x ↦ 2) x = 2 * x - 2 * x ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.warpSlope_two (x : ℝ) : warpSlope (fun x ↦ 2) x = 2 - 4 * x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction_two_one : (meridionalAction (fun x ↦ 2) fun x ↦ 1) = 4 / 3
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.profile_warp_heightCoordinate (K : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hpos : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 < K x) (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (ha : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, D.a (heightCoordinate K x) = 1 / K x) (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp D.a (heightCoordinate K x) = warp K x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.deriv_profile_heightCoordinate (K : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) K)
  (hpos : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 < K x) (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (ha : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, D.a (heightCoordinate K x) = 1 / K x) (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  deriv D.a (heightCoordinate K x) = -deriv K x / K x ^ 3
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.deriv_probe_heightCoordinate (K r : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) (hpos : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 < K x) (R : ℝ → ℝ) (hR : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) R)
  (hprobe : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, R (heightCoordinate K x) = r x) (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  deriv R (heightCoordinate K x) = deriv r x / K x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.deriv_deriv_probe_heightCoordinate (K r : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) K)
  (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) (hpos : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 < K x) (R : ℝ → ℝ) (hR : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) R)
  (hprobe : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, R (heightCoordinate K x) = r x) (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  deriv (deriv R) (heightCoordinate K x) = deriv (deriv r) x / K x ^ 2 - deriv r x * deriv K x / K x ^ 3
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalWeight_heightCoordinate (K : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hpos : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 < K x) (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (ha : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, D.a (heightCoordinate K x) = 1 / K x) (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  D.meridionalWeight (heightCoordinate K x) = warp K x * K x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.deriv_meridionalWeight_heightCoordinate (K : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) K)
  (hpos : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 < K x) (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2)
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (ha : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, D.a (heightCoordinate K x) = 1 / K x) (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  deriv D.meridionalWeight (heightCoordinate K x) = warpSlope K x + warp K x * deriv K x / K x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalOperator_heightCoordinate (K r : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) K)
  (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) (hpos : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 < K x) (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2)
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (R : ℝ → ℝ) (hR : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) R)
  (ha : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, D.a (heightCoordinate K x) = 1 / K x)
  (hprobe : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, R (heightCoordinate K x) = r x) (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  D.meridionalOperator R (heightCoordinate K x) =
    (warp K x * deriv (deriv r) x + 2 * warpSlope K x * deriv r x - K x * r x) / K x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalDensity_heightCoordinate_mul_curvature (K r : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hK : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) K) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) (hpos : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 < K x)
  (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2) (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (R : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hR : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) R) (ha : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, D.a (heightCoordinate K x) = 1 / K x)
  (hprobe : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, R (heightCoordinate K x) = r x) (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  D.meridionalDensity R (heightCoordinate K x) * K x =
    meridionalActionDensity (warp K x) (warpSlope K x) (K x) (r x) (deriv r x) (deriv (deriv r) x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.oneFormDissipation_eq_areaAction_of_profile_probe (K r : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hK : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) K) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) (hpos : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 < K x)
  (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2) (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (R : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hR : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) R) (ha : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, D.a (heightCoordinate K x) = 1 / K x)
  (hprobe : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, R (heightCoordinate K x) = r x) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm R hR) =
    2 * Real.pi * meridionalAction K r
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_smooth_geometric_realization (K r : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) K)
  (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) (hpos : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 < K x) (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2)
  (hone : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1) :
  ∃ X D R,
    ∃ (hR : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) R),
      ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) X ∧
        heightCoordinate K 0 = -1 ∧
          heightCoordinate K 1 = 1 ∧
            X (-1) = 0 ∧
              X 1 = 1 ∧
                Set.MapsTo (heightCoordinate K) (Set.Icc 0 1) (Set.Icc (-1) 1) ∧
                  Set.MapsTo X (Set.Icc (-1) 1) (Set.Icc 0 1) ∧
                    (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1,
                        X (heightCoordinate K x) = x ∧
                          D.a (heightCoordinate K x) = 1 / K x ∧
                            R (heightCoordinate K x) = r x ∧
                              RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp D.a (heightCoordinate K x) =
                                warp K x) ∧
                      (∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, heightCoordinate K (X v) = v ∧ deriv X v * K (X v) = 1) ∧
                        R = r ∘ X ∧
                          (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1,
                              deriv D.a (heightCoordinate K x) = -deriv K x / K x ^ 3 ∧
                                deriv R (heightCoordinate K x) = deriv r x / K x ∧
                                  deriv (deriv R) (heightCoordinate K x) =
                                    deriv (deriv r) x / K x ^ 2 - deriv r x * deriv K x / K x ^ 3) ∧
                            (∀ (p : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
                                DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt D.metric p / 2 =
                                  K (X (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight p))) ∧
                              RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm R hR) =
                                2 * Real.pi * meridionalAction K r
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.round_unit_probe_normalization :
  have D := RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.constant (1 / 2) ⋯;
  D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ 1) ⋯ ≠ 0 ∧
    (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ 1) ⋯) =
        2 * Real.pi * meridionalAction (fun x ↦ 2) fun x ↦ 1) ∧
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ 1) ⋯) = 8 * Real.pi / 3
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.monotone_obstacleExponential (q : ℝ) (hq : 0 < q) : Monotone (obstacleExponential q)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.monotone_obstacleLogProfile (C : ℝ) : Monotone (obstacleLogProfile C)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.evenReciprocalProfile_eq_low (C v : ℝ) (hv : |v| ≤ lowerContact (capParameter C)) :
  evenReciprocalProfile C v = 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.evenReciprocalProfile_eq_high (C v : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C)
  (hv : upperContact (capParameter C) ≤ |v|) : evenReciprocalProfile C v = 1 / C
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.evenReciprocalProfile_at_zero (C : ℝ) : evenReciprocalProfile C 0 = 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.evenReciprocalProfile_at_one (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) : evenReciprocalProfile C 1 = 1 / C
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.evenReciprocalProfile_one (v : ℝ) : evenReciprocalProfile 1 v = 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.antitoneOn_evenReciprocalProfile (C : ℝ) :
  AntitoneOn (evenReciprocalProfile C) (Set.Icc 0 1)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.monotoneOn_evenReciprocalProfile (C : ℝ) :
  MonotoneOn (evenReciprocalProfile C) (Set.Icc (-1) 0)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.evenReciprocalProfile_central_plateau (C : ℝ) :
  0 < lowerContact (capParameter C) ∧
    lowerContact (capParameter C) < 1 ∧
      Set.EqOn (evenReciprocalProfile C) (fun x ↦ 1)
        (Set.Icc (-lowerContact (capParameter C)) (lowerContact (capParameter C)))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.evenReciprocalProfile_strict_average_bounds (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 < C) :
  ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, evenReciprocalProfile C v < 2 ∧ 2 < (∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, evenReciprocalProfile C v) * C
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.clampedIntervalInverse : (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun f a b x ↦ Function.invFunOn f (Set.Icc a b) (max (f a) (min (f b) x))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.clampedIntervalInverse_mem (f : ℝ → ℝ) (a b : ℝ) (hab : a ≤ b)
  (hf : ContinuousOn f (Set.Icc a b)) (hm : StrictMonoOn f (Set.Icc a b)) (x : ℝ) :
  clampedIntervalInverse f a b x ∈ Set.Icc a b
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.clampedIntervalInverse_apply (f : ℝ → ℝ) (a b : ℝ) (hab : a ≤ b)
  (hm : StrictMonoOn f (Set.Icc a b)) (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc a b) : clampedIntervalInverse f a b (f x) = x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.apply_clampedIntervalInverse (f : ℝ → ℝ) (a b : ℝ) (hab : a ≤ b)
  (hf : ContinuousOn f (Set.Icc a b)) (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc (f a) (f b)) : f (clampedIntervalInverse f a b x) = x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.clampedIntervalInverse_continuous (f : ℝ → ℝ) (a b : ℝ) (hab : a ≤ b)
  (hf : ContinuousOn f (Set.Icc a b)) (hm : StrictMonoOn f (Set.Icc a b)) : Continuous (clampedIntervalInverse f a b)
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.length : (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ :=
fun a ↦ ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, a v
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.coordinate : (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun a v ↦ (∫ (s : ℝ) in -1..v, a s) / length a
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.inverse : (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun a ↦ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.clampedIntervalInverse (coordinate a) (-1) 1
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.curvature : (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun a x ↦ length a / a (inverse a x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.length_pos (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : Continuous a)
  (hpos : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < a v) : 0 < length a

  HasDerivAt (coordinate a) (a v / length a) v

  Continuous (coordinate a)

  (hpos : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < a v) : coordinate a 1 = 1

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.curvature_coordinate (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : Continuous a)

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.integral_comp_coordinate (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : Continuous a)
