RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.AreaNormalization.oneFormDissipation_pullback_meridional_eq
  {D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData} (N : D.AreaNormalization)
  (F :
    Diffeomorph (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑⊤)
  (R : ℝ → ℝ) (hR : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) R) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F)
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.meridionalOneForm R hR)) =
    2 * Real.pi * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction N.curvature (N.probe R)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.AreaNormalization.meridionalAction_nonneg_of_normalized_safety
  {D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData} (N : D.AreaNormalization)
  (hsafe :
    ∀ (r : ℝ → ℝ),
      ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r →
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.probeMass N.curvature r = 1 →
          0 ≤ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction N.curvature r)
  (R : ℝ → ℝ) (hR : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) R) : 0 ≤ D.meridionalAction R
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.AreaNormalization.exists_conjugated_haar_area_dissipation
  {D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData} (N : D.AreaNormalization)
  (F :
    Diffeomorph (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑⊤)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  ∃ R S,
    ∃ (hR : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) R) (hS : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) S),
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h =
          RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.meridionalOneForm R hR) +
            RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.azimuthalOneForm S hS) ∧
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F)
              h =
            2 * Real.pi *
                (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction N.curvature (N.probe R) +
                  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction N.curvature (N.probe S)) +
              RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation
                (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F)
                (h - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h) ∧
          0 ≤
              RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation
                (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F)
                (h - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h) ∧
            (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation
                  (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F)
                  (h - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h) =
                0 ↔
              h - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h = 0)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.AreaNormalization.oneFormDissipation_nonneg_of_normalized_safety
  {D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData} (N : D.AreaNormalization)
  (F :
    Diffeomorph (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑⊤)
  (hsafe :
    ∀ (r : ℝ → ℝ),
      ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r →
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.probeMass N.curvature r = 1 →
          0 ≤ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction N.curvature r)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  0 ≤ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F) h
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_eq_normalizedAreaAction
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (R : ℝ → ℝ) (hR : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) R) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm R hR) =
    2 * Real.pi *
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction D.areaNormalization.curvature
        (D.areaNormalization.probe R)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.IsRepresentedRotationalMetric.exists_normalized_area_representation
  {g :
    DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere}
  (hg : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.IsRepresentedRotationalMetric g) :
  ∃ D F N,
    g = DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F ∧
      (∀ (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection),
          ∃ R S,
            ∃ (hR : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) R) (hS : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) S),
              RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h =
                  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.meridionalOneForm R hR) +
                    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.azimuthalOneForm S hS) ∧
                RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g h =
                    2 * Real.pi *
                        (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction N.curvature (N.probe R) +
                          RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction N.curvature (N.probe S)) +
                      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g
                        (h - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h) ∧
                  0 ≤
                      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g
                        (h - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h) ∧
                    (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation g
                          (h - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h) =
                        0 ↔
                      h - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.conjugatedRotationalAverage F h = 0)) ∧
        ∀ (κ Λ : ℝ),
          0 < κ →
            (∀ (p : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
                κ ≤ DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt g p / 2 ∧
                  DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt g p / 2 ≤ Λ) →
              0 < RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.length D.a * κ ∧
                (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1,
                    N.curvature x ∈
                      Set.Icc (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.length D.a * κ)
                        (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.length D.a * Λ)) ∧
                  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.length D.a * Λ /
                      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.length D.a * κ) =
                    Λ / κ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalSafeCaps_of_normalized_area_safety (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C)
  (hsafe :
    ∀ (K : ℝ → ℝ),
      ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) K →
        (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 < K x) →
          ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2 →
            ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1 →
              ∀ (κ Λ : ℝ),
                0 < κ →
                  (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, K x ∈ Set.Icc κ Λ) →
                    Λ / κ ≤ C →
                      ∀ (r : ℝ → ℝ),
                        ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r → AreaProfile.probeMass K r = 1 → 0 ≤ AreaProfile.meridionalAction K r) :
  C ∈ rotationalSafeCaps
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.areaAction_nonneg_of_rotationalSafeCap (C : ℝ) (hC : C ∈ rotationalSafeCaps)
  (K r : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) K) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) (hpos : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 < K x)
  (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2) (hone : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1) (κ Λ : ℝ) (hκ : 0 < κ)
  (hbox : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, K x ∈ Set.Icc κ Λ) (hratio : Λ / κ ≤ C) : 0 ≤ AreaProfile.meridionalAction K r
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.AreaNormalization.oneFormDissipation_meridional_hasDerivAt
  {D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData} (N : D.AreaNormalization) (R S : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hR : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) R) (hS : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) S) :
  HasDerivAt
    (fun t ↦
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm (fun v ↦ R v + t * S v) ⋯))
    (4 * Real.pi *
      ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1,
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.warp N.curvature x * N.probe S x *
          RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalJacobiOperator N.curvature (N.probe R) x)
    0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.AreaNormalization.oneFormDissipation_meridional_stationary_iff
  {D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData} (N : D.AreaNormalization) (R : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hR : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) R) :
  (∀ (S : ℝ → ℝ) (hS : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) S),
      deriv
          (fun t ↦
            RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm (fun v ↦ R v + t * S v) ⋯))
          0 =
        0) ↔
    ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1,
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalJacobiOperator N.curvature (N.probe R) x = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.AreaNormalization.oneFormDissipation_pullback_meridional_hasDerivAt
  {D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData} (N : D.AreaNormalization)
  (F :
    Diffeomorph (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere ↑⊤)
  (R S : ℝ → ℝ) (hR : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) R) (hS : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) S) :
  HasDerivAt
    (fun t ↦
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric D.metric F)
        (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback F (D.meridionalOneForm (fun v ↦ R v + t * S v) ⋯)))
    (4 * Real.pi *
      ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1,
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.warp N.curvature x * N.probe S x *
          RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalJacobiOperator N.curvature (N.probe R) x)
    0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.AreaNormalization.oneFormDissipation_div_normSq_eq
  {D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData} (N : D.AreaNormalization) (R : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hR : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) R) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm R hR) /
      ∫ (p : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
        DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S D.metric p 1
          ((D.meridionalOneForm R hR)
            p) ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
          (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
          D.metric =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.rayleighQuotient N.curvature (N.probe R) /
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.length D.a ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.AreaNormalization.oneFormDissipation_div_normSq_hasDerivAt
  {D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData} (N : D.AreaNormalization) (R S : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hR : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) R) (hS : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) S) (hne : D.meridionalOneForm R hR ≠ 0) :
  HasDerivAt
    (fun t ↦
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm (fun v ↦ R v + t * S v) ⋯) /
        ∫ (p : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
          DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S D.metric p 1
            ((D.meridionalOneForm (fun v ↦ R v + t * S v) ⋯)
              p) ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
            (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
            D.metric)
    (2 /
        (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.length D.a ^ 2 *
          RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.probeMass N.curvature (N.probe R)) *
      ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1,
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.warp N.curvature x * N.probe S x *
          (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalJacobiOperator N.curvature (N.probe R) x -
            RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.rayleighQuotient N.curvature (N.probe R) * N.probe R x))
    0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.AreaNormalization.oneFormDissipation_div_normSq_euler_eq_of_isLocalMin
  {D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData} (N : D.AreaNormalization) (R : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hR : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) R) (hne : D.meridionalOneForm R hR ≠ 0)
  (hmin :
    ∀ (S : ℝ → ℝ) (hS : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) S),
      IsLocalMin
        (fun t ↦
          RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm (fun v ↦ R v + t * S v) ⋯) /
            ∫ (p : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
              DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S D.metric p 1
                ((D.meridionalOneForm (fun v ↦ R v + t * S v) ⋯)
                  p) ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
                (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
                D.metric)
        0)
  (x : ℝ) :
  x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1 →
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalJacobiOperator N.curvature (N.probe R) x =
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.rayleighQuotient N.curvature (N.probe R) * N.probe R x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.integral_sq_le_parabolic_weighted_energy (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ 1 r) :
  ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, r x ^ 2 ≤
    (12 * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * (1 - x) * r x ^ 2) + 4 * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, (x * (1 - x)) ^ 2 * deriv r x ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.quadratic_le_warp_of_curvature_lower (K : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)
  (lo : ℝ) (hlower : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, lo ≤ K x) (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2)
  (hone : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1) (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) : lo * x * (1 - x) ≤ warp K x

  (hi : ℝ) (hupper : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, K x ≤ hi) (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2)
  (hone : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1) (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) : warp K x ≤ hi * x * (1 - x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.warp_bounds_of_curvature_bounds (K : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K) (lo hi : ℝ)
  (hbox : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, lo ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi) (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2)

  lo * x * (1 - x) ≤ warp K x ∧ warp K x ≤ hi * x * (1 - x)

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.warp_nonneg_le_two_mul_min (K : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)

  (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) : 0 ≤ warp K x ∧ warp K x ≤ 2 * min x (1 - x)

  2 / C ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ 2 * C

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.integral_warp_mul_probe_operator (K r : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hr : ContDiff ℝ 2 r) (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2) (hone : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1) :

    -probeGradientEnergy K r - ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, warp K x * K x * r x ^ 2

  0 ≤ probeOperatorEnergy K r
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.probeGradientEnergy_interpolation (K r : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hr : ContDiff ℝ 2 r) (hnonneg : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 ≤ K x) (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2)
  (hone : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1) (t : ℝ) (ht : 0 ≤ t) :
  4 * t * probeGradientEnergy K r ≤ 4 * t ^ 2 * probeOperatorEnergy K r + probeMass K r
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.integral_sq_le_probeMass_gradient (K r : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hr : ContDiff ℝ 1 r) (lo : ℝ) (hlo : 0 ≤ lo) (hlower : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, lo ≤ K x)

  lo ^ 2 * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, r x ^ 2 ≤ 12 * lo * probeMass K r + 4 * probeGradientEnergy K r

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.polarProbeMass_le_four_mul_radius (K r : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hr : Continuous r) (hnonneg : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 ≤ K x) (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2)

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.probeOperatorEnergy_sub_le_action (K r : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hr : ContDiff ℝ 2 r) (hi : ℝ) (hbox : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi) (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2)
  (hone : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1) :
  probeOperatorEnergy K r - hi * probeGradientEnergy K r - 3 * hi * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, r x ^ 2 ≤ meridionalAction K r
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.actionMassCorrection (lo hi : ℝ) : ℝ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.actionMassCorrection_nonneg (lo hi : ℝ) (hlo : 0 < lo) (hhi : 0 ≤ hi) :
  0 ≤ actionMassCorrection lo hi
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction_coercive (K r : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hr : ContDiff ℝ 2 r) (lo hi : ℝ) (hlo : 0 < lo) (hbox : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, lo ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi)
  (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2) (hone : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1) :
  (probeOperatorEnergy K r + ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, r x ^ 2) / 4 ≤
    meridionalAction K r + actionMassCorrection lo hi * probeMass K r
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_uniform_action_coercivity (lo hi : ℝ) (hlo : 0 < lo) (hhi : 0 ≤ hi) :
  ∃ c > 0,
    ∃ C ≥ 0,
      ∀ (K r : ℝ → ℝ),
        Continuous K →
          ContDiff ℝ 2 r →
            (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, lo ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi) →
              ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2 →
                ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1 →
                  c * (probeOperatorEnergy K r + ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, r x ^ 2) ≤ meridionalAction K r + C * probeMass K r
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.probe_energies_le_of_unit_mass_action_le (K r : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hr : ContDiff ℝ 2 r) (lo hi : ℝ) (hlo : 0 < lo) (hbox : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, lo ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi)
  (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2) (hone : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1) (hmass : probeMass K r = 1) (B : ℝ)
  (hA : meridionalAction K r ≤ B) :
  probeOperatorEnergy K r ≤ 4 * (B + actionMassCorrection lo hi) ∧
    ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, r x ^ 2 ≤ 4 * (B + actionMassCorrection lo hi)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.polarProbeMass_le_of_unit_mass_action_le (K r : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hr : ContDiff ℝ 2 r) (lo hi : ℝ) (hlo : 0 < lo) (hbox : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, lo ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi)
  (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2) (hone : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1) (hmass : probeMass K r = 1) (B : ℝ)
  (hA : meridionalAction K r ≤ B) (δ : ℝ) (hδ0 : 0 ≤ δ) (hδ1 : δ ≤ 1) :
  polarProbeMass K r δ ≤ 16 * δ * (B + actionMassCorrection lo hi)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_uniform_polar_mass_radius (lo hi : ℝ) (hlo : 0 < lo) (hhi : 0 ≤ hi)
  (B ε : ℝ) (hε : 0 < ε) :
  ∃ δ > 0,
    δ ≤ 1 ∧
      ∀ (K r : ℝ → ℝ),
        Continuous K →
          ContDiff ℝ 2 r →
            (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, lo ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi) →
              ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2 →
                ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1 →
                  probeMass K r = 1 → meridionalAction K r ≤ B → ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 δ, polarProbeMass K r t ≤ ε
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction_coercive_of_curvature_ratio (K r : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hK : Continuous K) (hr : ContDiff ℝ 2 r) (kappa Lambda C : ℝ) (hkappa : 0 < kappa) (hC : 1 ≤ C)
  (hbox : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, kappa ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ Lambda) (hratio : Lambda / kappa ≤ C)
  (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2) (hone : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1) :
  (probeOperatorEnergy K r + ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, r x ^ 2) / 4 ≤
    meridionalAction K r + actionMassCorrection (2 / C) (2 * C) * probeMass K r

  (hε : 0 < ε) :
  ∃ δ > 0,
    δ ≤ 1 ∧
      ∀ (K r : ℝ → ℝ),
        Continuous K →
          ContDiff ℝ 2 r →
            ∀ (kappa Lambda : ℝ),
              0 < kappa →
                (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, kappa ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ Lambda) →
                  Lambda / kappa ≤ C →
                    ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2 →
                      ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1 →
                        probeMass K r = 1 → meridionalAction K r ≤ B → ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 δ, polarProbeMass K r t ≤ ε
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.integral_parabolic_mul_sq_le (p : ℝ → ℝ) (hp : ContDiff ℝ 1 p) :
  ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * (1 - x) * p x ^ 2 ≤
    (10 * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, (x * (1 - x)) ^ 2 * p x ^ 2) + ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, (x * (1 - x)) ^ 3 * deriv p x ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.integral_left_sq_le_parabolic_energy (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ 1 r) {δ t : ℝ}
  (hδ0 : 0 ≤ δ) (hδ1 : δ ≤ 1) (ht : 0 < t) (hδt : δ ≤ t ^ 2) :
  ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..δ, r x ^ 2 ≤
    (δ * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, r x ^ 2) +
      t * ((∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, r x ^ 2) + ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * (1 - x) * deriv r x ^ 2)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.integral_polar_sq_le_parabolic_energy (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ 1 r) {δ t : ℝ}
  (hδ0 : 0 ≤ δ) (hδ1 : δ ≤ 1) (ht : 0 < t) (hδt : δ ≤ t ^ 2) :
  (∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..δ, r x ^ 2) + ∫ (x : ℝ) in 1 - δ..1, r x ^ 2 ≤
    (2 * δ * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, r x ^ 2) +
      2 * t * ((∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, r x ^ 2) + ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * (1 - x) * deriv r x ^ 2)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.integral_warp_operator_add_curvature_sq (K r : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hr : ContDiff ℝ 2 r) (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2) (hone : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1) :
  ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, warp K x * (meridionalOperator K r x + K x * r x) ^ 2 =
    (∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, warp K x ^ 3 * deriv (deriv r) x ^ 2) +
      4 * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, warp K x ^ 2 * K x * deriv r x ^ 2

  (hr : ContDiff ℝ 2 r) (hi : ℝ) (hbox : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi) (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2)
  (hone : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1) :
  ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, warp K x ^ 3 * deriv (deriv r) x ^ 2 ≤ 2 * probeOperatorEnergy K r + 2 * hi ^ 2 * probeMass K r

  (hr : ContDiff ℝ 2 r) (lo hi : ℝ) (hlo : 0 < lo) (hbox : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, lo ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi)
  (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2) (hone : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1) :

    (10 * lo + 2) * probeOperatorEnergy K r + (5 * lo / 2 + 2 * hi ^ 2) * probeMass K r
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalActionRemainder (K r : ℝ → ℝ) (x : ℝ) : ℝ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalActionRemainder_continuous (K r : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hr : ContDiff ℝ 1 r) : Continuous (meridionalActionRemainder K r)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.polarActionRemainder (K r : ℝ → ℝ) (δ : ℝ) : ℝ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.polarActionRemainder_le (K r : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hr : ContDiff ℝ 1 r) (hi : ℝ) (hbox : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi) (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2)
  (hone : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1) (a : ℝ) (ha : 0 < a) (δ : ℝ) (hδ0 : 0 ≤ δ) (hδ1 : δ ≤ 1) :
  polarActionRemainder K r δ ≤
    (hi / a + 2 * hi + hi ^ 2) * ((∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..δ, r x ^ 2) + ∫ (x : ℝ) in 1 - δ..1, r x ^ 2) +
      2 * hi * a * probeGradientEnergy K r
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_uniform_probe_regularity_bound (lo hi : ℝ) (hlo : 0 < lo)
  (hhi : 0 ≤ hi) (B : ℝ) :
  ∃ M > 0,
    ∀ (K r : ℝ → ℝ),
      Continuous K →
        ContDiff ℝ 2 r →
          (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, lo ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi) →
            ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2 →
              ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1 →
                probeMass K r = 1 →
                  meridionalAction K r ≤ B →
                    probeOperatorEnergy K r ≤ M ∧
                      ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, r x ^ 2 ≤ M ∧
                        probeGradientEnergy K r ≤ M ∧ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * (1 - x) * deriv r x ^ 2 ≤ M
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_uniform_polar_action_radius (lo hi : ℝ) (hlo : 0 < lo) (hhi : 0 ≤ hi)
  (B ε : ℝ) (hε : 0 < ε) :
  ∃ δ > 0,
    δ ≤ 1 ∧
      ∀ (K r : ℝ → ℝ),
        Continuous K →
          ContDiff ℝ 2 r →
            (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, lo ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi) →
              ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2 →
                ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1 →
                  probeMass K r = 1 →
                    meridionalAction K r ≤ B →
                      ∀ s ∈ Set.Icc 0 δ,
                        (∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..s, r x ^ 2) + ∫ (x : ℝ) in 1 - s..1, r x ^ 2 ≤ ε ∧
                          polarActionRemainder K r s ≤ ε
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_uniform_polar_action_radius_of_ratio (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) (B ε : ℝ)
  (hε : 0 < ε) :
  ∃ δ > 0,
    δ ≤ 1 ∧
      ∀ (K r : ℝ → ℝ),
        Continuous K →
          ContDiff ℝ 2 r →
            ∀ (kappa Lambda : ℝ),
              0 < kappa →
                (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, kappa ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ Lambda) →
                  Lambda / kappa ≤ C →
                    ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2 →
                      ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1 →
                        probeMass K r = 1 →
                          meridionalAction K r ≤ B →
                            ∀ s ∈ Set.Icc 0 δ,
                              (∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..s, r x ^ 2) + ∫ (x : ℝ) in 1 - s..1, r x ^ 2 ≤ ε ∧
                                polarActionRemainder K r s ≤ ε
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.integrable_mul_of_ae_abs_le {μ : MeasureTheory.Measure ℝ} {K g : ℝ → ℝ} {B : ℝ}

  MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ K x * g x) μ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.abs_integral_mul_sub_le_of_ae_abs_le {μ : MeasureTheory.Measure ℝ} {K g h : ℝ → ℝ}
  {B : ℝ} (hK : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable K μ) (hB : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂μ, |K x| ≤ B)
  (hg : MeasureTheory.Integrable g μ) (hh : MeasureTheory.Integrable h μ) :
  |∫ (x : ℝ), K x * g x ∂μ - ∫ (x : ℝ), K x * h x ∂μ| ≤ B * ∫ (x : ℝ), ‖g x - h x‖ ∂μ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.tendsto_integral_mul_of_bounded_L2_pairings {μ : MeasureTheory.Measure ℝ}
  [MeasureTheory.IsFiniteMeasure μ] [μ.WeaklyRegular] {K : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ} {K0 : ℝ → ℝ} {B : ℝ}
  (hK : ∀ (n : ℕ), MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (K n) μ) (hK0 : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable K0 μ)
  (hKB : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂μ, |K n x| ≤ B) (hK0B : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂μ, |K0 x| ≤ B)
  (hpair :
    ∀ (v : ↥(MeasureTheory.Lp ℝ 2 μ)),
      Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ ∫ (x : ℝ), K n x * ↑↑v x ∂μ) Filter.atTop (nhds (∫ (x : ℝ), K0 x * ↑↑v x ∂μ)))
  (g : ℝ → ℝ) :
  MeasureTheory.Integrable g μ →
    Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ ∫ (x : ℝ), K n x * g x ∂μ) Filter.atTop (nhds (∫ (x : ℝ), K0 x * g x ∂μ))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HilbertWeakSubsequence.norm_le_of_tendsto_inner.{u_1} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [InnerProductSpace ℝ E] (u : ℕ → E) (x : E) (B : ℝ) (hbound : ∀ (n : ℕ), ‖u n‖ ≤ B)
  (hweak : ∀ (v : E), Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ inner ℝ (u n) v) Filter.atTop (nhds (inner ℝ x v))) : ‖x‖ ≤ B
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HilbertWeakSubsequence.exists_subseq_tendsto_inner_of_norm_le.{u_1} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [InnerProductSpace ℝ E] [CompleteSpace E] [TopologicalSpace.SeparableSpace E] (u : ℕ → E)
  (B : ℝ) (hbound : ∀ (n : ℕ), ‖u n‖ ≤ B) :
  ∃ x,
    ‖x‖ ≤ B ∧
      ∃ φ, StrictMono φ ∧ ∀ (v : E), Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ inner ℝ (u (φ n)) v) Filter.atTop (nhds (inner ℝ x v))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.exists_subseq_locallyUniform_of_local_derivative_energy (r : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ)
  (hr : ∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ 1 (r n))
  (hlocal :
    ∀ (a b : ℝ),
      0 < a →
        a < b → b < 1 → ∃ M, ∀ (n : ℕ), ∫ (x : ℝ) in a..b, r n x ^ 2 ≤ M ∧ ∫ (x : ℝ) in a..b, deriv (r n) x ^ 2 ≤ M) :
  ∃ g φ, StrictMono φ ∧ TendstoLocallyUniformly (fun n x ↦ r (φ n) ↑x) (⇑g) Filter.atTop
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.mul_integral_sq_le_parabolic_energy (v : ℝ → ℝ) (hv : Continuous v) (a b : ℝ)
  (ha : 0 < a) (hab : a < b) (hb : b < 1) (k : ℕ) :
  (a * (1 - b)) ^ k * ∫ (x : ℝ) in a..b, v x ^ 2 ≤ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, (x * (1 - x)) ^ k * v x ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.exists_subseq_locallyUniform_probe_deriv_of_weighted_energy (r : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ)
  (hr : ∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ 2 (r n)) (M : ℝ)
  (hbound :
    ∀ (n : ℕ),
      ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, r n x ^ 2 ≤ M ∧
        ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * (1 - x) * deriv (r n) x ^ 2 ≤ M ∧
          ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, (x * (1 - x)) ^ 3 * deriv (deriv (r n)) x ^ 2 ≤ M) :
  ∃ g p φ,
    StrictMono φ ∧
      TendstoLocallyUniformly (fun n x ↦ r (φ n) ↑x) (⇑g) Filter.atTop ∧
        TendstoLocallyUniformly (fun n x ↦ deriv (r (φ n)) ↑x) (⇑p) Filter.atTop
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.sq_sub_le_length_mul_integral_deriv_sq (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ 1 r) (a b : ℝ)
  (hab : a ≤ b) : (r b - r a) ^ 2 ≤ (b - a) * ∫ (t : ℝ) in a..b, deriv r t ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.sq_sub_le_abs_mul_integral_deriv_sq (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ 1 r) (a b x y : ℝ)
  (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc a b) (hy : y ∈ Set.Icc a b) : (r x - r y) ^ 2 ≤ |x - y| * ∫ (t : ℝ) in a..b, deriv r t ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.length_mul_sq_le_integral_sq_add_integral_deriv_sq (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ 1 r)
  (a b x : ℝ) (hab : a < b) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc a b) :
  (b - a) * r x ^ 2 ≤ (2 * ∫ (t : ℝ) in a..b, r t ^ 2) + 2 * (b - a) ^ 2 * ∫ (t : ℝ) in a..b, deriv r t ^ 2

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives (r p z : ℝ → ℝ) : Prop
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives : (ℝ → ℝ) → (ℝ → ℝ) → (ℝ → ℝ) → Prop :=
fun r p z ↦
  HasWeakDerivOn (Set.Ioo 0 1) r p ∧
    HasWeakDerivOn (Set.Ioo 0 1) p z ∧
      MeasureTheory.MemLp r 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
        MeasureTheory.MemLp (fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * p x) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
          MeasureTheory.MemLp (fun x ↦ x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * z x) 2
            (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives.integrable_sq {r p z : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r p z) :
  MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ r x ^ 2) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives.integrable_weighted_deriv_sq {r p z : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r p z) :
  MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ x * (1 - x) * p x ^ 2) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives.integrable_weighted_secondDeriv_sq {r p z : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r p z) :
  MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ (x * (1 - x)) ^ 3 * z x ^ 2) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.hasParabolicWeakDerivatives_of_contDiff {r : ℝ → ℝ} (hr : ContDiff ℝ 2 r) :
  HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r (deriv r) (deriv (deriv r))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives.ae_unique {r p z p' z' : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r p z) (h' : HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r p' z') :
  p =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)] p' ∧ z =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)] z'
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives.memLp_deriv_compact {r p z : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r p z) {K : Set ℝ} (hK : IsCompact K) (hKI : K ⊆ Set.Ioo 0 1) :
  MeasureTheory.MemLp p 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict K)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives.memLp_secondDeriv_compact {r p z : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r p z) {K : Set ℝ} (hK : IsCompact K) (hKI : K ⊆ Set.Ioo 0 1) :
  MeasureTheory.MemLp z 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict K)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.integrable_mul_of_locallyIntegrableOn {U : Set ℝ} {f φ : ℝ → ℝ}
  (hf : MeasureTheory.LocallyIntegrableOn f U MeasureTheory.volume) (hφ : Continuous φ) (hφc : HasCompactSupport φ)
  (hφU : tsupport φ ⊆ U) : MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ φ x * f x) MeasureTheory.volume
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasWeakDerivOn (U : Set ℝ) (r p : ℝ → ℝ) : Prop
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasWeakDerivOn : Set ℝ → (ℝ → ℝ) → (ℝ → ℝ) → Prop :=
fun U r p ↦
  MeasureTheory.LocallyIntegrableOn r U MeasureTheory.volume ∧
    MeasureTheory.LocallyIntegrableOn p U MeasureTheory.volume ∧
      ∀ (φ : ℝ → ℝ),
        ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) φ → HasCompactSupport φ → tsupport φ ⊆ U → ∫ (x : ℝ), deriv φ x * r x = -∫ (x : ℝ), φ x * p x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasWeakDerivOn.integrable_deriv_test_mul {U : Set ℝ} {r p φ : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : HasWeakDerivOn U r p) (hφ : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) φ) (hφc : HasCompactSupport φ) (hφU : tsupport φ ⊆ U) :
  MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ deriv φ x * r x) MeasureTheory.volume
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasWeakDerivOn.integrable_test_mul_weakDeriv {U : Set ℝ} {r p φ : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : HasWeakDerivOn U r p) (hφ : Continuous φ) (hφc : HasCompactSupport φ) (hφU : tsupport φ ⊆ U) :
  MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ φ x * p x) MeasureTheory.volume
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasWeakDerivOn.ae_unique {U : Set ℝ} {r p q : ℝ → ℝ} (hp : HasWeakDerivOn U r p)
  (hq : HasWeakDerivOn U r q) (hU : IsOpen U) : p =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.volume.restrict U] q

  (hr : ContDiffOn ℝ 1 r U) : HasWeakDerivOn U r (deriv r)

  (hr : ContDiff ℝ 1 r) : HasWeakDerivOn U r (deriv r)

  (hrs : r =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.volume.restrict U] s) (hpq : p =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.volume.restrict U] q) : HasWeakDerivOn U s q

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_curvature_subsequence_of_ratio_bound (K : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ) (κ Λ : ℕ → ℝ)
  (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C)
  (hmeas : ∀ (n : ℕ), MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (K n) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hκ : ∀ (n : ℕ), 0 < κ n)
  (hbox : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), κ n ≤ K n x ∧ K n x ≤ Λ n)
  (hratio : ∀ (n : ℕ), Λ n / κ n ≤ C) (hmass : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, K n x = 2)
  (hmoment : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, x * K n x = 1) :
  ∃ K0 κ0 Λ0 φ,
    MeasureTheory.MemLp K0 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
      StrictMono φ ∧
        Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ κ (φ n)) Filter.atTop (nhds κ0) ∧
          Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ Λ (φ n)) Filter.atTop (nhds Λ0) ∧
            (∀ (v : ↥(MeasureTheory.Lp ℝ 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))),
                Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, K (φ n) x * ↑↑v x) Filter.atTop
                  (nhds (∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, K0 x * ↑↑v x))) ∧
              ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, K0 x = 2 ∧
                ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, x * K0 x = 1 ∧
                  κ0 ∈ Set.Icc (2 / C) 2 ∧
                    Λ0 ∈ Set.Icc 2 (2 * C) ∧
                      0 < κ0 ∧
                        Λ0 / κ0 ≤ C ∧ ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), κ0 ≤ K0 x ∧ K0 x ≤ Λ0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_normalized_joint_weak_limit (K r : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ) (κ Λ : ℕ → ℝ) (C B : ℝ)
  (hC : 1 ≤ C) (hK : ∀ (n : ℕ), Continuous (K n)) (hr : ∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ 2 (r n)) (hκ : ∀ (n : ℕ), 0 < κ n)
  (hbox : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, κ n ≤ K n x ∧ K n x ≤ Λ n) (hratio : ∀ (n : ℕ), Λ n / κ n ≤ C)
  (hzero : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K n x = 2) (hone : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K n x = 1)
  (hmass : ∀ (n : ℕ), probeMass (K n) (r n) = 1) (hA : ∀ (n : ℕ), meridionalAction (K n) (r n) ≤ B) :
  ∃ K0 r0 p0 z0 κ0 Λ0 φ,
    StrictMono φ ∧
      MeasureTheory.MemLp K0 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
        MeasureTheory.MemLp K0 ⊤ (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
          0 < κ0 ∧
            Λ0 / κ0 ≤ C ∧
              (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), κ0 ≤ K0 x ∧ K0 x ≤ Λ0) ∧
                Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ κ (φ n)) Filter.atTop (nhds κ0) ∧
                  Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ Λ (φ n)) Filter.atTop (nhds Λ0) ∧
                    ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K0 x = 2 ∧
                      ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K0 x = 1 ∧
                        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r0 p0 z0 ∧
                          ContinuousOn r0 (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
                            ContinuousOn p0 (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
                              ContinuousOn (warp K0) (Set.Icc 0 1) ∧
                                ContinuousOn (warpSlope K0) (Set.Icc 0 1) ∧
                                  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasWeakDerivOn (Set.Ioo 0 1) (warp K0)
                                      (warpSlope K0) ∧
                                    (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasWeakDerivOn (Set.Ioo 0 1) (warpSlope K0) fun x ↦
                                        -2 * K0 x) ∧
                                      IntervalIntegrable
                                          (fun x ↦
                                            meridionalActionDensity (warp K0 x) (warpSlope K0 x) (K0 x) (r0 x) (p0 x)
                                              (z0 x))
                                          MeasureTheory.volume 0 1 ∧
                                        MeasureTheory.MemLp
                                            (fun x ↦ √(warp K0 x) * weakMeridionalOperator K0 r0 p0 z0 x) 2
                                            (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
                                          MeasureTheory.MemLp (fun x ↦ √(warp K0 x) * (K0 x * r0 x)) 2
                                              (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
                                            (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1,
                                                2 / C * x * (1 - x) ≤ warp K0 x ∧
                                                  warp K0 x ≤ 2 * C * x * (1 - x) ∧ |warpSlope K0 x| ≤ 2) ∧
                                              (warp K0 0 = 0 ∧
                                                  warp K0 1 = 0 ∧ warpSlope K0 0 = 2 ∧ warpSlope K0 1 = -2) ∧
                                                MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ warp K0 x * r0 x ^ 2)
                                                    (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
                                                  probeMass K0 r0 = 1 ∧
                                                    (∃ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, r0 x ≠ 0) ∧
                                                      Filter.Tendsto
                                                          (fun n ↦
                                                            MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (r (φ n) - r0) 2
                                                              (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                                                          Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
                                                        TendstoLocallyUniformlyOn (fun n ↦ r (φ n)) r0 Filter.atTop
                                                            (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
                                                          TendstoLocallyUniformlyOn (fun n ↦ deriv (r (φ n))) p0
                                                              Filter.atTop (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
                                                            TendstoUniformlyOn (fun n ↦ warp (K (φ n))) (warp K0)
                                                                Filter.atTop (Set.Icc 0 1) ∧
                                                              TendstoUniformlyOn (fun n ↦ warpSlope (K (φ n)))
                                                                  (warpSlope K0) Filter.atTop (Set.Icc 0 1) ∧
                                                                (∀ (v : ℝ → ℝ),
                                                                    MeasureTheory.Integrable v
                                                                        (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) →
                                                                      Filter.Tendsto
                                                                        (fun n ↦
                                                                          ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, K (φ n) x * v x)
                                                                        Filter.atTop
                                                                        (nhds (∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, K0 x * v x))) ∧
                                                                  ∀
                                                                    (v :
                                                                      ↥(MeasureTheory.Lp ℝ 2
                                                                          (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict
                                                                            (Set.Ioo 0 1)))),
                                                                    Filter.Tendsto
                                                                      (fun n ↦
                                                                        ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1,
                                                                          x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) *
                                                                              deriv (deriv (r (φ n))) x *
                                                                            ↑↑v x)
                                                                      Filter.atTop
                                                                      (nhds
                                                                        (∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1,
                                                                          x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * z0 x * ↑↑v x))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_subseq_locallyUniform_probe_deriv_of_action_bound (K r : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ)
  (hK : ∀ (n : ℕ), Continuous (K n)) (hr : ∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ 2 (r n)) (lo hi : ℝ) (hlo : 0 < lo)
  (hbox : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, lo ≤ K n x ∧ K n x ≤ hi) (hzero : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K n x = 2)
  (hone : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K n x = 1) (hmass : ∀ (n : ℕ), probeMass (K n) (r n) = 1) (B : ℝ)
  (hA : ∀ (n : ℕ), meridionalAction (K n) (r n) ≤ B) :
  ∃ g p φ,
    StrictMono φ ∧
      TendstoLocallyUniformly (fun n x ↦ r (φ n) ↑x) (⇑g) Filter.atTop ∧
        TendstoLocallyUniformly (fun n x ↦ deriv (r (φ n)) ↑x) (⇑p) Filter.atTop

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.memLp_weak_operator_and_potential_of_warp_bounds (K r p z : ℝ → ℝ) (hi : ℝ)
  (hgraph : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r p z) (hhi : 0 ≤ hi)
  (hK : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable K (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hf : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (warp K) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hb : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (warpSlope K) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hKbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), 0 ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi)
  (hfbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), 0 ≤ warp K x ∧ warp K x ≤ hi * (x * (1 - x)))
  (hbbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), |warpSlope K x| ≤ 2) :
  MeasureTheory.MemLp (fun x ↦ √(warp K x) * weakMeridionalOperator K r p z x) 2
      (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
    MeasureTheory.MemLp (fun x ↦ √(warp K x) * (K x * r x)) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1))

  (hi : ℝ) (hgraph : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r p z) (hhi : 0 ≤ hi)
  (hK : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable K (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hf : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (warp K) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hb : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (warpSlope K) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hKbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), 0 ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi)
  (hfbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), 0 ≤ warp K x ∧ warp K x ≤ hi * (x * (1 - x)))
  (hbbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), |warpSlope K x| ≤ 2) :
  MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ meridionalActionDensity (warp K x) (warpSlope K x) (K x) (r x) (p x) (z x))
    (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakMeridionalAction_intervalIntegrable_of_warp_bounds (K r p z : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hi : ℝ) (hgraph : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r p z) (hhi : 0 ≤ hi)
  (hK : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable K (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hf : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (warp K) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hb : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (warpSlope K) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hKbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), 0 ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi)
  (hfbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), 0 ≤ warp K x ∧ warp K x ≤ hi * (x * (1 - x)))
  (hbbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), |warpSlope K x| ≤ 2) :
  IntervalIntegrable (fun x ↦ meridionalActionDensity (warp K x) (warpSlope K x) (K x) (r x) (p x) (z x))
    MeasureTheory.volume 0 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakMeridionalAction_decomposition_of_warp_bounds (K r p z : ℝ → ℝ) (hi : ℝ)
  (hgraph : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r p z) (hhi : 0 ≤ hi)
  (hK : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable K (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hf : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (warp K) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hb : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (warpSlope K) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hKbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), 0 ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi)
  (hfbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), 0 ≤ warp K x ∧ warp K x ≤ hi * (x * (1 - x)))
  (hbbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), |warpSlope K x| ≤ 2) :
  weakMeridionalAction K r p z =
    ((∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, warp K x * (weakMeridionalOperator K r p z x ^ 2 + (K x * r x) ^ 2)) -
        ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, K x * warpSlope K x * warp K x * r x * p x) -
      1 / 2 * ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, K x * warpSlope K x ^ 2 * r x ^ 2

  {μ : MeasureTheory.Measure α} {a : ℕ → α → ℝ} {a0 : α → ℝ} {D : ℝ}

  {μ : MeasureTheory.Measure α} {u : ℕ → α → ℝ} {u0 : α → ℝ} {a : ℕ → α → ℝ} {a0 : α → ℝ} {B D : ℝ}
  (hu : ∀ (n : ℕ), MeasureTheory.MemLp (u n) 2 μ) (hu0 : MeasureTheory.MemLp u0 2 μ)
  (hB : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∫ (x : α), u n x ^ 2 ∂μ ≤ B)
  (hweak :
    ∀ (v : ↥(MeasureTheory.Lp ℝ 2 μ)),
      Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ ∫ (x : α), u n x * ↑↑v x ∂μ) Filter.atTop (nhds (∫ (x : α), u0 x * ↑↑v x ∂μ)))
  (ha : ∀ (n : ℕ), MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (a n) μ) (ha0 : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable a0 μ)
  (hD : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∀ᵐ (x : α) ∂μ, |a n x| ≤ D) (hD0 : ∀ᵐ (x : α) ∂μ, |a0 x| ≤ D)

    MeasureTheory.MemLp (fun x ↦ a0 x * u0 x) 2 μ ∧
      ∀ (v : ↥(MeasureTheory.Lp ℝ 2 μ)),
        Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ ∫ (x : α), a n x * u n x * ↑↑v x ∂μ) Filter.atTop
          (nhds (∫ (x : α), a0 x * u0 x * ↑↑v x ∂μ))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_uniform_parabolic_graph_bound (lo hi : ℝ) (hlo : 0 < lo)
  (hhi : 0 ≤ hi) (B : ℝ) :
  ∃ M > 0,
    ∀ (K r : ℝ → ℝ),
      Continuous K →
        ContDiff ℝ 2 r →
          (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, lo ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi) →
            ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2 →
              ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1 →
                probeMass K r = 1 →
                  meridionalAction K r ≤ B →
                    ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, r x ^ 2 ≤ M ∧
                      ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, (√(x * (1 - x)) * deriv r x) ^ 2 ≤ M ∧
                        ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, (x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (deriv r) x) ^ 2 ≤ M
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weak_derivative_part_of_area_limits (K r : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ) (K0 r0 p0 z0 : ℝ → ℝ)
  (lo hi B : ℝ) (hlo : 0 < lo) (hK : ∀ (n : ℕ), Continuous (K n)) (hr : ∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ 2 (r n))
  (hbox : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, lo ≤ K n x ∧ K n x ≤ hi) (hzero : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K n x = 2)
  (hone : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K n x = 1) (hmass : ∀ (n : ℕ), probeMass (K n) (r n) = 1)
  (hAction : ∀ (n : ℕ), meridionalAction (K n) (r n) ≤ B)
  (hgraph : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r0 p0 z0)
  (hf : ∀ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ warp (K n) x) Filter.atTop (nhds (warp K0 x)))
  (hb : ∀ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ warpSlope (K n) x) Filter.atTop (nhds (warpSlope K0 x)))
  (hp : ∀ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ deriv (r n) x) Filter.atTop (nhds (p0 x)))
  (hz :
    ∀ (v : ↥(MeasureTheory.Lp ℝ 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))),
      Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (deriv (r n)) x * ↑↑v x)
        Filter.atTop (nhds (∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * z0 x * ↑↑v x))) :
  (∀ (n : ℕ),
      MeasureTheory.MemLp
        (fun x ↦ √(warp (K n) x) * (warp (K n) x * deriv (deriv (r n)) x + 2 * warpSlope (K n) x * deriv (r n) x)) 2
        (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1))) ∧
    MeasureTheory.MemLp (fun x ↦ √(warp K0 x) * (warp K0 x * z0 x + 2 * warpSlope K0 x * p0 x)) 2
        (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
      ∀ (v : ↥(MeasureTheory.Lp ℝ 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))),
        Filter.Tendsto
          (fun n ↦
            ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1,
              √(warp (K n) x) * (warp (K n) x * deriv (deriv (r n)) x + 2 * warpSlope (K n) x * deriv (r n) x) * ↑↑v x)
          Filter.atTop
          (nhds (∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, √(warp K0 x) * (warp K0 x * z0 x + 2 * warpSlope K0 x * p0 x) * ↑↑v x))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.curvature_action_pairings_tendsto (K r : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ) (K0 r0 p0 z0 : ℝ → ℝ)
  (lo hi B : ℝ) (hlo : 0 < lo) (hK : ∀ (n : ℕ), Continuous (K n)) (hr : ∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ 2 (r n))
  (hbox : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, lo ≤ K n x ∧ K n x ≤ hi) (hzero : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K n x = 2)
  (hone : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K n x = 1) (hmass : ∀ (n : ℕ), probeMass (K n) (r n) = 1)
  (hA : ∀ (n : ℕ), meridionalAction (K n) (r n) ≤ B)
  (hgraph : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r0 p0 z0)
  (hstrong :

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_normalized_joint_weak_limit_le_liminf_action (K r : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ)
  (κ Λ : ℕ → ℝ) (C B : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) (hK : ∀ (n : ℕ), Continuous (K n)) (hr : ∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ 2 (r n))
  (hκ : ∀ (n : ℕ), 0 < κ n) (hbox : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, κ n ≤ K n x ∧ K n x ≤ Λ n)
  (hratio : ∀ (n : ℕ), Λ n / κ n ≤ C) (hzero : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K n x = 2)
  (hone : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K n x = 1) (hmass : ∀ (n : ℕ), probeMass (K n) (r n) = 1)
  (hA : ∀ (n : ℕ), meridionalAction (K n) (r n) ≤ B) :
  ∃ K0 r0 p0 z0 κ0 Λ0 φ,
    StrictMono φ ∧
      MeasureTheory.MemLp K0 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
        MeasureTheory.MemLp K0 ⊤ (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
          0 < κ0 ∧
            Λ0 / κ0 ≤ C ∧
              (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), κ0 ≤ K0 x ∧ K0 x ≤ Λ0) ∧
                Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ κ (φ n)) Filter.atTop (nhds κ0) ∧
                  Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ Λ (φ n)) Filter.atTop (nhds Λ0) ∧
                    ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K0 x = 2 ∧
                      ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K0 x = 1 ∧
                        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r0 p0 z0 ∧
                          ContinuousOn r0 (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
                            ContinuousOn p0 (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
                              ContinuousOn (warp K0) (Set.Icc 0 1) ∧
                                ContinuousOn (warpSlope K0) (Set.Icc 0 1) ∧
                                  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasWeakDerivOn (Set.Ioo 0 1) (warp K0)
                                      (warpSlope K0) ∧

                                        -2 * K0 x) ∧

                                              (warp K0 0 = 0 ∧
                                                  warp K0 1 = 0 ∧ warpSlope K0 0 = 2 ∧ warpSlope K0 1 = -2) ∧
                                                MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ warp K0 x * r0 x ^ 2)
                                                    (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
                                                  probeMass K0 r0 = 1 ∧

                                                              (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))

                                                            (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧

                                                              Filter.atTop (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧

                                                                Filter.atTop (Set.Icc 0 1) ∧

                                                                  (warpSlope K0) Filter.atTop (Set.Icc 0 1) ∧
                                                                (∀ (v : ℝ → ℝ),
                                                                    MeasureTheory.Integrable v
                                                                        (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) →
                                                                      Filter.Tendsto

                                                                        Filter.atTop
                                                                        (nhds (∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, K0 x * v x))) ∧
                                                                  (∀
                                                                      (v :
                                                                        ↥(MeasureTheory.Lp ℝ 2
                                                                            (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict
                                                                              (Set.Ioo 0 1)))),
                                                                      Filter.Tendsto

          (∀ (n : ℕ), ∑ i ∈ s n, w n i = 1) ∧

              Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ ∑ i ∈ s n, w n i • u i) Filter.atTop (nhds x)

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.smooth_area_pair_convexCombination.{u_1} {ι : Type u_1} (s : Finset ι)
  (w : ι → ℝ) (K r : ι → ℝ → ℝ) (κ Λ : ι → ℝ) (C : ℝ) (hw : ∀ i ∈ s, 0 ≤ w i) (hsum : ∑ i ∈ s, w i = 1)
  (hK : ∀ i ∈ s, ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (K i)) (hr : ∀ i ∈ s, ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (r i)) (hκ : ∀ i ∈ s, 0 < κ i)
  (hbox : ∀ i ∈ s, ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, κ i ≤ K i x ∧ K i x ≤ Λ i) (hratio : ∀ i ∈ s, Λ i / κ i ≤ C)
  (hzero : ∀ i ∈ s, ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K i x = 2) (hone : ∀ i ∈ s, ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K i x = 1) :
  (ContDiff ℝ ↑⊤ fun x ↦ ∑ i ∈ s, w i * K i x) ∧
    (ContDiff ℝ ↑⊤ fun x ↦ ∑ i ∈ s, w i * r i x) ∧
      ((deriv fun x ↦ ∑ i ∈ s, w i * r i x) = fun x ↦ ∑ i ∈ s, w i * deriv (r i) x) ∧
        (deriv (deriv fun x ↦ ∑ i ∈ s, w i * r i x) = fun x ↦ ∑ i ∈ s, w i * deriv (deriv (r i)) x) ∧
          0 < ∑ i ∈ s, w i * κ i ∧
            (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, ∑ i ∈ s, w i * κ i ≤ ∑ i ∈ s, w i * K i x ∧ ∑ i ∈ s, w i * K i x ≤ ∑ i ∈ s, w i * Λ i) ∧
              (∑ i ∈ s, w i * Λ i) / ∑ i ∈ s, w i * κ i ≤ C ∧
                ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, ∑ i ∈ s, w i * K i x = 2 ∧ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * ∑ i ∈ s, w i * K i x = 1

  [MeasurableSpace α] {μ : MeasureTheory.Measure α} {ι : Type u_2} [Finite ι] (f : ℕ → ι → α → ℝ) (g : ι → α → ℝ)
  (hf : ∀ (n : ℕ) (i : ι), MeasureTheory.MemLp (f n i) 2 μ) (hg : ∀ (i : ι), MeasureTheory.MemLp (g i) 2 μ)
  (hweak :
    ∀ (i : ι) (v : ↥(MeasureTheory.Lp ℝ 2 μ)),
      Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ ∫ (x : α), f n i x * ↑↑v x ∂μ) Filter.atTop (nhds (∫ (x : α), g i x * ↑↑v x ∂μ))) :
  ∃ s w,
    (∀ (n i : ℕ), 0 ≤ w n i) ∧
      (∀ (n i : ℕ), i ∉ s n → w n i = 0) ∧
        (∀ (n i : ℕ), i ∈ s n → n ≤ i) ∧
          (∀ (n : ℕ), ∑ i ∈ s n, w n i = 1) ∧
            (∀ (n : ℕ) (j : ι), MeasureTheory.MemLp (fun x ↦ ∑ i ∈ s n, w n i * f i j x) 2 μ) ∧
              ∀ (j : ι),
                Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (fun x ↦ ∑ i ∈ s n, w n i * f i j x - g j x) 2 μ)
                  Filter.atTop (nhds 0)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_smooth_geometric_recovery_of_scalar_limits (K r : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ)
  (κ Λ : ℕ → ℝ) (C A₀ : ℝ) (hK : ∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (K n)) (hr : ∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (r n))
  (hκ : ∀ (n : ℕ), 0 < κ n) (hbox : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, K n x ∈ Set.Icc (κ n) (Λ n))
  (hratio : ∀ (n : ℕ), Λ n / κ n ≤ C) (hzero : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K n x = 2)
  (hone : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K n x = 1)
  (hmass : Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ probeMass (K n) (r n)) Filter.atTop (nhds 1))
  (hAction : Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ meridionalAction (K n) (r n)) Filter.atTop (nhds A₀)) :
  ∃ N α X D R,
    ∃ (hR : ∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (R n)),
      (∀ (n : ℕ), α n = 1 / √(probeMass (K (n + N)) (r (n + N)))) ∧
        (∀ (n : ℕ), 0 < α n) ∧
          Filter.Tendsto α Filter.atTop (nhds 1) ∧
            (∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ ↑⊤ fun x ↦ α n * r (n + N) x) ∧
              (∀ (n : ℕ), (probeMass (K (n + N)) fun x ↦ α n * r (n + N) x) = 1) ∧
                (∀ (n : ℕ), ∃ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, α n * r (n + N) x ≠ 0) ∧
                  (∀ (n : ℕ),
                      (meridionalAction (K (n + N)) fun x ↦ α n * r (n + N) x) =
                        α n ^ 2 * meridionalAction (K (n + N)) (r (n + N))) ∧
                    Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ meridionalAction (K (n + N)) fun x ↦ α n * r (n + N) x) Filter.atTop
                        (nhds A₀) ∧
                      (∀ (n : ℕ),
                          0 < κ (n + N) ∧
                            Λ (n + N) / κ (n + N) ≤ C ∧
                              (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, K (n + N) x ∈ Set.Icc (κ (n + N)) (Λ (n + N))) ∧
                                ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K (n + N) x = 2 ∧ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K (n + N) x = 1) ∧
                        (∀ (n : ℕ),
                            ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (X n) ∧
                              heightCoordinate (K (n + N)) 0 = -1 ∧
                                heightCoordinate (K (n + N)) 1 = 1 ∧
                                  X n (-1) = 0 ∧
                                    X n 1 = 1 ∧
                                      Set.MapsTo (heightCoordinate (K (n + N))) (Set.Icc 0 1) (Set.Icc (-1) 1) ∧
                                        Set.MapsTo (X n) (Set.Icc (-1) 1) (Set.Icc 0 1) ∧
                                          (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1,
                                              X n (heightCoordinate (K (n + N)) x) = x ∧
                                                (D n).a (heightCoordinate (K (n + N)) x) = 1 / K (n + N) x ∧
                                                  R n (heightCoordinate (K (n + N)) x) = α n * r (n + N) x ∧
                                                    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp (D n).a
                                                        (heightCoordinate (K (n + N)) x) =
                                                      warp (K (n + N)) x) ∧
                                            (∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1,
                                                heightCoordinate (K (n + N)) (X n v) = v ∧
                                                  deriv (X n) v * K (n + N) (X n v) = 1) ∧
                                              R n = (fun x ↦ α n * r (n + N) x) ∘ X n ∧
                                                (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1,
                                                    deriv (D n).a (heightCoordinate (K (n + N)) x) =
                                                        -deriv (K (n + N)) x / K (n + N) x ^ 3 ∧
                                                      deriv (R n) (heightCoordinate (K (n + N)) x) =
                                                          deriv (fun x ↦ α n * r (n + N) x) x / K (n + N) x ∧
                                                        deriv (deriv (R n)) (heightCoordinate (K (n + N)) x) =
                                                          deriv (deriv fun x ↦ α n * r (n + N) x) x / K (n + N) x ^ 2 -
                                                            deriv (fun x ↦ α n * r (n + N) x) x * deriv (K (n + N)) x /
                                                              K (n + N) x ^ 3) ∧
                                                  (∀ (p : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
                                                      DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt
                                                            (D n).metric p /
                                                          2 =
                                                        K (n + N)
                                                          (X n (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight p))) ∧
                                                    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation (D n).metric
                                                        ((D n).meridionalOneForm (R n) ⋯) =
                                                      2 * Real.pi *
                                                        meridionalAction (K (n + N)) fun x ↦ α n * r (n + N) x) ∧
                          (∀ (n : ℕ), Set.MapsTo (heightCoordinate (K (n + N))) (Set.Ioo 0 1) (Set.Ioo (-1) 1)) ∧
                            (∀ (n : ℕ) (p : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
                                DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt (D n).metric p / 2 ∈
                                  Set.Icc (κ (n + N)) (Λ (n + N))) ∧
                              (∀ (n : ℕ), (D n).meridionalOneForm (R n) ⋯ ≠ 0) ∧
                                Filter.Tendsto
                                  (fun n ↦
                                    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation (D n).metric
                                      ((D n).meridionalOneForm (R n) ⋯))
                                  Filter.atTop (nhds (2 * Real.pi * A₀))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_smooth_convex_recovery_of_weak_graph_pairings (K r : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ)
  (κ Λ : ℕ → ℝ) (C : ℝ) (K0 r0 p0 z0 : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : ∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (K n))
  (hr : ∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (r n)) (hκ : ∀ (n : ℕ), 0 < κ n)
  (hbox : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, κ n ≤ K n x ∧ K n x ≤ Λ n) (hratio : ∀ (n : ℕ), Λ n / κ n ≤ C)
  (hzero : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K n x = 2) (hone : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K n x = 1)
  (hK0 : MeasureTheory.MemLp K0 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hgraph : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r0 p0 z0)
  (hKW :
    ∀ (v : ↥(MeasureTheory.Lp ℝ 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))),
      Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, K n x * ↑↑v x) Filter.atTop
        (nhds (∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, K0 x * ↑↑v x)))
  (hRW :
    ∀ (v : ↥(MeasureTheory.Lp ℝ 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))),
      Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, r n x * ↑↑v x) Filter.atTop
        (nhds (∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, r0 x * ↑↑v x)))
  (hPW :
    ∀ (v : ↥(MeasureTheory.Lp ℝ 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))),
      Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (r n) x * ↑↑v x) Filter.atTop
        (nhds (∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, √(x * (1 - x)) * p0 x * ↑↑v x)))
  (hZW :
    ∀ (v : ↥(MeasureTheory.Lp ℝ 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))),
      Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (deriv (r n)) x * ↑↑v x)
        Filter.atTop (nhds (∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * z0 x * ↑↑v x))) :
  ∃ s w,
    (∀ (n i : ℕ), 0 ≤ w n i) ∧
      (∀ (n i : ℕ), i ∉ s n → w n i = 0) ∧
        (∀ (n i : ℕ), i ∈ s n → n ≤ i) ∧
          (∀ (n : ℕ), ∑ i ∈ s n, w n i = 1) ∧
            have Km := fun n x ↦ ∑ i ∈ s n, w n i * K i x;
            have rm := fun n x ↦ ∑ i ∈ s n, w n i * r i x;
            have κm := fun n ↦ ∑ i ∈ s n, w n i * κ i;
            have Λm := fun n ↦ ∑ i ∈ s n, w n i * Λ i;
            (∀ (n : ℕ),
                ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (Km n) ∧
                  ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (rm n) ∧
                    0 < κm n ∧
                      (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, κm n ≤ Km n x ∧ Km n x ≤ Λm n) ∧
                        Λm n / κm n ≤ C ∧ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, Km n x = 2 ∧ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * Km n x = 1) ∧
              Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (Km n - K0) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                  Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
                Filter.Tendsto
                    (fun n ↦ MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (rm n - r0) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                    Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
                  Filter.Tendsto
                      (fun n ↦
                        MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (rm n) x - √(x * (1 - x)) * p0 x) 2
                          (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                      Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
                    Filter.Tendsto
                      (fun n ↦
                        MeasureTheory.eLpNorm
                          (fun x ↦
                            x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (deriv (rm n)) x - x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * z0 x)
                          2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                      Filter.atTop (nhds 0)

        (nhds (∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, √(x * (1 - x)) * p0 x * ↑↑v x)))

        Filter.atTop (nhds (∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * z0 x * ↑↑v x)))
