RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_joint_weak_limit_with_smooth_geometric_recovery (K r : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ)
  (κ Λ : ℕ → ℝ) (C B : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) (hK : ∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (K n)) (hr : ∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (r n))
  (hκ : ∀ (n : ℕ), 0 < κ n) (hbox : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, κ n ≤ K n x ∧ K n x ≤ Λ n)
  (hratio : ∀ (n : ℕ), Λ n / κ n ≤ C) (hzero : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K n x = 2)
  (hone : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K n x = 1) (hmass : ∀ (n : ℕ), probeMass (K n) (r n) = 1)
  (hA : ∀ (n : ℕ), meridionalAction (K n) (r n) ≤ B) :
  ∃ K0 r0 p0 z0 κ0 Λ0 φ,
    StrictMono φ ∧
      MeasureTheory.MemLp K0 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
        MeasureTheory.MemLp K0 ⊤ (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
          0 < κ0 ∧
            Λ0 / κ0 ≤ C ∧
              (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), κ0 ≤ K0 x ∧ K0 x ≤ Λ0) ∧
                Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ κ (φ n)) Filter.atTop (nhds κ0) ∧
                  Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ Λ (φ n)) Filter.atTop (nhds Λ0) ∧
                    ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K0 x = 2 ∧
                      ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K0 x = 1 ∧
                        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r0 p0 z0 ∧
                          ContinuousOn r0 (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
                            ContinuousOn p0 (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
                              ContinuousOn (warp K0) (Set.Icc 0 1) ∧
                                ContinuousOn (warpSlope K0) (Set.Icc 0 1) ∧
                                  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasWeakDerivOn (Set.Ioo 0 1) (warp K0)
                                      (warpSlope K0) ∧
                                    (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasWeakDerivOn (Set.Ioo 0 1) (warpSlope K0) fun x ↦
                                        -2 * K0 x) ∧
                                      IntervalIntegrable
                                          (fun x ↦
                                            meridionalActionDensity (warp K0 x) (warpSlope K0 x) (K0 x) (r0 x) (p0 x)
                                              (z0 x))
                                          MeasureTheory.volume 0 1 ∧
                                        MeasureTheory.MemLp
                                            (fun x ↦ √(warp K0 x) * weakMeridionalOperator K0 r0 p0 z0 x) 2
                                            (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
                                          MeasureTheory.MemLp (fun x ↦ √(warp K0 x) * (K0 x * r0 x)) 2
                                              (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
                                            (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1,
                                                2 / C * x * (1 - x) ≤ warp K0 x ∧
                                                  warp K0 x ≤ 2 * C * x * (1 - x) ∧ |warpSlope K0 x| ≤ 2) ∧
                                              (warp K0 0 = 0 ∧
                                                  warp K0 1 = 0 ∧ warpSlope K0 0 = 2 ∧ warpSlope K0 1 = -2) ∧
                                                MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ warp K0 x * r0 x ^ 2)
                                                    (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
                                                  probeMass K0 r0 = 1 ∧
                                                    (∃ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, r0 x ≠ 0) ∧
                                                      Filter.Tendsto
                                                          (fun n ↦
                                                            MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (r (φ n) - r0) 2
                                                              (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                                                          Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
                                                        TendstoLocallyUniformlyOn (fun n ↦ r (φ n)) r0 Filter.atTop
                                                            (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
                                                          TendstoLocallyUniformlyOn (fun n ↦ deriv (r (φ n))) p0
                                                              Filter.atTop (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
                                                            TendstoUniformlyOn (fun n ↦ warp (K (φ n))) (warp K0)
                                                                Filter.atTop (Set.Icc 0 1) ∧
                                                              TendstoUniformlyOn (fun n ↦ warpSlope (K (φ n)))
                                                                  (warpSlope K0) Filter.atTop (Set.Icc 0 1) ∧
                                                                (∀ (v : ℝ → ℝ),
                                                                    MeasureTheory.Integrable v
                                                                        (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) →
                                                                      Filter.Tendsto
                                                                        (fun n ↦
                                                                          ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, K (φ n) x * v x)
                                                                        Filter.atTop
                                                                        (nhds (∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, K0 x * v x))) ∧
                                                                  (∀
                                                                      (v :
                                                                        ↥(MeasureTheory.Lp ℝ 2
                                                                            (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict
                                                                              (Set.Ioo 0 1)))),
                                                                      Filter.Tendsto
                                                                        (fun n ↦
                                                                          ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1,
                                                                            x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) *
                                                                                deriv (deriv (r (φ n))) x *
                                                                              ↑↑v x)
                                                                        Filter.atTop
                                                                        (nhds
                                                                          (∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1,
                                                                            x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * z0 x *
                                                                              ↑↑v x))) ∧
                                                                    (∀
                                                                        (v :
                                                                          ↥(MeasureTheory.Lp ℝ 2
                                                                              (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict
                                                                                (Set.Ioo 0 1)))),
                                                                        Filter.Tendsto
                                                                          (fun n ↦
                                                                            ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1,
                                                                              √(warp (K (φ n)) x) *
                                                                                  meridionalOperator (K (φ n)) (r (φ n))
                                                                                    x *
                                                                                ↑↑v x)
                                                                          Filter.atTop
                                                                          (nhds
                                                                            (∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1,
                                                                              √(warp K0 x) *
                                                                                  weakMeridionalOperator K0 r0 p0 z0 x *
                                                                                ↑↑v x))) ∧
                                                                      (∀
                                                                          (v :
                                                                            ↥(MeasureTheory.Lp ℝ 2
                                                                                (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict
                                                                                  (Set.Ioo 0 1)))),
                                                                          Filter.Tendsto
                                                                            (fun n ↦
                                                                              ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1,
                                                                                √(warp (K (φ n)) x) *
                                                                                    (K (φ n) x * r (φ n) x) *
                                                                                  ↑↑v x)
                                                                            Filter.atTop
                                                                            (nhds
                                                                              (∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1,
                                                                                √(warp K0 x) * (K0 x * r0 x) *
                                                                                  ↑↑v x))) ∧
                                                                        weakMeridionalAction K0 r0 p0 z0 ≤
                                                                            Filter.liminf
                                                                              (fun n ↦ meridionalAction (K n) (r n))
                                                                              Filter.atTop ∧
                                                                          ∃ Ks rs κs Λs,
                                                                            (∀ (n : ℕ),
                                                                                ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (Ks n) ∧
                                                                                  ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (rs n) ∧
                                                                                    0 < κs n ∧ ⋯) ∧
                                                                              Filter.Tendsto
                                                                                  (fun n ↦
                                                                                    MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (Ks n - K0) 2
                                                                                      (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict
                                                                                        (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                                                                                  Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
                                                                                (∀ᵐ (x :
                                                                                    ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict
                                                                                      (Set.Ioo 0 1),
                                                                                    Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ Ks n x)
                                                                                      Filter.atTop (nhds (K0 x))) ∧
                                                                                  Filter.Tendsto ⋯ Filter.atTop
                                                                                      (nhds 0) ∧
                                                                                    ⋯ ∧ ⋯
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.strong_L2_const_mul_of_tendsto.{u_1, u_2} {Ω : Type u_1} {ι : Type u_2}
  [MeasurableSpace Ω] {μ : MeasureTheory.Measure Ω} {l : Filter ι} {u : ι → Ω → ℝ} {u0 : Ω → ℝ} {a : ι → ℝ} {a0 : ℝ}
  (hu : ∀ (n : ι), MeasureTheory.MemLp (u n) 2 μ) (hu0 : MeasureTheory.MemLp u0 2 μ)
  (hstrong : Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (u n - u0) 2 μ) l (nhds 0))
  (ha : Filter.Tendsto a l (nhds a0)) :
  Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (fun x ↦ a n * u n x - a0 * u0 x) 2 μ) l (nhds 0)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.parabolicGraph_tendsto_const_mul {r : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ} {r0 p0 z0 : ℝ → ℝ} {a : ℕ → ℝ} {a0 : ℝ}
  (hr : ∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ 2 (r n)) (hgraph : HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r0 p0 z0)
  (ha : Filter.Tendsto a Filter.atTop (nhds a0))
  (hR :
    Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (r n - r0) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
      Filter.atTop (nhds 0))
  (hP :
    Filter.Tendsto
      (fun n ↦
        MeasureTheory.eLpNorm ((fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (r n) x) - fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * p0 x) 2
          (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
      Filter.atTop (nhds 0))
  (hZ :
    Filter.Tendsto
      (fun n ↦
        MeasureTheory.eLpNorm
          ((fun x ↦ x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (deriv (r n)) x) - fun x ↦ x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * z0 x)
          2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
      Filter.atTop (nhds 0)) :
  Filter.Tendsto
      (fun n ↦
        MeasureTheory.eLpNorm ((fun x ↦ a n * r n x) - fun x ↦ a0 * r0 x) 2
          (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
      Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
    Filter.Tendsto
        (fun n ↦
          MeasureTheory.eLpNorm
            ((fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (fun y ↦ a n * r n y) x) - fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * (a0 * p0 x)) 2
            (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
        Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
      Filter.Tendsto
        (fun n ↦
          MeasureTheory.eLpNorm
            ((fun x ↦ x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (deriv fun y ↦ a n * r n y) x) - fun x ↦
              x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * (a0 * z0 x))
            2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
        Filter.atTop (nhds 0)

  (h : IsSmoothAreaCompetitor C K r) (x : ℝ) : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1 → 2 / C ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ 2 * C

  (h : IsSmoothAreaCompetitor C K r) : MeasureTheory.MemLp K 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1))

  (meridionalAction (fun x ↦ 2) fun x ↦ √3) = 4

  (h : IsSmoothAreaCompetitor C K r) (hC : 1 ≤ C) : -actionMassCorrection (2 / C) (2 * C) ≤ meridionalAction K r

  -actionMassCorrection (2 / C) (2 * C) ≤ smoothAreaInfimum C
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_smooth_area_minimizing_sequence (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) :
  ∃ K r,
    (∀ (n : ℕ), IsSmoothAreaCompetitor C (K n) (r n)) ∧
      (∀ (n : ℕ), meridionalAction (K n) (r n) ≤ 5) ∧
        Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ meridionalAction (K n) (r n)) Filter.atTop (nhds (smoothAreaInfimum C))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor (C : ℝ) (K r p z : ℝ → ℝ) : Prop
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsSmoothAreaCompetitor.recoverable {C : ℝ} {K r : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : IsSmoothAreaCompetitor C K r) : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r (deriv r) (deriv (deriv r))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.exists_action_recovery {C : ℝ} {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ}
  (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) :
  MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn (fun x ↦ meridionalActionDensity (warp K x) (warpSlope K x) (K x) (r x) (p x) (z x))
      (Set.Ioo 0 1) MeasureTheory.volume ∧
    MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn (fun x ↦ warp K x * r x ^ 2) (Set.Ioo 0 1) MeasureTheory.volume ∧
      ∃ Ks rs,
        (∀ (n : ℕ), IsSmoothAreaCompetitor C (Ks n) (rs n)) ∧
          Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (Ks n - K) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
              Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
            Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (rs n - r) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
              Filter.Tendsto
                  (fun n ↦
                    MeasureTheory.eLpNorm ((fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (rs n) x) - fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * p x) 2
                      (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                  Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
                Filter.Tendsto
                    (fun n ↦
                      MeasureTheory.eLpNorm
                        ((fun x ↦ x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (deriv (rs n)) x) - fun x ↦
                          x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * z x)
                        2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                    Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
                  Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ meridionalAction (Ks n) (rs n)) Filter.atTop
                    (nhds (weakMeridionalAction K r p z))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.infimum_le_action {C : ℝ} {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ}
  (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) : smoothAreaInfimum C ≤ weakMeridionalAction K r p z
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_recoverable_area_minimizer (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) :
  ∃ K r p z,
    IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z ∧
      ContinuousOn r (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
        ContinuousOn p (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
          (∃ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, r x ≠ 0) ∧
            weakMeridionalAction K r p z = smoothAreaInfimum C ∧
              ∀ (K' r' p' z' : ℝ → ℝ),
                IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K' r' p' z' →
                  weakMeridionalAction K r p z ≤ weakMeridionalAction K' r' p' z'
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_area_minimizer_with_geometric_recovery (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) :
  ∃ K0 r0 p0 z0,
    IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K0 r0 p0 z0 ∧
      ContinuousOn r0 (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
        ContinuousOn p0 (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
          (∃ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, r0 x ≠ 0) ∧
            weakMeridionalAction K0 r0 p0 z0 = smoothAreaInfimum C ∧
              (∀ (K r p z : ℝ → ℝ),
                  IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z →
                    weakMeridionalAction K0 r0 p0 z0 ≤ weakMeridionalAction K r p z) ∧
                ∃ Ks rs κs Λs,
                  (∀ (n : ℕ), IsSmoothAreaCompetitor C (Ks n) (rs n)) ∧
                    Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ meridionalAction (Ks n) (rs n)) Filter.atTop (nhds (smoothAreaInfimum C)) ∧
                      ∃ N α X D R,
                        ∃ (hR : ∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (R n)),
                          (∀ (n : ℕ), α n = 1 / √(probeMass (Ks (n + N)) (rs (n + N)))) ∧
                            (∀ (n : ℕ), 0 < α n) ∧
                              Filter.Tendsto α Filter.atTop (nhds 1) ∧
                                Filter.Tendsto
                                    (fun n ↦
                                      MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (Ks (n + N) - K0) 2
                                        (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                                    Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
                                  Filter.Tendsto
                                      (fun n ↦
                                        MeasureTheory.eLpNorm ((fun x ↦ α n * rs (n + N) x) - r0) 2
                                          (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                                      Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
                                    Filter.Tendsto
                                        (fun n ↦
                                          MeasureTheory.eLpNorm
                                            ((fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (fun y ↦ α n * rs (n + N) y) x) - fun x ↦
                                              √(x * (1 - x)) * p0 x)
                                            2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                                        Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
                                      Filter.Tendsto
                                          (fun n ↦
                                            MeasureTheory.eLpNorm
                                              ((fun x ↦
                                                  x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) *
                                                    deriv (deriv fun y ↦ α n * rs (n + N) y) x) -
                                                fun x ↦ x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * z0 x)
                                              2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                                          Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
                                        (∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ ↑⊤ fun x ↦ α n * rs (n + N) x) ∧
                                          (∀ (n : ℕ), (probeMass (Ks (n + N)) fun x ↦ α n * rs (n + N) x) = 1) ∧
                                            (∀ (n : ℕ), ∃ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, α n * rs (n + N) x ≠ 0) ∧
                                              (∀ (n : ℕ),
                                                  (meridionalAction (Ks (n + N)) fun x ↦ α n * rs (n + N) x) =
                                                    α n ^ 2 * meridionalAction (Ks (n + N)) (rs (n + N))) ∧
                                                Filter.Tendsto
                                                    (fun n ↦ meridionalAction (Ks (n + N)) fun x ↦ α n * rs (n + N) x)
                                                    Filter.atTop (nhds (smoothAreaInfimum C)) ∧
                                                  (∀ (n : ℕ),
                                                      0 < κs (n + N) ∧
                                                        Λs (n + N) / κs (n + N) ≤ C ∧
                                                          (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1,
                                                              Ks (n + N) x ∈ Set.Icc (κs (n + N)) (Λs (n + N))) ∧
                                                            ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, Ks (n + N) x = 2 ∧
                                                              ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * Ks (n + N) x = 1) ∧
                                                    (∀ (n : ℕ),
                                                        ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (X n) ∧
                                                          heightCoordinate (Ks (n + N)) 0 = -1 ∧
                                                            heightCoordinate (Ks (n + N)) 1 = 1 ∧
                                                              X n (-1) = 0 ∧
                                                                X n 1 = 1 ∧
                                                                  Set.MapsTo (heightCoordinate (Ks (n + N)))
                                                                      (Set.Icc 0 1) (Set.Icc (-1) 1) ∧
                                                                    Set.MapsTo (X n) (Set.Icc (-1) 1) (Set.Icc 0 1) ∧
                                                                      (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1,
                                                                          X n (heightCoordinate (Ks (n + N)) x) = x ∧
                                                                            (D n).a (heightCoordinate (Ks (n + N)) x) =
                                                                                1 / Ks (n + N) x ∧
                                                                              R n (heightCoordinate (Ks (n + N)) x) =
                                                                                  α n * rs (n + N) x ∧
                                                                                RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp
                                                                                    (D n).a
                                                                                    (heightCoordinate (Ks (n + N)) x) =
                                                                                  warp (Ks (n + N)) x) ∧
                                                                        (∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1,
                                                                            heightCoordinate (Ks (n + N)) (X n v) = v ∧
                                                                              ⋯ * Ks (n + N) (X n v) = 1) ∧
                                                                          R n = (fun x ↦ α n * rs (n + N) x) ∘ X n ∧
                                                                            (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, ⋯ ∧ ⋯) ∧
                                                                              (∀
                                                                                  (p :
                                                                                    ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
                                                                                  ⋯ =
                                                                                    Ks (n + N)
                                                                                      (X n
                                                                                        (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight
                                                                                          p))) ∧
                                                                                RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation
                                                                                    (D n).metric
                                                                                    ((D n).meridionalOneForm (R n) ⋯) =
                                                                                  2 * Real.pi *
                                                                                    meridionalAction (Ks (n + N))
                                                                                      fun x ↦ α n * rs (n + N) x) ∧
                                                      (∀ (n : ℕ),
                                                          Set.MapsTo (heightCoordinate (Ks (n + N))) (Set.Ioo 0 1)
                                                            (Set.Ioo (-1) 1)) ∧
                                                        (∀ (n : ℕ)
                                                            (p : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
                                                            DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt
                                                                  (D n).metric p /
                                                                2 ∈
                                                              Set.Icc (κs (n + N)) (Λs (n + N))) ∧
                                                          (∀ (n : ℕ), (D n).meridionalOneForm (R n) ⋯ ≠ 0) ∧
                                                            Filter.Tendsto
                                                              (fun n ↦
                                                                RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation
                                                                  (D n).metric ((D n).meridionalOneForm (R n) ⋯))
                                                              Filter.atTop (nhds (2 * Real.pi * smoothAreaInfimum C))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.AreaNormalization.isSmoothAreaCompetitor_normalizedProbe

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.AreaNormalization.smoothAreaInfimum_le_length_sq_mul_oneFormDissipation_div_normSq
  {D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData} (N : D.AreaNormalization) (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C)
  (κ Λ : ℝ) (hκ : 0 < κ)
  (hbox :
    ∀ (p : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
      κ ≤ DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt D.metric p / 2 ∧
        DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt D.metric p / 2 ≤ Λ)
  (hratio : Λ / κ ≤ C) (R : ℝ → ℝ) (hR : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) R) (hne : D.meridionalOneForm R hR ≠ 0) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.smoothAreaInfimum C ≤
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.length D.a ^ 2 *
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm R hR) /
        ∫ (p : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
          DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S D.metric p 1
            ((D.meridionalOneForm R hR)
              p) ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
            (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
            D.metric)

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.parabolicGraph_tendsto_add_fixed_smooth {rn : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ} {r p z : ℝ → ℝ}
  (hRnC2 : ∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ 2 (rn n))
  (hStrong3 :
    Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (rn n - r) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
        Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
      Filter.Tendsto
          (fun n ↦
            MeasureTheory.eLpNorm ((fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (rn n) x) - fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * p x) 2
              (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
          Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
        Filter.Tendsto
          (fun n ↦
            MeasureTheory.eLpNorm
              ((fun x ↦ x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (deriv (rn n)) x) - fun x ↦
                x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * z x)
              2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
          Filter.atTop (nhds 0))
  (s : ℝ → ℝ) (hs : ContDiff ℝ 2 s) (t : ℝ) :
  Filter.Tendsto
      (fun n ↦
        MeasureTheory.eLpNorm ((fun x ↦ rn n x + t * s x) - fun x ↦ r x + t * s x) 2
          (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
      Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
    Filter.Tendsto
        (fun n ↦
          MeasureTheory.eLpNorm
            ((fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (fun y ↦ rn n y + t * s y) x) - fun x ↦
              √(x * (1 - x)) * (p x + t * deriv s x))
            2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
        Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
      Filter.Tendsto
        (fun n ↦
          MeasureTheory.eLpNorm
            ((fun x ↦ x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (deriv fun y ↦ rn n y + t * s y) x) - fun x ↦
              x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * (z x + t * deriv (deriv s) x))
            2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
        Filter.atTop (nhds 0)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.strong_curvature_subsequence {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) :
  ∃ Ks rs,
    (∀ (n : ℕ), IsSmoothAreaCompetitor C (Ks n) (rs n)) ∧
      Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (Ks n - K) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
          Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
        Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (rs n - r) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
            Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
          Filter.Tendsto
              (fun n ↦
                MeasureTheory.eLpNorm ((fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (rs n) x) - fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * p x) 2
                  (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
              Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
            Filter.Tendsto
                (fun n ↦
                  MeasureTheory.eLpNorm
                    ((fun x ↦ x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (deriv (rs n)) x) - fun x ↦
                      x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * z x)
                    2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
              ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1),
                Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ Ks n x) Filter.atTop (nhds (K x))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.geometry_bounds {C : ℝ} {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ}
  (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) :
  ContinuousOn (warp K) (Set.Icc 0 1) ∧
    ContinuousOn (warpSlope K) (Set.Icc 0 1) ∧
      (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 2 / C * x * (1 - x) ≤ warp K x ∧ warp K x ≤ 2 * C * x * (1 - x) ∧ |warpSlope K x| ≤ 2) ∧
        (warp K 0 = 0 ∧ warp K 1 = 0 ∧ warpSlope K 0 = 2 ∧ warpSlope K 1 = -2) ∧
          ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), 2 / C ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ 2 * C
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakMeridionalPairingDensity (K r p z s q u : ℝ → ℝ) (x : ℝ) : ℝ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakMeridionalPairing (K r p z s q u : ℝ → ℝ) : ℝ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakMeridionalPairing_symm (K r p z s q u : ℝ → ℝ) :
  weakMeridionalPairing K r p z s q u = weakMeridionalPairing K s q u r p z
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakMeridionalPairing_self (K r p z : ℝ → ℝ) :
  weakMeridionalPairing K r p z r p z = weakMeridionalAction K r p z
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakMeridionalAction_const_mul (K r p z : ℝ → ℝ) (c : ℝ) :
  (weakMeridionalAction K (fun x ↦ c * r x) (fun x ↦ c * p x) fun x ↦ c * z x) = c ^ 2 * weakMeridionalAction K r p z
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.integrable_weakMeridionalPairingDensity_of_warp_bounds
  (K r p z s q u : ℝ → ℝ) (hi : ℝ) (hgraphR : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r p z)
  (hgraphS : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives s q u) (hhi : 0 ≤ hi)
  (hK : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable K (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hf : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (warp K) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hb : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (warpSlope K) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hKbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), 0 ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi)
  (hfbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), 0 ≤ warp K x ∧ warp K x ≤ hi * (x * (1 - x)))
  (hbbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), |warpSlope K x| ≤ 2) :
  MeasureTheory.Integrable (weakMeridionalPairingDensity K r p z s q u) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakMeridionalPairing_intervalIntegrable_of_warp_bounds
  (K r p z s q u : ℝ → ℝ) (hi : ℝ) (hgraphR : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r p z)
  (hgraphS : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives s q u) (hhi : 0 ≤ hi)
  (hK : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable K (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hf : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (warp K) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hb : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (warpSlope K) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hKbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), 0 ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi)
  (hfbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), 0 ≤ warp K x ∧ warp K x ≤ hi * (x * (1 - x)))
  (hbbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), |warpSlope K x| ≤ 2) :
  IntervalIntegrable (weakMeridionalPairingDensity K r p z s q u) MeasureTheory.volume 0 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakMeridionalAction_add_mul (K r p z s q u : ℝ → ℝ) (hi : ℝ)
  (hgraphR : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r p z)
  (hgraphS : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives s q u) (hhi : 0 ≤ hi)
  (hK : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable K (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hf : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (warp K) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hb : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (warpSlope K) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hKbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), 0 ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi)
  (hfbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), 0 ≤ warp K x ∧ warp K x ≤ hi * (x * (1 - x)))
  (hbbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), |warpSlope K x| ≤ 2) (t : ℝ) :
  (weakMeridionalAction K (fun x ↦ r x + t * s x) (fun x ↦ p x + t * q x) fun x ↦ z x + t * u x) =
    weakMeridionalAction K r p z + 2 * t * weakMeridionalPairing K r p z s q u + t ^ 2 * weakMeridionalAction K s q u
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakMeridionalAction_hasDerivAt (K r p z s q u : ℝ → ℝ) (hi : ℝ)
  (hgraphR : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r p z)
  (hgraphS : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives s q u) (hhi : 0 ≤ hi)
  (hK : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable K (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hf : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (warp K) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hb : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (warpSlope K) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hKbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), 0 ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi)
  (hfbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), 0 ≤ warp K x ∧ warp K x ≤ hi * (x * (1 - x)))
  (hbbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), |warpSlope K x| ≤ 2) (t : ℝ) :
  HasDerivAt (fun v ↦ weakMeridionalAction K (fun x ↦ r x + v * s x) (fun x ↦ p x + v * q x) fun x ↦ z x + v * u x)
    (2 * weakMeridionalPairing K r p z s q u + 2 * t * weakMeridionalAction K s q u) t
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.integrable_probeMassDensity_of_warp_bounds (K r : ℝ → ℝ) (hi : ℝ)

  MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ warp K x * r x ^ 2) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1))

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.integrable_probeMassPairing_of_warp_bounds (K r s : ℝ → ℝ) (hi : ℝ)
  (hR : MeasureTheory.MemLp r 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hS : MeasureTheory.MemLp s 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1))) (hhi : 0 ≤ hi)
  (hf : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (warp K) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
