RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.probeMassPairing_intervalIntegrable_of_warp_bounds (K r s : ℝ → ℝ) (hi : ℝ)
  (hR : MeasureTheory.MemLp r 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hS : MeasureTheory.MemLp s 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1))) (hhi : 0 ≤ hi)
  (hf : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (warp K) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hfbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), 0 ≤ warp K x ∧ warp K x ≤ hi * (x * (1 - x))) :
  IntervalIntegrable (fun x ↦ warp K x * s x * r x) MeasureTheory.volume 0 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.probeMass_add_mul_of_warp_bounds (K r s : ℝ → ℝ) (hi : ℝ)
  (hR : MeasureTheory.MemLp r 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hS : MeasureTheory.MemLp s 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1))) (hhi : 0 ≤ hi)
  (hf : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (warp K) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hfbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), 0 ≤ warp K x ∧ warp K x ≤ hi * (x * (1 - x)))
  (t : ℝ) :
  (probeMass K fun x ↦ r x + t * s x) =
    (probeMass K r + 2 * t * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, warp K x * s x * r x) + t ^ 2 * probeMass K s
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.probeMass_hasDerivAt_of_warp_bounds (K r s : ℝ → ℝ) (hi : ℝ)
  (hR : MeasureTheory.MemLp r 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hS : MeasureTheory.MemLp s 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1))) (hhi : 0 ≤ hi)
  (hf : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (warp K) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hfbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), 0 ≤ warp K x ∧ warp K x ≤ hi * (x * (1 - x)))
  (t : ℝ) :
  HasDerivAt (fun v ↦ probeMass K fun x ↦ r x + v * s x)
    ((2 * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, warp K x * s x * r x) + 2 * t * probeMass K s) t
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.normalized_probe_add_mul {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) (s : ℝ → ℝ) (hs : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s)
  (t : ℝ) (hm : 0 < probeMass K fun x ↦ r x + t * s x) :
  IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K (normalizedProbe K fun x ↦ r x + t * s x)
    (fun x ↦ 1 / √(probeMass K fun y ↦ r y + t * s y) * (p x + t * deriv s x)) fun x ↦
    1 / √(probeMass K fun y ↦ r y + t * s y) * (z x + t * deriv (deriv s) x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.weak_probe_equation_of_minimum {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z)
  (hmin :
    ∀ (K' r' p' z' : ℝ → ℝ),
      IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K' r' p' z' → weakMeridionalAction K r p z ≤ weakMeridionalAction K' r' p' z')
  (s : ℝ → ℝ) (hs : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s) :
  weakMeridionalPairing K r p z s (deriv s) (deriv (deriv s)) =
    weakMeridionalAction K r p z * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, warp K x * s x * r x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_recoverable_area_minimizer_weak_probe_equation (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) :
  ∃ K r p z,
    IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z ∧
      ContinuousOn r (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
        ContinuousOn p (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
          (∃ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, r x ≠ 0) ∧
            weakMeridionalAction K r p z = smoothAreaInfimum C ∧
              (∀ (K' r' p' z' : ℝ → ℝ),
                  IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K' r' p' z' →
                    weakMeridionalAction K r p z ≤ weakMeridionalAction K' r' p' z') ∧
                ∀ (s : ℝ → ℝ),
                  ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s →
                    weakMeridionalPairing K r p z s (deriv s) (deriv (deriv s)) =
                      smoothAreaInfimum C * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, warp K x * s x * r x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.warp_affine_of_integrableOn (K J : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hK : MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn K (Set.Ioo 0 1) MeasureTheory.volume)
  (hJ : MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn J (Set.Ioo 0 1) MeasureTheory.volume) (t x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  warp (fun y ↦ (1 - t) * K y + t * J y) x = (1 - t) * warp K x + t * warp J x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.warpSlope_affine_of_integrableOn (K J : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hK : MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn K (Set.Ioo 0 1) MeasureTheory.volume)
  (hJ : MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn J (Set.Ioo 0 1) MeasureTheory.volume) (t x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  warpSlope (fun y ↦ (1 - t) * K y + t * J y) x = (1 - t) * warpSlope K x + t * warpSlope J x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.curvature_moments_affine_of_integrableOn (K J : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hK : MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn K (Set.Ioo 0 1) MeasureTheory.volume)
  (hJ : MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn J (Set.Ioo 0 1) MeasureTheory.volume) (hK0 : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2)
  (hK1 : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1) (hJ0 : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, J x = 2) (hJ1 : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * J x = 1)
  (t : ℝ) : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, (1 - t) * K x + t * J x = 2 ∧ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * ((1 - t) * K x + t * J x) = 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.probeMass_affine_curvature_of_integrableOn (K J r : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hK : MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn K (Set.Ioo 0 1) MeasureTheory.volume)
  (hJ : MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn J (Set.Ioo 0 1) MeasureTheory.volume)
  (hMK : IntervalIntegrable (fun x ↦ warp K x * r x ^ 2) MeasureTheory.volume 0 1)
  (hMJ : IntervalIntegrable (fun x ↦ warp J x * r x ^ 2) MeasureTheory.volume 0 1) (t : ℝ) :
  probeMass (fun y ↦ (1 - t) * K y + t * J y) r = (1 - t) * probeMass K r + t * probeMass J r
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.probeMass_curvature_difference_intervalIntegrable (K J r : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hMK : IntervalIntegrable (fun x ↦ warp K x * r x ^ 2) MeasureTheory.volume 0 1)
  (hMJ : IntervalIntegrable (fun x ↦ warp J x * r x ^ 2) MeasureTheory.volume 0 1) :
  IntervalIntegrable (fun x ↦ (warp J x - warp K x) * r x ^ 2) MeasureTheory.volume 0 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.probeMass_curvature_segment_hasDerivAt (K J r : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hK : MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn K (Set.Ioo 0 1) MeasureTheory.volume)
  (hJ : MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn J (Set.Ioo 0 1) MeasureTheory.volume)
  (hMK : IntervalIntegrable (fun x ↦ warp K x * r x ^ 2) MeasureTheory.volume 0 1)
  (hMJ : IntervalIntegrable (fun x ↦ warp J x * r x ^ 2) MeasureTheory.volume 0 1) (t : ℝ) :
  HasDerivAt (fun s ↦ probeMass (fun y ↦ (1 - s) * K y + s * J y) r)
    (∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, (warp J x - warp K x) * r x ^ 2) t
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsSmoothAreaCurvature (C : ℝ) (K : ℝ → ℝ) : Prop
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsSmoothAreaCompetitor.curvature_admissible {C : ℝ} {K r : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : IsSmoothAreaCompetitor C K r) : IsSmoothAreaCurvature C K
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsSmoothAreaCurvature.with_unit_probe {C : ℝ} {K : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : IsSmoothAreaCurvature C K) (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) (hmass : probeMass K r = 1) :
  IsSmoothAreaCompetitor C K r
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsSmoothAreaCurvature.common_bounds {C : ℝ} {K : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : IsSmoothAreaCurvature C K) (x : ℝ) : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1 → 2 / C ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ 2 * C
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsSmoothAreaCurvature.memLp_curvature {C : ℝ} {K : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : IsSmoothAreaCurvature C K) : MeasureTheory.MemLp K 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsSmoothAreaCurvature.geometry_bounds {C : ℝ} {K : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : IsSmoothAreaCurvature C K) (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  2 / C * x * (1 - x) ≤ warp K x ∧ warp K x ≤ 2 * C * x * (1 - x) ∧ |warpSlope K x| ≤ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsSmoothAreaCurvature.convex_combination {C : ℝ} {K J : ℝ → ℝ}
  (hK : IsSmoothAreaCurvature C K) (hJ : IsSmoothAreaCurvature C J) (t : ℝ) (ht : t ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  IsSmoothAreaCurvature C fun x ↦ (1 - t) * K x + t * J x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.isSmoothAreaCurvature_round (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) :
  IsSmoothAreaCurvature C fun x ↦ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakCurvatureSegmentDensity (K J r p z : ℝ → ℝ) (x : ℝ) : ℝ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakCurvatureSegmentVariation (K J r p z : ℝ → ℝ) : ℝ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakCurvatureSegmentDensity_integrable {C : ℝ} {K J r p z : ℝ → ℝ}
  (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) (hJ : IsSmoothAreaCurvature C J) :
  MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn (weakCurvatureSegmentDensity K J r p z) (Set.Ioo 0 1) MeasureTheory.volume
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakCurvatureSegmentDensity_intervalIntegrable {C : ℝ} {K J r p z : ℝ → ℝ}
  (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) (hJ : IsSmoothAreaCurvature C J) :
  IntervalIntegrable (weakCurvatureSegmentDensity K J r p z) MeasureTheory.volume 0 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakMeridionalAction_curvature_segment_hasDerivAt {C : ℝ} {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ}
  (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) (J : ℝ → ℝ) (hJ : IsSmoothAreaCurvature C J) :
  HasDerivAt (fun t ↦ weakMeridionalAction (fun x ↦ (1 - t) * K x + t * J x) r p z)
    (weakCurvatureSegmentVariation K J r p z) 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.probeMass_curvature_segment_pos {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) (J : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hJ : IsSmoothAreaCurvature C J) (t : ℝ) (ht : t ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) : 0 < probeMass (fun x ↦ (1 - t) * K x + t * J x) r
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.normalized_curvature_segment {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) (J : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hJ : IsSmoothAreaCurvature C J) (t : ℝ) (ht : t ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C (fun x ↦ (1 - t) * K x + t * J x) (normalizedProbe (fun x ↦ (1 - t) * K x + t * J x) r)
    (fun x ↦ 1 / √(probeMass (fun y ↦ (1 - t) * K y + t * J y) r) * p x) fun x ↦
    1 / √(probeMass (fun y ↦ (1 - t) * K y + t * J y) r) * z x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.curvature_segment_inequality_of_minimum {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z)
  (hmin :
    ∀ (K' r' p' z' : ℝ → ℝ),
      IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K' r' p' z' → weakMeridionalAction K r p z ≤ weakMeridionalAction K' r' p' z')
  (J : ℝ → ℝ) (hJ : IsSmoothAreaCurvature C J) :
  0 ≤
    weakCurvatureSegmentVariation K J r p z -
      weakMeridionalAction K r p z * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, (warp J x - warp K x) * r x ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_recoverable_area_minimizer_weak_conditions (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) :
  ∃ K r p z,
    IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z ∧
      ContinuousOn r (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
        ContinuousOn p (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
          (∃ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, r x ≠ 0) ∧
            weakMeridionalAction K r p z = smoothAreaInfimum C ∧
              (∀ (K' r' p' z' : ℝ → ℝ),
                  IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K' r' p' z' →
                    weakMeridionalAction K r p z ≤ weakMeridionalAction K' r' p' z') ∧
                (∀ (s : ℝ → ℝ),
                    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s →
                      weakMeridionalPairing K r p z s (deriv s) (deriv (deriv s)) =
                        smoothAreaInfimum C * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, warp K x * s x * r x) ∧
                  ∀ (J : ℝ → ℝ),
                    IsSmoothAreaCurvature C J →
                      0 ≤
                        weakCurvatureSegmentVariation K J r p z -
                          smoothAreaInfimum C * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, (warp J x - warp K x) * r x ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.unitDirichletKernel (x s : ℝ) : ℝ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.unitDirichletSlopeKernel (x s : ℝ) : ℝ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.continuous_unitDirichletKernel : Continuous fun v ↦ unitDirichletKernel v.1 v.2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.measurable_unitDirichletKernel : Measurable fun v ↦ unitDirichletKernel v.1 v.2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.measurable_unitDirichletSlopeKernel :
  Measurable fun v ↦ unitDirichletSlopeKernel v.1 v.2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.abs_unitDirichletKernel_le (x s : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) (hs : s ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  |unitDirichletKernel x s| ≤ x * (1 - x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.abs_unitDirichletSlopeKernel_le_one (x s : ℝ) (hs : s ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  |unitDirichletSlopeKernel x s| ≤ 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.integral_unitDirichletKernel (u : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hu : MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn u (Set.Ioo 0 1) MeasureTheory.volume) (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, unitDirichletKernel x s * u s =
    (x * ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, (1 - s) * u s) - ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..x, (x - s) * u s
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.integral_unitDirichletSlopeKernel (u : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hu : MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn u (Set.Ioo 0 1) MeasureTheory.volume) (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, unitDirichletSlopeKernel x s * u s = (∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, (1 - s) * u s) - ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..x, u s
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.integrable_kernel_adjoint_of_weighted_integrable.{u_1, u_2} {α : Type u_1}
  {β : Type u_2} [MeasurableSpace α] [MeasurableSpace β] {μ : MeasureTheory.Measure α} {ν : MeasureTheory.Measure β}
  {g : α → β → ℝ} {w a : α → ℝ} [MeasureTheory.SFinite μ] [MeasureTheory.IsFiniteMeasure ν]
  (hg : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (Function.uncurry g) (μ.prod ν))
  (ha : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable a μ) (hwa : MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ w x * a x) μ)
  (hbound : ∀ᵐ (v : α × β) ∂μ.prod ν, |g v.1 v.2| ≤ |w v.1|) :
  MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun y ↦ ∫ (x : α), g x y * a x ∂μ) ν
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.integral_kernel_pairing_of_weighted_integrable.{u_1, u_2} {α : Type u_1} {β : Type u_2}
  [MeasurableSpace α] [MeasurableSpace β] {μ : MeasureTheory.Measure α} {ν : MeasureTheory.Measure β} {g : α → β → ℝ}
  {w a : α → ℝ} [MeasureTheory.SFinite μ] [MeasureTheory.IsFiniteMeasure ν] {ξ : β → ℝ} {D : ℝ}
  (hg : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (Function.uncurry g) (μ.prod ν))
  (ha : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable a μ) (hwa : MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ w x * a x) μ)
  (hbound : ∀ᵐ (v : α × β) ∂μ.prod ν, |g v.1 v.2| ≤ |w v.1|) (hξ : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable ξ ν)
  (hD : ∀ᵐ (y : β) ∂ν, |ξ y| ≤ D) :
  MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ a x * ∫ (y : β), g x y * ξ y ∂ν) μ ∧
    MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun y ↦ (∫ (x : α), g x y * a x ∂μ) * ξ y) ν ∧
      ∫ (x : α), a x * ∫ (y : β), g x y * ξ y ∂ν ∂μ = ∫ (y : β), (∫ (x : α), g x y * a x ∂μ) * ξ y ∂ν
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakCurvatureWarpCoefficient (K r p z : ℝ → ℝ) (x : ℝ) : ℝ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakCurvatureSlopeCoefficient (K r p z : ℝ → ℝ) (x : ℝ) : ℝ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakCurvatureCoefficient (K r p z : ℝ → ℝ) (x : ℝ) : ℝ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakCurvatureWarpCoefficient_deriv (K r : ℝ → ℝ) :
  weakCurvatureWarpCoefficient K r (deriv r) (deriv (deriv r)) = curvatureWarpCoefficient K r
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakCurvatureSlopeCoefficient_deriv (K r : ℝ → ℝ) :
  weakCurvatureSlopeCoefficient K r (deriv r) (deriv (deriv r)) = curvatureSlopeCoefficient K r
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakCurvatureCoefficient_deriv (K r : ℝ → ℝ) :
  weakCurvatureCoefficient K r (deriv r) (deriv (deriv r)) = curvatureCoefficient K r
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.curvature_coefficients_integrable {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) :
  MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (weakCurvatureWarpCoefficient K r p z)
      (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
    MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ x * (1 - x) * weakCurvatureWarpCoefficient K r p z x)
        (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
      MeasureTheory.Integrable (weakCurvatureSlopeCoefficient K r p z) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
        MeasureTheory.Integrable (weakCurvatureCoefficient K r p z) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.warp_sub_eq_integral_unitDirichletKernel (K L : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hK : MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn K (Set.Ioo 0 1) MeasureTheory.volume)
  (hL : MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn L (Set.Ioo 0 1) MeasureTheory.volume)
  (hzero : ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, K s = ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, L s)
  (hfirst : ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, s * K s = ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, s * L s) (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  warp K x - warp L x = 2 * ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.unitDirichletKernel x s * (K s - L s)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.warpSlope_sub_eq_integral_unitDirichletSlopeKernel (K L : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hK : MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn K (Set.Ioo 0 1) MeasureTheory.volume)
  (hL : MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn L (Set.Ioo 0 1) MeasureTheory.volume)
  (hzero : ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, K s = ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, L s)
  (hfirst : ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, s * K s = ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, s * L s) (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  warpSlope K x - warpSlope L x =
    2 * ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.unitDirichletSlopeKernel x s * (K s - L s)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakCurvatureAdjoint (K r p z : ℝ → ℝ) (lam s : ℝ) : ℝ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.weakCurvatureAdjoint_integrable {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) (lam : ℝ) :
  MeasureTheory.Integrable (weakCurvatureAdjoint K r p z lam) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.curvature_variation_eq_integral_adjoint {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) (J : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hJ : IsSmoothAreaCurvature C J) (lam : ℝ) :
  weakCurvatureSegmentVariation K J r p z - lam * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, (warp J x - warp K x) * r x ^ 2 =
    ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, weakCurvatureAdjoint K r p z lam s * (J s - K s)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.curvature_adjoint_inequality_of_minimum {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z)
  (hmin :
    ∀ (K' r' p' z' : ℝ → ℝ),
      IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K' r' p' z' → weakMeridionalAction K r p z ≤ weakMeridionalAction K' r' p' z')
  (J : ℝ → ℝ) (hJ : IsSmoothAreaCurvature C J) :
  0 ≤ ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, weakCurvatureAdjoint K r p z (weakMeridionalAction K r p z) s * (J s - K s)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_recoverable_area_minimizer_with_curvature_adjoint (C : ℝ)
  (hC : 1 ≤ C) :
  ∃ K r p z,
    IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z ∧
      ContinuousOn r (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
        ContinuousOn p (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
          (∃ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, r x ≠ 0) ∧
            weakMeridionalAction K r p z = smoothAreaInfimum C ∧
              (∀ (K' r' p' z' : ℝ → ℝ),
                  IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K' r' p' z' →
                    weakMeridionalAction K r p z ≤ weakMeridionalAction K' r' p' z') ∧
                (∀ (s : ℝ → ℝ),
                    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s →
                      weakMeridionalPairing K r p z s (deriv s) (deriv (deriv s)) =
                        smoothAreaInfimum C * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, warp K x * s x * r x) ∧
                  MeasureTheory.Integrable (weakCurvatureAdjoint K r p z (smoothAreaInfimum C))
                      (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
                    ∀ (J : ℝ → ℝ),
                      IsSmoothAreaCurvature C J →
                        0 ≤ ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, weakCurvatureAdjoint K r p z (smoothAreaInfimum C) s * (J s - K s)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.exists_contDiff_approx_Icc_of_memLp (f : ℝ → ℝ) (a b ε : ℝ) (hab : a ≤ b)
  (hf : MeasureTheory.MemLp f 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), f x ∈ Set.Icc a b) (hε : 0 < ε) :
  ∃ g,
    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) g ∧
      (∀ (x : ℝ), g x ∈ Set.Icc (a - ε) (b + ε)) ∧
        MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (f - g) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal ε
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.exists_contDiff_interior_moment_basis :
  ∃ ψ0 ψ1 B,
    0 < B ∧
      ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) ψ0 ∧
        ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) ψ1 ∧
          HasCompactSupport ψ0 ∧
            HasCompactSupport ψ1 ∧
              tsupport ψ0 ⊆ Set.Ioo 0 1 ∧
                tsupport ψ1 ⊆ Set.Ioo 0 1 ∧
                  (∀ (x : ℝ), |ψ0 x| ≤ B ∧ |ψ1 x| ≤ B) ∧
                    ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, ψ0 x = 1 ∧
                      ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * ψ0 x = 0 ∧ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, ψ1 x = 0 ∧ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * ψ1 x = 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.exists_contDiff_exact_moments_of_memLp_of_interior_box (f : ℝ → ℝ) (lo hi δ ε : ℝ)
  (hf : MeasureTheory.MemLp f 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1))) (hδ : 0 < δ)
  (hbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), f x ∈ Set.Icc (lo + δ) (hi - δ)) (hε : 0 < ε) :
  ∃ g,
    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) g ∧
      (∀ (x : ℝ), g x ∈ Set.Icc lo hi) ∧
        ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, g x = ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, f x ∧
          ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * g x = ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * f x ∧
            MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (f - g) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal ε
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.exists_contDiff_Icc_exact_balanced_moments (f : ℝ → ℝ) (lo hi c ε : ℝ)
  (hf : MeasureTheory.MemLp f 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), f x ∈ Set.Icc lo hi)
  (hm0 : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, f x = c) (hm1 : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * f x = c / 2) (hε : 0 < ε) :
  ∃ g,
    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) g ∧
      (∀ (x : ℝ), g x ∈ Set.Icc lo hi) ∧
        ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, g x = c ∧
          ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * g x = c / 2 ∧
            MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (f - g) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ≤ ENNReal.ofReal ε
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.exists_contDiff_Icc_exact_balanced_moments_sequence (f : ℝ → ℝ) (lo hi c : ℝ)
  (hf : MeasureTheory.MemLp f 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), f x ∈ Set.Icc lo hi)
  (hm0 : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, f x = c) (hm1 : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * f x = c / 2) :
  ∃ g,
    (∀ (n : ℕ),
        ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (g n) ∧
          (∀ (x : ℝ), g n x ∈ Set.Icc lo hi) ∧ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, g n x = c ∧ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * g n x = c / 2) ∧
      Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (g n - f) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
        Filter.atTop (nhds 0)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsBoundedAreaCurvature (C : ℝ) (K : ℝ → ℝ) : Prop
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsBoundedAreaCurvature.memLp_curvature {C : ℝ} {K : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : IsBoundedAreaCurvature C K) : MeasureTheory.MemLp K 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsSmoothAreaCurvature.bounded {C : ℝ} {K : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : IsSmoothAreaCurvature C K) : IsBoundedAreaCurvature C K
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.curvature_bounded {C : ℝ} {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) : IsBoundedAreaCurvature C K
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsBoundedAreaCurvature.exists_smooth_recovery {C : ℝ} {K : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : IsBoundedAreaCurvature C K) :
  ∃ κ Λ Ks,
    0 < κ ∧
      (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), κ ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ Λ) ∧
        Λ / κ ≤ C ∧
          (∀ (n : ℕ), IsSmoothAreaCurvature C (Ks n)) ∧
            (∀ (n : ℕ) (x : ℝ), κ ≤ Ks n x ∧ Ks n x ≤ Λ) ∧
              Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (Ks n - K) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                Filter.atTop (nhds 0)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.bounded_curvature_adjoint_inequality_of_minimum
  {C : ℝ} {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z)
  (hmin :
    ∀ (K' r' p' z' : ℝ → ℝ),
      IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K' r' p' z' → weakMeridionalAction K r p z ≤ weakMeridionalAction K' r' p' z')
  (J : ℝ → ℝ) (hJ : IsBoundedAreaCurvature C J) :
  0 ≤ ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, weakCurvatureAdjoint K r p z (weakMeridionalAction K r p z) s * (J s - K s)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_recoverable_area_minimizer_with_bounded_curvature_adjoint (C : ℝ)
  (hC : 1 ≤ C) :
  ∃ K r p z,
    IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z ∧
      ContinuousOn r (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
        ContinuousOn p (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
          (∃ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, r x ≠ 0) ∧
            weakMeridionalAction K r p z = smoothAreaInfimum C ∧
              (∀ (K' r' p' z' : ℝ → ℝ),
                  IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K' r' p' z' →
                    weakMeridionalAction K r p z ≤ weakMeridionalAction K' r' p' z') ∧
                (∀ (s : ℝ → ℝ),
                    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s →
                      weakMeridionalPairing K r p z s (deriv s) (deriv (deriv s)) =
                        smoothAreaInfimum C * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, warp K x * s x * r x) ∧
                  MeasureTheory.Integrable (weakCurvatureAdjoint K r p z (smoothAreaInfimum C))
                      (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
                    ∀ (J : ℝ → ℝ),
                      IsBoundedAreaCurvature C J →
                        0 ≤ ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, weakCurvatureAdjoint K r p z (smoothAreaInfimum C) s * (J s - K s)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.box_variational_inequality_iff.{u_1} {α : Type u_1} [MeasurableSpace α]
  {μ : MeasureTheory.Measure α} {W K : α → ℝ} {lo hi : ℝ} (hW : MeasureTheory.Integrable W μ)
  (hK : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable K μ) (hKbox : ∀ᵐ (x : α) ∂μ, lo ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi) :
  (∀ (J : α → ℝ),
      MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable J μ →
        (∀ᵐ (x : α) ∂μ, lo ≤ J x ∧ J x ≤ hi) → 0 ≤ ∫ (x : α), W x * (J x - K x) ∂μ) ↔
    ∀ᵐ (x : α) ∂μ, (lo < K x → W x ≤ 0) ∧ (K x < hi → 0 ≤ W x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.exists_box_moment_multipliers (W K : ℝ → ℝ) (lo hi c : ℝ)
  (hW : MeasureTheory.Integrable W (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hK : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable K (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hKbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), lo ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi)
  (hK0 : ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, K x = c) (hK1 : ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, x * K x = c / 2) (hloc : lo < c)
  (hchi : c < hi)
  (hmin :
    ∀ (J : ℝ → ℝ),
      MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable J (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) →
        (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), lo ≤ J x ∧ J x ≤ hi) →
          ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, J x = c →
            ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, x * J x = c / 2 → 0 ≤ ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, W x * (J x - K x)) :
  ∃ a b,
    ∀ (J : ℝ → ℝ),
      MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable J (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) →
        (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), lo ≤ J x ∧ J x ≤ hi) →
          0 ≤ ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, (W x - a - b * x) * (J x - K x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsBoundedAreaCurvature.exists_strict_box {C : ℝ} {K : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : IsBoundedAreaCurvature C K) (hC : 1 < C) :
  ∃ κ Λ,
    0 < κ ∧ κ < 2 ∧ 2 < Λ ∧ (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), κ ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ Λ) ∧ Λ / κ ≤ C
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsBoundedAreaCurvature.ae_eq_round_of_cap_one {K : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : IsBoundedAreaCurvature 1 K) : K =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)] fun x ↦ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.exists_curvature_moment_multipliers {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 < C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z)
  (hmin :
    ∀ (K' r' p' z' : ℝ → ℝ),
      IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K' r' p' z' → weakMeridionalAction K r p z ≤ weakMeridionalAction K' r' p' z') :
  ∃ κ Λ a b,
    0 < κ ∧
      κ < 2 ∧
        2 < Λ ∧
          (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), κ ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ Λ) ∧
            Λ / κ ≤ C ∧
              ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1),
                (κ < K x → weakCurvatureAdjoint K r p z (weakMeridionalAction K r p z) x - a - b * x ≤ 0) ∧
                  (K x < Λ → 0 ≤ weakCurvatureAdjoint K r p z (weakMeridionalAction K r p z) x - a - b * x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_recoverable_area_minimizer_with_moment_multipliers (C : ℝ)
  (hC : 1 ≤ C) :
  ∃ K r p z κ Λ a b,
    IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z ∧
      ContinuousOn r (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
        ContinuousOn p (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
          (∃ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, r x ≠ 0) ∧
            weakMeridionalAction K r p z = smoothAreaInfimum C ∧
              (∀ (K' r' p' z' : ℝ → ℝ),
                  IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K' r' p' z' →
                    weakMeridionalAction K r p z ≤ weakMeridionalAction K' r' p' z') ∧
                (∀ (s : ℝ → ℝ),
                    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s →
                      weakMeridionalPairing K r p z s (deriv s) (deriv (deriv s)) =
                        smoothAreaInfimum C * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, warp K x * s x * r x) ∧
                  MeasureTheory.Integrable (weakCurvatureAdjoint K r p z (smoothAreaInfimum C))
                      (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
                    0 < κ ∧
                      (1 < C → κ < 2 ∧ 2 < Λ) ∧
                        (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), κ ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ Λ) ∧
                          Λ / κ ≤ C ∧
                            (C = 1 → K =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)] fun x ↦ 2) ∧
                              ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1),
                                (κ < K x → weakCurvatureAdjoint K r p z (smoothAreaInfimum C) x - a - b * x ≤ 0) ∧
                                  (K x < Λ → 0 ≤ weakCurvatureAdjoint K r p z (smoothAreaInfimum C) x - a - b * x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.second_order_weak_euler_flux (a b : ℝ) (hab : a ≤ b) (U V H : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hU : MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn U (Set.Ioo a b) MeasureTheory.volume)
  (hV : MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn V (Set.Ioo a b) MeasureTheory.volume)
  (hH : MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn H (Set.Ioo a b) MeasureTheory.volume)
  (hEuler :
    ∀ (φ : ℝ → ℝ), ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) φ → ∫ (x : ℝ) in a..b, U x * deriv (deriv φ) x + V x * deriv φ x + H x * φ x = 0) :
  have P := fun x ↦ -∫ (t : ℝ) in a..x, H t;
  have A := fun x ↦ ∫ (t : ℝ) in a..x, V t + P t;
  U =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo a b)] A ∧
    AbsolutelyContinuousOnInterval P a b ∧
      AbsolutelyContinuousOnInterval A a b ∧
        P a = 0 ∧
          P b = 0 ∧
            A a = 0 ∧
              A b = 0 ∧
                (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo a b), HasDerivAt P (-H x) x) ∧
                  ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo a b), HasDerivAt A (V x + P x) x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakProbeSecondCoefficient (K r p z : ℝ → ℝ) (x : ℝ) : ℝ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakProbeFirstCoefficient (K r p z : ℝ → ℝ) (x : ℝ) : ℝ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakProbeZerothCoefficient (K r p z : ℝ → ℝ) (lam x : ℝ) : ℝ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakMeridionalPairingDensity_eq_probe_coefficients (K r p z s t u : ℝ → ℝ)
  (lam x : ℝ) :
  weakMeridionalPairingDensity K r p z s t u x - lam * warp K x * r x * s x =
    weakProbeSecondCoefficient K r p z x * u x + weakProbeFirstCoefficient K r p z x * t x +
      weakProbeZerothCoefficient K r p z lam x * s x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.weak_probe_euler_coefficients_integrable {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) (lam : ℝ) :
  MeasureTheory.Integrable (weakProbeSecondCoefficient K r p z) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
    MeasureTheory.Integrable (weakProbeFirstCoefficient K r p z) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
      MeasureTheory.Integrable (weakProbeZerothCoefficient K r p z lam) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakProbeEulerFlux (K r p z : ℝ → ℝ) (lam x : ℝ) : ℝ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakProbeEulerMomentum (K r p z : ℝ → ℝ) (lam x : ℝ) : ℝ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.probe_euler_flux_of_minimum {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z)
  (hmin :
    ∀ (K' r' p' z' : ℝ → ℝ),
      IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K' r' p' z' → weakMeridionalAction K r p z ≤ weakMeridionalAction K' r' p' z') :
  have lam := weakMeridionalAction K r p z;
  weakProbeSecondCoefficient K r p z =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)]
      weakProbeEulerMomentum K r p z lam ∧
    AbsolutelyContinuousOnInterval (weakProbeEulerFlux K r p z lam) 0 1 ∧
      AbsolutelyContinuousOnInterval (weakProbeEulerMomentum K r p z lam) 0 1 ∧
        weakProbeEulerFlux K r p z lam 0 = 0 ∧
          weakProbeEulerFlux K r p z lam 1 = 0 ∧
            weakProbeEulerMomentum K r p z lam 0 = 0 ∧
              weakProbeEulerMomentum K r p z lam 1 = 0 ∧
                (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1),
                    HasDerivAt (weakProbeEulerFlux K r p z lam) (-weakProbeZerothCoefficient K r p z lam x) x) ∧
                  (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1),
                      HasDerivAt (weakProbeEulerMomentum K r p z lam)
                        (weakProbeFirstCoefficient K r p z x + weakProbeEulerFlux K r p z lam x) x) ∧
                    ContinuousOn (fun x ↦ weakProbeEulerMomentum K r p z lam x / warp K x ^ 2) (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
                      weakMeridionalOperator K r p z =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)] fun x ↦
                        weakProbeEulerMomentum K r p z lam x / warp K x ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_recoverable_area_minimizer_with_natural_flux (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) :
  ∃ K r p z κ Λ a b,
    IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z ∧
      ContinuousOn r (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
        ContinuousOn p (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
          (∃ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, r x ≠ 0) ∧
            weakMeridionalAction K r p z = smoothAreaInfimum C ∧
              (∀ (K' r' p' z' : ℝ → ℝ),
                  IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K' r' p' z' →
                    weakMeridionalAction K r p z ≤ weakMeridionalAction K' r' p' z') ∧
                (∀ (s : ℝ → ℝ),
                    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s →
                      weakMeridionalPairing K r p z s (deriv s) (deriv (deriv s)) =
                        smoothAreaInfimum C * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, warp K x * s x * r x) ∧
                  MeasureTheory.Integrable (weakCurvatureAdjoint K r p z (smoothAreaInfimum C))
                      (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
                    0 < κ ∧
                      (1 < C → κ < 2 ∧ 2 < Λ) ∧
                        (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), κ ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ Λ) ∧
                          Λ / κ ≤ C ∧
                            (C = 1 → K =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)] fun x ↦ 2) ∧
                              (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1),
                                  (κ < K x → weakCurvatureAdjoint K r p z (smoothAreaInfimum C) x - a - b * x ≤ 0) ∧
                                    (K x < Λ → 0 ≤ weakCurvatureAdjoint K r p z (smoothAreaInfimum C) x - a - b * x)) ∧
                                weakProbeSecondCoefficient K r p z =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)]
                                    weakProbeEulerMomentum K r p z (smoothAreaInfimum C) ∧
                                  AbsolutelyContinuousOnInterval (weakProbeEulerFlux K r p z (smoothAreaInfimum C)) 0
                                      1 ∧
                                    AbsolutelyContinuousOnInterval
                                        (weakProbeEulerMomentum K r p z (smoothAreaInfimum C)) 0 1 ∧
                                      weakProbeEulerFlux K r p z (smoothAreaInfimum C) 0 = 0 ∧
                                        weakProbeEulerFlux K r p z (smoothAreaInfimum C) 1 = 0 ∧
                                          weakProbeEulerMomentum K r p z (smoothAreaInfimum C) 0 = 0 ∧
                                            weakProbeEulerMomentum K r p z (smoothAreaInfimum C) 1 = 0 ∧
                                              (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1),
                                                  HasDerivAt (weakProbeEulerFlux K r p z (smoothAreaInfimum C))
                                                    (-weakProbeZerothCoefficient K r p z (smoothAreaInfimum C) x) x) ∧
                                                (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1),
                                                    HasDerivAt (weakProbeEulerMomentum K r p z (smoothAreaInfimum C))
                                                      (weakProbeFirstCoefficient K r p z x +
                                                        weakProbeEulerFlux K r p z (smoothAreaInfimum C) x)
                                                      x) ∧
                                                  ContinuousOn
                                                      (fun x ↦
                                                        weakProbeEulerMomentum K r p z (smoothAreaInfimum C) x /
                                                          warp K x ^ 2)
                                                      (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
                                                    weakMeridionalOperator K r p
                                                        z =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)]
                                                      fun x ↦
                                                      weakProbeEulerMomentum K r p z (smoothAreaInfimum C) x /
                                                        warp K x ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives.mul_contDiff {r p z : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r p z) (u : ℝ → ℝ) (hu : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) u) :
  HasParabolicWeakDerivatives (fun x ↦ r x * u x) (fun x ↦ p x * u x + r x * deriv u x) fun x ↦
    z x * u x + 2 * p x * deriv u x + r x * deriv (deriv u) x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.parabolicGraph_tendsto_mul_contDiff {rn : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ} {r p z : ℝ → ℝ}
  (hrn : ∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ 2 (rn n)) (hgraph : HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r p z)
  (hR :
    Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (rn n - r) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
      Filter.atTop (nhds 0))
  (hP :
    Filter.Tendsto
      (fun n ↦
        MeasureTheory.eLpNorm ((fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (rn n) x) - fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * p x) 2
          (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
      Filter.atTop (nhds 0))
  (hZ :
    Filter.Tendsto
      (fun n ↦
        MeasureTheory.eLpNorm
          ((fun x ↦ x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (deriv (rn n)) x) - fun x ↦ x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * z x)
          2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
      Filter.atTop (nhds 0))
  (u : ℝ → ℝ) (hu : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) u) :
  Filter.Tendsto
      (fun n ↦
        MeasureTheory.eLpNorm ((fun x ↦ rn n x * u x) - fun x ↦ r x * u x) 2
          (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
      Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
    Filter.Tendsto
        (fun n ↦
          MeasureTheory.eLpNorm
            ((fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (fun y ↦ rn n y * u y) x) - fun x ↦
              √(x * (1 - x)) * (p x * u x + r x * deriv u x))
            2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
        Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
      Filter.Tendsto
        (fun n ↦
          MeasureTheory.eLpNorm
            ((fun x ↦ x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (deriv fun y ↦ rn n y * u y) x) - fun x ↦
              x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * (z x * u x + 2 * p x * deriv u x + r x * deriv (deriv u) x))
            2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
        Filter.atTop (nhds 0)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.normalized_probe_mul {C : ℝ} {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ}
  (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) (s : ℝ → ℝ) (hs : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s)
  (hm : 0 < probeMass K fun x ↦ r x * s x) :
  IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K (normalizedProbe K fun x ↦ r x * s x)
    (fun x ↦ 1 / √(probeMass K fun y ↦ r y * s y) * (p x * s x + r x * deriv s x)) fun x ↦
    1 / √(probeMass K fun y ↦ r y * s y) * (z x * s x + 2 * p x * deriv s x + r x * deriv (deriv s) x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.weak_probe_mul_equation_of_minimum {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z)
  (hmin :
    ∀ (K' r' p' z' : ℝ → ℝ),
      IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K' r' p' z' → weakMeridionalAction K r p z ≤ weakMeridionalAction K' r' p' z')
  (u : ℝ → ℝ) (hu : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) u) :
  (weakMeridionalPairing K r p z (fun x ↦ r x * u x) (fun x ↦ p x * u x + r x * deriv u x) fun x ↦
      z x * u x + 2 * p x * deriv u x + r x * deriv (deriv u) x) =
    weakMeridionalAction K r p z * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, warp K x * (r x * u x) * r x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.weakMeridionalPicone_density_identity (K r p z u v w : ℝ → ℝ) (x : ℝ) :
  meridionalActionDensity (warp K x) (warpSlope K x) (K x) (r x * u x) (p x * u x + r x * v x)
        (z x * u x + 2 * p x * v x + r x * w x) -
      weakMeridionalPairingDensity K r p z (fun y ↦ r y * u y ^ 2) (fun y ↦ p y * u y ^ 2 + 2 * r y * u y * v y)
        (fun y ↦ z y * u y ^ 2 + 4 * p y * u y * v y + 2 * r y * (v y ^ 2 + u y * w y)) x =
    warp K x * (warp K x * r x * w x + 2 * (warp K x * p x + warpSlope K x * r x) * v x) ^ 2 -
      2 * warp K x ^ 2 * r x * weakMeridionalOperator K r p z x * v x ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction_mul_sub_pairing_eq_picone (K r u : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hK : Continuous K) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) (hu : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) u) :
  (meridionalAction K fun x ↦ r x * u x) -
      weakMeridionalPairing K r (deriv r) (deriv (deriv r)) (fun x ↦ r x * u x ^ 2) (deriv fun x ↦ r x * u x ^ 2)
        (deriv (deriv fun x ↦ r x * u x ^ 2)) =
    ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1,
      warp K x *
          (warp K x * r x * deriv (deriv u) x + 2 * (warp K x * deriv r x + warpSlope K x * r x) * deriv u x) ^ 2 -
        2 * warp K x ^ 2 * r x * meridionalOperator K r x * deriv u x ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction_ge_mass_of_ground_state (C lam : ℝ) (K r : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hK : IsSmoothAreaCurvature C K) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) (hrpos : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 < r x)
  (heigen : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, meridionalJacobiOperator K r x = lam * r x)
  (hoperator : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, meridionalOperator K r x ≤ 0) (s : ℝ → ℝ) :
  ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s → lam * probeMass K s ≤ meridionalAction K s
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.picone_identity_of_minimum {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z)
  (hmin :
    ∀ (K' r' p' z' : ℝ → ℝ),
      IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K' r' p' z' → weakMeridionalAction K r p z ≤ weakMeridionalAction K' r' p' z')
  (u : ℝ → ℝ) (hu : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) u) :
  have R := fun x ↦
    warp K x * (warp K x * r x * deriv (deriv u) x + 2 * (warp K x * p x + warpSlope K x * r x) * deriv u x) ^ 2 -
      2 * warp K x ^ 2 * r x * weakMeridionalOperator K r p z x * deriv u x ^ 2;
  IntervalIntegrable R MeasureTheory.volume 0 1 ∧
    ((weakMeridionalAction K (fun x ↦ r x * u x) (fun x ↦ p x * u x + r x * deriv u x) fun x ↦
          z x * u x + 2 * p x * deriv u x + r x * deriv (deriv u) x) -
        weakMeridionalAction K r p z * probeMass K fun x ↦ r x * u x) =
      ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, R x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasWeakDerivOn.exists_integral_representative_Ioo {a b c : ℝ} {r p : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : HasWeakDerivOn (Set.Ioo a b) r p) (hc : c ∈ Set.Ioo a b) :
  ∃ d, r =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo a b)] fun x ↦ d + ∫ (t : ℝ) in c..x, p t
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasWeakDerivOn.exists_continuous_representative_Ioo {a b c : ℝ} {r p : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : HasWeakDerivOn (Set.Ioo a b) r p) (hc : c ∈ Set.Ioo a b) :

    ContinuousOn R (Set.Ioo a b) ∧
      r =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo a b)] R ∧ HasWeakDerivOn (Set.Ioo a b) R p

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives.contDiffOn_one_of_continuousOn {r p z : ℝ → ℝ}
  (h : HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r p z) (hr : ContinuousOn r (Set.Ioo 0 1)) : ContDiffOn ℝ 1 r (Set.Ioo 0 1)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.not_exists_parabolicWeakDerivatives_abs_sub (c : ℝ) (hc : c ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1) :
  ¬∃ p z, HasParabolicWeakDerivatives (fun x ↦ |x - c|) p z
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.primitive_weak_derivatives {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasWeakDerivOn (Set.Ioo 0 1) (warp K) (warpSlope K) ∧
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasWeakDerivOn (Set.Ioo 0 1) (warpSlope K) fun x ↦ -2 * K x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.primitive_local_regularity {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) :
  (∀ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, HasDerivAt (warp K) (warpSlope K x) x) ∧
    (∀ a ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, ∀ b ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, AbsolutelyContinuousOnInterval (warpSlope K) a b) ∧
      ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), HasDerivAt (warpSlope K) (-2 * K x) x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.probe_first_order_system_of_minimum {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) (hr : ContinuousOn r (Set.Ioo 0 1))
  (hp : ContinuousOn p (Set.Ioo 0 1))
  (hmin :
    ∀ (K' r' p' z' : ℝ → ℝ),
      IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K' r' p' z' → weakMeridionalAction K r p z ≤ weakMeridionalAction K' r' p' z') :
  have lam := weakMeridionalAction K r p z;
  have P := weakProbeEulerFlux K r p z lam;
  have q := fun x ↦ weakProbeEulerMomentum K r p z lam x / warp K x ^ 2;
  (∀ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, HasDerivAt r (p x) x) ∧
    ContDiffOn ℝ 1 r (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
      ContinuousOn q (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
        weakMeridionalOperator K r p z =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)] q ∧
          (∀ a ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1,
              ∀ b ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, AbsolutelyContinuousOnInterval p a b ∧ AbsolutelyContinuousOnInterval q a b) ∧
            ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1),
              HasDerivAt p ((q x - 2 * warpSlope K x * p x + K x * r x) / warp K x) x ∧
                HasDerivAt q ((P x - K x * warpSlope K x * warp K x * r x / 2) / warp K x ^ 2) x ∧
                  HasDerivAt P
                    (warp K x * K x * q x + K x * warpSlope K x * warp K x * p x / 2 -
                        (K x ^ 2 * warp K x - K x * warpSlope K x ^ 2 / 2) * r x +
                      lam * warp K x * r x)
                    x

  (hpos : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 < K x) (hr : ContDiff ℝ 2 r) (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2)

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_zero_action_recoverable_area_minimizer :
  ∃ K r p z,
    IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalThreshold K r p z ∧
      ContinuousOn r (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
        ContinuousOn p (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
          (∃ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, r x ≠ 0) ∧
            probeMass K r = 1 ∧
              weakMeridionalAction K r p z = 0 ∧
                (∀ (K' r' p' z' : ℝ → ℝ),
                    IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalThreshold K' r' p' z' →
                      weakMeridionalAction K r p z ≤ weakMeridionalAction K' r' p' z') ∧
                  (∀ (s : ℝ → ℝ), ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s → weakMeridionalPairing K r p z s (deriv s) (deriv (deriv s)) = 0) ∧
                    ∃ Ks rs,
                      (∀ (n : ℕ),
                          IsSmoothAreaCompetitor RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalThreshold (Ks n) (rs n)) ∧
                        Filter.Tendsto
                            (fun n ↦ MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (Ks n - K) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                            Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
                          Filter.Tendsto
                              (fun n ↦ MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (rs n - r) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                              Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
                            Filter.Tendsto
                                (fun n ↦
                                  MeasureTheory.eLpNorm
                                    ((fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (rs n) x) - fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * p x) 2
                                    (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                                Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
                              Filter.Tendsto
                                  (fun n ↦
                                    MeasureTheory.eLpNorm
                                      ((fun x ↦ x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (deriv (rs n)) x) - fun x ↦
                                        x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * z x)
                                      2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                                  Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
                                Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ meridionalAction (Ks n) (rs n)) Filter.atTop (nhds 0)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.smoothAreaInfimum_one : smoothAreaInfimum 1 = 4
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.one_lt_rotationalThreshold : 1 < rotationalThreshold
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_smooth_area_competitor_action_neg_of_rotationalThreshold_lt {C : ℝ}
  (hC : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalThreshold < C) :
  ∃ K r, IsSmoothAreaCompetitor C K r ∧ meridionalAction K r < 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.exists_smooth_geometric_negative_witness_of_rotationalThreshold_lt {D : ℝ}
  (hD : rotationalThreshold < D) :
  ∃ C κ Λ K r X P R,
    ∃ (hR : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) R),
      rotationalThreshold < C ∧
        C < D ∧
          AreaProfile.IsSmoothAreaCompetitor C K r ∧
            0 < κ ∧
              (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, κ ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ Λ) ∧
                Λ / κ ≤ C ∧
                  Λ / κ < D ∧
                    ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2 ∧
                      ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1 ∧
                        AreaProfile.probeMass K r = 1 ∧
                          AreaProfile.meridionalAction K r < 0 ∧
                            ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) X ∧
                              AreaProfile.heightCoordinate K 0 = -1 ∧
                                AreaProfile.heightCoordinate K 1 = 1 ∧
                                  X (-1) = 0 ∧
                                    X 1 = 1 ∧
                                      Set.MapsTo (AreaProfile.heightCoordinate K) (Set.Icc 0 1) (Set.Icc (-1) 1) ∧
                                        Set.MapsTo X (Set.Icc (-1) 1) (Set.Icc 0 1) ∧
                                          (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1,
                                              X (AreaProfile.heightCoordinate K x) = x ∧
                                                P.a (AreaProfile.heightCoordinate K x) = 1 / K x ∧
                                                  R (AreaProfile.heightCoordinate K x) = r x ∧
                                                    RotationalProfile.warp P.a (AreaProfile.heightCoordinate K x) =
                                                      AreaProfile.warp K x) ∧
                                            (∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1,
                                                AreaProfile.heightCoordinate K (X v) = v ∧ deriv X v * K (X v) = 1) ∧
                                              R = r ∘ X ∧
                                                (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1,
                                                    deriv P.a (AreaProfile.heightCoordinate K x) =
                                                        -deriv K x / K x ^ 3 ∧
                                                      deriv R (AreaProfile.heightCoordinate K x) = deriv r x / K x ∧
                                                        deriv (deriv R) (AreaProfile.heightCoordinate K x) =
                                                          deriv (deriv r) x / K x ^ 2 -
                                                            deriv r x * deriv K x / K x ^ 3) ∧
                                                  (∀ (p : ↑RotationalSphere),
                                                      DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt P.metric
                                                            p /
                                                          2 =
                                                        K (X (sphereHeight p))) ∧
                                                    oneFormDissipation P.metric (P.meridionalOneForm R hR) =
                                                        2 * Real.pi * AreaProfile.meridionalAction K r ∧
                                                      IsRepresentedRotationalMetric P.metric ∧
                                                        (∀ (p : ↑RotationalSphere),
                                                            κ ≤
                                                                DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt
                                                                    P.metric p /
                                                                  2 ∧
                                                              DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt
                                                                    P.metric p /
                                                                  2 ≤
                                                                Λ) ∧
                                                          P.meridionalOneForm R hR ≠ 0 ∧
                                                            oneFormDissipation P.metric (P.meridionalOneForm R hR) < 0

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.probeMass_gradient_le_probeOperatorEnergy (K r : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hr : ContDiff ℝ 2 r) (lo : ℝ) (hlo : 0 ≤ lo) (hlower : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, lo ≤ K x)
  (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2) (hone : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1) :
  lo ^ 2 * probeMass K r + 2 * lo * probeGradientEnergy K r ≤ probeOperatorEnergy K r
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_uniform_meridional_inverse_graph_bound (lo hi : ℝ) (hlo : 0 < lo) :
  ∃ M > 0,
    ∀ (K r : ℝ → ℝ),
      Continuous K →
        ContDiff ℝ 2 r →
          (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, lo ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi) →
            ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2 →
              ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1 →
                ((∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, r x ^ 2) + ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * (1 - x) * deriv r x ^ 2) +
                    ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, (x * (1 - x)) ^ 3 * deriv (deriv r) x ^ 2 ≤
                  M * probeOperatorEnergy K r
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasWeakDerivOn.absolutelyContinuous_mul_of_parabolic_bounds {r p f b : ℝ → ℝ} {B : ℝ}
  (hr : HasWeakDerivOn (Set.Ioo 0 1) r p) (hf : HasWeakDerivOn (Set.Ioo 0 1) f b) (hrc : ContinuousOn r (Set.Ioo 0 1))
  (hfc : ContinuousOn f (Set.Ioo 0 1)) (hr2 : MeasureTheory.MemLp r 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hp2 : MeasureTheory.MemLp (fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * p x) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hfb : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, |f x| ≤ B * (x * (1 - x)))
  (hbb : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), |b x| ≤ B) :
  AbsolutelyContinuousOnInterval (fun x ↦ f x * r x) 0 1 ∧
    f 0 * r 0 = 0 ∧
      f 1 * r 1 = 0 ∧
        MeasureTheory.MemLp (fun x ↦ f x * r x) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
          MeasureTheory.MemLp (fun x ↦ b x * r x + f x * p x) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
            ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1),
              HasDerivAt (fun y ↦ f y * r y) (b x * r x + f x * p x) x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.transformed_probe_absolutelyContinuous {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) (hr : ContinuousOn r (Set.Ioo 0 1)) :
  AbsolutelyContinuousOnInterval (fun x ↦ warp K x * r x) 0 1 ∧
    warp K 0 * r 0 = 0 ∧
      warp K 1 * r 1 = 0 ∧
        MeasureTheory.MemLp (fun x ↦ warp K x * r x) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
          MeasureTheory.MemLp (fun x ↦ warpSlope K x * r x + warp K x * p x) 2
              (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
            ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1),
              HasDerivAt (fun y ↦ warp K y * r y) (warpSlope K x * r x + warp K x * p x) x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.transformed_probe_equation {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) (hr : ContinuousOn r (Set.Ioo 0 1))
  (hp : ContinuousOn p (Set.Ioo 0 1)) :
  (∀ a ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1,
      ∀ b ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, AbsolutelyContinuousOnInterval (fun x ↦ warpSlope K x * r x + warp K x * p x) a b) ∧
    ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1),
      HasDerivAt (fun y ↦ warpSlope K y * r y + warp K y * p y)
        (weakMeridionalOperator K r p z x - K x / warp K x * (warp K x * r x)) x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.parabolic_weight_mul_sq_le_energy (u : ℝ → ℝ) (hu : ContDiff ℝ 1 u) (x : ℝ)
  (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  x * (1 - x) * u x ^ 2 ≤ 4 * ((∫ (y : ℝ) in 0..1, u y ^ 2) + ∫ (y : ℝ) in 0..1, y * (1 - y) * deriv u y ^ 2)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalOperator_sub_curvature_energy_le (K L u : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hK : Continuous K) (hL : Continuous L) (hu : ContDiff ℝ 2 u)
  (hzero : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, L x)
  (hone : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * L x) :
  ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * (1 - x) * (meridionalOperator K u x - meridionalOperator L u x) ^ 2 ≤
    60 *
        (((∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, u x ^ 2) + ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * (1 - x) * deriv u x ^ 2) +
          ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, (x * (1 - x)) ^ 3 * deriv (deriv u) x ^ 2) *
      ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, (K x - L x) ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_uniform_meridional_inverse_stability (lo hi : ℝ) (hlo : 0 < lo) :
  ∃ M > 0,
    ∀ (K L u v g h : ℝ → ℝ),
      Continuous K →
        Continuous L →
          ContDiff ℝ 2 u →
            ContDiff ℝ 2 v →
              (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, lo ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi) →
                ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2 →
                  ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1 →
                    ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, L x = 2 →
                      ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * L x = 1 →
                        (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, -meridionalOperator K u x = g x) →
                          (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, -meridionalOperator L v x = h x) →
                            ((∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, (u x - v x) ^ 2) +
                                  ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * (1 - x) * deriv (fun y ↦ u y - v y) x ^ 2) +
                                ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, (x * (1 - x)) ^ 3 * deriv (deriv fun y ↦ u y - v y) x ^ 2 ≤
                              M *
                                ((2 * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * (1 - x) * (g x - h x) ^ 2) +
                                  120 *
                                      (((∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, v x ^ 2) + ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * (1 - x) * deriv v x ^ 2) +
                                        ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, (x * (1 - x)) ^ 3 * deriv (deriv v) x ^ 2) *
                                    ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, (K x - L x) ^ 2)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_smooth_meridional_inverse (K g : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) K)
  (hg : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) g) (hpos : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 < K x) (hzero : ∫ (t : ℝ) in 0..1, K t = 2)
  (hone : ∫ (t : ℝ) in 0..1, t * K t = 1) :
  ∃ u,
    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) u ∧
      (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, -meridionalOperator K u x = g x) ∧
        ((∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 ≤ g x) → ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 ≤ u x) ∧
          ∀ (v : ℝ → ℝ),
            ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) v → (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, -meridionalOperator K v x = g x) → Set.EqOn v u (Set.Icc 0 1)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.exists_parabolic_graph_limit_of_cauchy (r p z : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ)
  (h : ∀ (n : ℕ), HasParabolicWeakDerivatives (r n) (p n) (z n))
  (hc :
    CauchySeq fun n ↦
      (MeasureTheory.MemLp.toLp (r n) ⋯, MeasureTheory.MemLp.toLp (fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * p n x) ⋯,
        MeasureTheory.MemLp.toLp (fun x ↦ x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * z n x) ⋯)) :
  ∃ r0 p0 z0,
    HasParabolicWeakDerivatives r0 p0 z0 ∧
      ContinuousOn r0 (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
        ContinuousOn p0 (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
          Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (r n - r0) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
              Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
            Filter.Tendsto
                (fun n ↦
                  MeasureTheory.eLpNorm ((fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * p n x) - fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * p0 x) 2
                    (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
              Filter.Tendsto
                (fun n ↦
                  MeasureTheory.eLpNorm
                    ((fun x ↦ x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * z n x) - fun x ↦ x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * z0 x) 2
                    (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                Filter.atTop (nhds 0)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.exists_smooth_nonnegative_source_split (q : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ) (q0 a : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hq : ∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (q n)) (ha : Continuous a) (hapos : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 ≤ a x)
  (hq0 : MeasureTheory.MemLp (fun x ↦ a x * q0 x) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hstrong :
    Filter.Tendsto
      (fun n ↦ MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (fun x ↦ a x * q n x - a x * q0 x) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
      Filter.atTop (nhds 0)) :
  ∃ gp gm,
    (∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (gp n) ∧ ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (gm n)) ∧
      (∀ (n : ℕ) (x : ℝ), 0 ≤ gp n x ∧ 0 ≤ gm n x) ∧
        (∀ (n : ℕ) (x : ℝ), gp n x - gm n x = q n x) ∧
          (∀ (n : ℕ),
              MeasureTheory.MemLp (fun x ↦ a x * gp n x) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
                MeasureTheory.MemLp (fun x ↦ a x * gm n x) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1))) ∧
            MeasureTheory.MemLp (fun x ↦ a x * max (q0 x) 0) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
              MeasureTheory.MemLp (fun x ↦ a x * max (-q0 x) 0) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
                Filter.Tendsto
                    (fun n ↦
                      MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (fun x ↦ a x * gp n x - a x * max (q0 x) 0) 2
                        (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                    Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
                  Filter.Tendsto
                    (fun n ↦
                      MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (fun x ↦ a x * gm n x - a x * max (-q0 x) 0) 2
                        (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                    Filter.atTop (nhds 0)

  (hW :

      (fun n ↦
        MeasureTheory.eLpNorm

      Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
    Filter.Tendsto
        (fun n ↦
          MeasureTheory.eLpNorm
            ((fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (fun y ↦ rs n y + ss n y) x) - fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * (p x + v x)) 2
            (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
        Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
      Filter.Tendsto
        (fun n ↦

            ((fun x ↦ x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (deriv fun y ↦ rs n y + ss n y) x) - fun x ↦
              x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * (z x + w x))
            2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
        Filter.atTop (nhds 0)

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_nonnegative_meridional_inverse_recovery (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C)
  (K g : ℝ → ℝ) (Ks gs : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ) (hK : IsBoundedAreaCurvature C K) (hKs : ∀ (n : ℕ), IsSmoothAreaCurvature C (Ks n))
  (hgs : ∀ (n : ℕ), ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (gs n)) (hgpos : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 ≤ gs n x)
  (hg : MeasureTheory.MemLp (fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * g x) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))

          (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))

    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives u p z ∧
      ContinuousOn u (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧

          (∀ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, 0 ≤ u x) ∧

                        MeasureTheory.eLpNorm ((fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (us n) x) - fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * p x)
                          2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                      Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧

                      (fun n ↦
                        MeasureTheory.eLpNorm
                          ((fun x ↦ x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (deriv (us n)) x) - fun x ↦

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.exists_nonnegative_operator_split {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) (hr : ContinuousOn r (Set.Ioo 0 1))
  (hp : ContinuousOn p (Set.Ioo 0 1)) :
  ∃ u pu zu v pv zv Ks us vs,
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives u pu zu ∧
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives v pv zv ∧
        ContinuousOn u (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
          ContinuousOn pu (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
            ContinuousOn v (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
              ContinuousOn pv (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
                (∀ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, 0 ≤ u x ∧ 0 ≤ v x) ∧
                  (r = fun x ↦ v x - u x) ∧
                    (p = fun x ↦ pv x - pu x) ∧
                      (z = fun x ↦ zv x - zu x) ∧
                        (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1),
                            -weakMeridionalOperator K u pu zu x = max (weakMeridionalOperator K r p z x) 0) ∧
                          (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1),
                              -weakMeridionalOperator K v pv zv x = max (-weakMeridionalOperator K r p z x) 0) ∧
                            (∀ (n : ℕ), IsSmoothAreaCurvature C (Ks n)) ∧
                              (∀ (n : ℕ),
                                  ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (us n) ∧
                                    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (vs n) ∧
                                      ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1,
                                        0 ≤ us n x ∧
                                          0 ≤ vs n x ∧
                                            meridionalOperator (Ks n) (us n) x ≤ 0 ∧
                                              meridionalOperator (Ks n) (vs n) x ≤ 0) ∧
                                Filter.Tendsto
                                    (fun n ↦
                                      MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (Ks n - K) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                                    Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
                                  (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1),
                                      Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ Ks n x) Filter.atTop (nhds (K x))) ∧
                                    (1 ≤ probeMass K fun x ↦ u x + v x) ∧
                                      ∀ (a b : ℝ),
                                        have R := fun x ↦ a * u x + b * v x;
                                        have P := fun x ↦ a * pu x + b * pv x;
                                        have Z := fun x ↦ a * zu x + b * zv x;
                                        have Rs := fun n x ↦ a * us n x + b * vs n x;
                                        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasParabolicWeakDerivatives R P Z ∧
                                          Filter.Tendsto
                                              (fun n ↦
                                                MeasureTheory.eLpNorm (Rs n - R) 2
                                                  (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                                              Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
                                            Filter.Tendsto
                                                (fun n ↦
                                                  MeasureTheory.eLpNorm
                                                    ((fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (Rs n) x) - fun x ↦
                                                      √(x * (1 - x)) * P x)
                                                    2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                                                Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
                                              Filter.Tendsto
                                                  (fun n ↦
                                                    MeasureTheory.eLpNorm
                                                      ((fun x ↦ x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * deriv (deriv (Rs n)) x) -
                                                        fun x ↦ x * (1 - x) * √(x * (1 - x)) * Z x)
                                                      2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
                                                  Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
                                                MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn
                                                    (fun x ↦
                                                      meridionalActionDensity (warp K x) (warpSlope K x) (K x) (R x)
                                                        (P x) (Z x))
                                                    (Set.Ioo 0 1) MeasureTheory.volume ∧
                                                  MeasureTheory.IntegrableOn (fun x ↦ warp K x * R x ^ 2) (Set.Ioo 0 1)
                                                      MeasureTheory.volume ∧
                                                    Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ meridionalAction (Ks n) (Rs n)) Filter.atTop
                                                        (nhds (weakMeridionalAction K R P Z)) ∧
                                                      Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ probeMass (Ks n) (Rs n)) Filter.atTop
                                                          (nhds (probeMass K R)) ∧
                                                        (0 < probeMass K R →
                                                          IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K (normalizedProbe K R)
                                                            (fun x ↦ 1 / √(probeMass K R) * P x) fun x ↦
                                                            1 / √(probeMass K R) * Z x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.exists_box_centered_moment_multiplier (W K : ℝ → ℝ) (lo hi : ℝ) (hlohi : lo < hi)
  (hW : MeasureTheory.Integrable W (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hK : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable K (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hKbox : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), lo ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ hi)
  (hKm : ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, (x - 1 / 2) * K x = 0)
  (hmin :
    ∀ (J : ℝ → ℝ),
      MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable J (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) →
        (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), lo ≤ J x ∧ J x ≤ hi) →
          ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, (x - 1 / 2) * J x = 0 → 0 ≤ ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, W x * (J x - K x)) :
  ∃ t,
    ∀ (J : ℝ → ℝ),
      MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable J (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) →
        (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), lo ≤ J x ∧ J x ≤ hi) →
          0 ≤ ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, (W x - t * (x - 1 / 2)) * (J x - K x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.curvature_adjoint_scale_identity {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) (lam : ℝ) :
  MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ weakCurvatureAdjoint K r p z lam x * K x)
      (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
    ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, weakCurvatureAdjoint K r p z lam x * K x =
      3 * weakMeridionalAction K r p z - lam * probeMass K r
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.exists_scale_balanced_curvature_multiplier
  {C : ℝ} {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 < C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z)
  (hzero : weakMeridionalAction K r p z = 0)
  (hmin :
    ∀ (K' r' p' z' : ℝ → ℝ),
      IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K' r' p' z' → weakMeridionalAction K r p z ≤ weakMeridionalAction K' r' p' z') :
  ∃ κ t,
    0 < κ ∧
      κ < 2 ∧
        2 < C * κ ∧
          (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), κ ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ C * κ) ∧
            have Z := fun x ↦ weakCurvatureAdjoint K r p z 0 x - t * (x - 1 / 2);
            MeasureTheory.Integrable Z (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
              MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ Z x * K x) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
                (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1),
                    (κ < K x → Z x ≤ 0) ∧ (K x < C * κ → 0 ≤ Z x)) ∧
                  ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, Z x * K x = 0 ∧
                    ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, max (Z x) 0 = C * ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, max (-Z x) 0 ∧
                      ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, weakCurvatureAdjoint K r p z 0 x =
                        (C - 1) * ∫ (x : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, max (-Z x) 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.weighted_dirichlet_adjoint_local_regularity (A : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hAm : MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable A (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hA : MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun s ↦ s * (1 - s) * A s) (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1))) :
  have G := fun x ↦ ∫ (s : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, unitDirichletKernel s x * A s;
  have J := fun x ↦ (∫ (s : ℝ) in x..1, (1 - s) * A s) - ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..x, s * A s;
  (∀ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1,
      IntervalIntegrable (fun s ↦ s * A s) MeasureTheory.volume 0 x ∧
        IntervalIntegrable (fun s ↦ (1 - s) * A s) MeasureTheory.volume x 1 ∧
          MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun s ↦ unitDirichletKernel s x * A s)
              (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
            G x = ((1 - x) * ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..x, s * A s) + x * ∫ (s : ℝ) in x..1, (1 - s) * A s) ∧
    ContDiffOn ℝ 1 G (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
      ContinuousOn J (Set.Ioo 0 1) ∧
        (∀ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, HasDerivAt G (J x) x) ∧
          (∀ a ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1,
              ∀ b ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, AbsolutelyContinuousOnInterval G a b ∧ AbsolutelyContinuousOnInterval J a b) ∧
            ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), HasDerivAt J (-A x) x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.slope_kernel_adjoint_absolutelyContinuous (B : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hB : MeasureTheory.Integrable B (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1))) :
  have H := fun x ↦ ∫ (s : ℝ) in Set.Ioo 0 1, unitDirichletSlopeKernel s x * B s;
  (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, H x = (∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..x, B s) - x * ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, B s) ∧
    AbsolutelyContinuousOnInterval H 0 1 ∧
      H 0 = 0 ∧
        H 1 = 0 ∧ ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), HasDerivAt H (B x - ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, B s) x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.HasWeakDerivOn.absolutelyContinuous_mul_sq_of_parabolic_bounds {r p f b : ℝ → ℝ} {B : ℝ}
  (hr : HasWeakDerivOn (Set.Ioo 0 1) r p) (hf : HasWeakDerivOn (Set.Ioo 0 1) f b) (hrc : ContinuousOn r (Set.Ioo 0 1))
  (hfc : ContinuousOn f (Set.Ioo 0 1)) (hr2 : MeasureTheory.MemLp r 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hp2 : MeasureTheory.MemLp (fun x ↦ √(x * (1 - x)) * p x) 2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)))
  (hfb : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, |f x| ≤ B * (x * (1 - x)))
  (hbb : ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), |b x| ≤ B) :
  AbsolutelyContinuousOnInterval (fun x ↦ f x * r x ^ 2) 0 1 ∧
    f 0 * r 0 ^ 2 = 0 ∧
      f 1 * r 1 ^ 2 = 0 ∧
        MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ b x * r x ^ 2 + 2 * f x * r x * p x)
            (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)) ∧
          ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1),
            HasDerivAt (fun y ↦ f y * r y ^ 2) (b x * r x ^ 2 + 2 * f x * r x * p x) x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.exists_regular_curvature_representative {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 < C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) (hr : ContinuousOn r (Set.Ioo 0 1))
  (hp : ContinuousOn p (Set.Ioo 0 1))
  (hmin :
    ∀ (K' r' p' z' : ℝ → ℝ),
      IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K' r' p' z' → weakMeridionalAction K r p z ≤ weakMeridionalAction K' r' p' z') :
  ∃ κ Λ Kc,
    0 < κ ∧
      κ < 2 ∧
        2 < Λ ∧
          Λ / κ ≤ C ∧
            Kc =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1)] K ∧
              (∀ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, κ ≤ Kc x ∧ Kc x ≤ Λ) ∧
                ContinuousOn Kc {x | x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1 ∧ r x ≠ 0} ∧
                  (∀ (a b : ℝ),
                      Set.uIcc a b ⊆ {x | x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1 ∧ r x ≠ 0} → AbsolutelyContinuousOnInterval Kc a b) ∧
                    ∀ a ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1,
                      ∀ b ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, AbsolutelyContinuousOnInterval (fun x ↦ Kc x * warp K x * r x ^ 2) a b
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor.exists_free_curvature_interval {C : ℝ}
  {K r p z : ℝ → ℝ} (hC : 1 < C) (h : IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K r p z) (hr : ContinuousOn r (Set.Ioo 0 1))
  (hp : ContinuousOn p (Set.Ioo 0 1)) (hzero : weakMeridionalAction K r p z = 0)
  (hmin :
    ∀ (K' r' p' z' : ℝ → ℝ),
      IsRecoverableAreaCompetitor C K' r' p' z' → weakMeridionalAction K r p z ≤ weakMeridionalAction K' r' p' z') :
  ∃ κ Λ a b η,
    0 < κ ∧
      κ < 2 ∧
        2 < Λ ∧
          Λ / κ ≤ C ∧
            (∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioo 0 1), κ ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ Λ) ∧
              0 < a ∧
                a < b ∧
                  b < 1 ∧
                    0 < η ∧
                      (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc a b, η ≤ warp K x ∧ η ≤ |r x|) ∧
                        ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Icc a b), κ + η ≤ K x ∧ K x ≤ Λ - η
